1→<!-- desloppify-begin -->
     2→<!-- desloppify-skill-version: 2 -->
     3→---
     4→name: desloppify
     5→description: >
     6→  Codebase health scanner and technical debt tracker. Use when the user asks
     7→  about code quality, technical debt, dead code, large files, god classes,
     8→  duplicate functions, code smells, naming issues, import cycles, or coupling
     9→  problems. Also use when asked for a health score, what to fix next, or to
    10→  create a cleanup plan. Supports 28 languages.
    11→allowed-tools: Bash(desloppify *)
    12→---
    13→
    14→# Desloppify
    15→
    16→## 1. Your Job
    17→
    18→Maximise the **strict score** honestly. Your main loop: run `desloppify next`, fix what it says, resolve it, run `next` again. Over and over. Follow the scan output's **INSTRUCTIONS FOR AGENTS** — don't substitute your own analysis.
    19→
    20→**Don't be lazy.** Do large refactors and small detailed fixes with equal energy. If it takes touching 20 files, touch 20 files. If it's a one-line change, make it. No task is too big or too small — fix things properly, not minimally.
    21→
    22→## 2. The Workflow
    23→
    24→Two loops. The **outer loop** rescans periodically to measure progress.
    25→The **inner loop** is where you spend most of your time: fixing issues one by one.
    26→
    27→### Outer loop — scan and check
    28→
    29→```bash
    30→desloppify scan --path .       # analyse the codebase
    31→desloppify status              # check scores — are we at target?
    32→```
    33→If not at target, work the inner loop. Rescan periodically — especially after clearing a cluster or batch of related fixes. Issues cascade-resolve and new ones may surface.
    34→
    35→### Inner loop — fix issues
    36→
    37→Repeat until the queue is clear:
    38→
    39→```
    40→1. desloppify next              ← tells you exactly what to fix next
    41→2. Fix the issue in code
    42→3. Resolve it (next shows you the exact command including required attestation)
    43→```
    44→
    45→Score may temporarily drop after fixes — cascade effects are normal, keep going.
    46→If `next` suggests an auto-fixer, run `desloppify fix <fixer> --dry-run` to preview, then apply.
    47→
    48→**To be strategic**, use `plan` to shape what `next` gives you:
    49→```bash
    50→desloppify plan                        # see the full ordered queue
    51→desloppify plan move <pat> top         # reorder — what unblocks the most?
    52→desloppify plan cluster create <name>  # group related issues to batch-fix
    53→desloppify plan focus <cluster>        # scope next to one cluster
    54→desloppify plan skip <pat>             # skip / hide from next
    55→desloppify plan done <pat>             # mark complete
    56→desloppify plan reopen <pat>           # reopen
    57→```
    58→
    59→### Subjective reviews
    60→
    61→The scan will prompt you when a subjective review is needed — just follow its instructions.
    62→If you need to trigger one manually:
    63→```bash
    64→desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import
    65→```
    66→
    67→### Synthesis (after review)
    68→
    69→After a subjective review, `synthesis::pending` appears at the top of the queue.
    70→Complete the 4-stage workflow before moving to fixes:
    71→```bash
    72→desloppify plan synthesize                          # dashboard — see stage progress
    73→desloppify plan synthesize --stage observe --report "themes and root causes..."
    74→desloppify plan synthesize --stage reflect --report "comparison against completed work..."
    75→desloppify plan synthesize --stage organize --report "summary of priorities..."
    76→desloppify plan synthesize --complete --strategy "execution plan..."
    77→```
    78→
    79→### Other useful commands
    80→
    81→```bash
    82→desloppify next --count 5                         # top 5 priorities
    83→desloppify next --cluster <name>                  # drill into a cluster
    84→desloppify show <pattern>                         # filter by file/detector/ID
    85→desloppify show --status open                     # all open findings
    86→desloppify plan skip --permanent "<id>" --note "reason" # accept debt (lowers strict score)
    87→desloppify scan --path . --reset-subjective       # reset subjective baseline to 0
    88→```
    89→
    90→## 3. Reference
    91→
    92→### How scoring works
    93→
    94→Overall score = **40% mechanical** + **60% subjective**.
    95→
    96→- **Mechanical (40%)**: auto-detected issues — duplication, dead code, smells, unused imports, security. Fixed by changing code and rescanning.
    97→- **Subjective (60%)**: design quality review — naming, error handling, abstractions, clarity. Starts at **0%** until reviewed. The scan will prompt you when a review is needed.
    98→- **Strict score** is the north star: wontfix items count as open. The gap between overall and strict is your wontfix debt.
    99→- **Score types**: overall (lenient), strict (wontfix counts), objective (mechanical only), verified (confirmed fixes only).
   100→
   101→### Subjective reviews in detail
   102→
   103→- **Preferred**: `desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` — does everything in one command.
   104→- **Manual path**: `desloppify review --prepare` → review per dimension → `desloppify review --import file.json`.
   105→- Import first, fix after — import creates tracked state entries for correlation.
   106→- Integrity: reviewers score from evidence only. Scores hitting exact targets trigger auto-reset.
   107→- Even moderate scores (60-80) dramatically improve overall health.
   108→- Stale dimensions auto-surface in `next` — just follow the queue.
   109→
   110→### Key concepts
   111→
   112→- **Tiers**: T1 auto-fix → T2 quick manual → T3 judgment call → T4 major refactor.
   113→- **Auto-clusters**: related findings are auto-grouped in `next`. Drill in with `next --cluster <name>`.
   114→- **Zones**: production/script (scored), test/config/generated/vendor (not scored). Fix with `zone set`.
   115→- **Wontfix cost**: widens the lenient↔strict gap. Challenge past decisions when the gap grows.
   116→- Score can temporarily drop after fixes (cascade effects are normal).
   117→
   118→## 4. Escalate Tool Issues Upstream
   119→
   120→When desloppify itself appears wrong or inconsistent:
   121→
   122→1. Capture a minimal repro (`command`, `path`, `expected`, `actual`).
   123→2. Open a GitHub issue in `peteromallet/desloppify`.
   124→3. If you can fix it safely, open a PR linked to that issue.
   125→4. If unsure whether it is tool bug vs user workflow, issue first, PR second.
   126→
   127→## Prerequisite
   128→
   129→`command -v desloppify >/dev/null 2>&1 && echo "desloppify: installed" || echo "NOT INSTALLED — run: pip install --upgrade git+https://github.com/peteromallet/desloppify.git"`
   130→
   131→<!-- desloppify-end -->
   132→

1→"""Skill-document versioning and install metadata helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import get_project_root
     9→
    10→# Bump this integer whenever docs/SKILL.md changes in a way that agents
    11→# should pick up (new commands, changed workflows, removed sections).
    12→SKILL_VERSION = 2
    13→
    14→SKILL_VERSION_RE = re.compile(r"<!--\s*desloppify-skill-version:\s*(\d+)\s*-->")
    15→SKILL_OVERLAY_RE = re.compile(r"<!--\s*desloppify-overlay:\s*(\w+)\s*-->")
    16→
    17→SKILL_BEGIN = "<!-- desloppify-begin -->"
    18→SKILL_END = "<!-- desloppify-end -->"
    19→
    20→# Locations where the skill doc might be installed, relative to project root.
    21→SKILL_SEARCH_PATHS = (
    22→    ".claude/skills/desloppify/SKILL.md",
    23→    ".opencode/skills/desloppify/SKILL.md",
    24→    "AGENTS.md",
    25→    "CLAUDE.md",
    26→    ".cursor/rules/desloppify.md",
    27→    ".github/copilot-instructions.md",
    28→)
    29→
    30→# Interface name → (target file, overlay filename, dedicated).
    31→# Dedicated files are overwritten entirely; shared files get section replacement.
    32→SKILL_TARGETS: dict[str, tuple[str, str, bool]] = {
    33→    "claude": (".claude/skills/desloppify/SKILL.md", "CLAUDE", True),
    34→    # OpenCode support added with thanks to @H3xKatana.
    35→    "opencode": (".opencode/skills/desloppify/SKILL.md", "OPENCODE", True),
    36→    "codex": ("AGENTS.md", "CODEX", False),
    37→    "cursor": (".cursor/rules/desloppify.md", "CURSOR", True),
    38→    "copilot": (".github/copilot-instructions.md", "COPILOT", False),
    39→    "windsurf": ("AGENTS.md", "WINDSURF", False),
    40→    "gemini": ("AGENTS.md", "GEMINI", False),
    41→}
    42→
    43→
    44→@dataclass
    45→class SkillInstall:
    46→    """Detected skill document installation."""
    47→
    48→    rel_path: str
    49→    version: int
    50→    overlay: str | None
    51→    stale: bool
    52→
    53→
    54→def find_installed_skill() -> SkillInstall | None:
    55→    """Find installed skill document metadata, or None."""
    56→    project_root = get_project_root()
    57→    for rel_path in SKILL_SEARCH_PATHS:
    58→        full = project_root / rel_path
    59→        if not full.is_file():
    60→            continue
    61→        try:
    62→            content = full.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    63→        except OSError as exc:
    64→            _ = exc
    65→            continue
    66→        version_match = SKILL_VERSION_RE.search(content)
    67→        if not version_match:
    68→            continue
    69→        installed_version = int(version_match.group(1))
    70→        overlay_match = SKILL_OVERLAY_RE.search(content)
    71→        overlay = overlay_match.group(1) if overlay_match else None
    72→        return SkillInstall(
    73→            rel_path=rel_path,
    74→            version=installed_version,
    75→            overlay=overlay,
    76→            stale=installed_version < SKILL_VERSION,
    77→        )
    78→    return None
    79→
    80→
    81→def check_skill_version() -> str | None:
    82→    """Return a warning if installed skill doc is outdated."""
    83→    install = find_installed_skill()
    84→    if not install or not install.stale:
    85→        return None
    86→    return (
    87→        f"Your desloppify skill document is outdated "
    88→        f"(v{install.version}, current v{SKILL_VERSION}). "
    89→        "Run: desloppify update-skill"
    90→    )
    91→
    92→
    93→__all__ = [
    94→    "SKILL_VERSION",
    95→    "SKILL_VERSION_RE",
    96→    "SKILL_OVERLAY_RE",
    97→    "SKILL_BEGIN",
    98→    "SKILL_END",
    99→    "SKILL_SEARCH_PATHS",
   100→    "SKILL_TARGETS",
   101→    "SkillInstall",
   102→    "find_installed_skill",
   103→    "check_skill_version",
   104→]
   105→

1→"""plan command: generate prioritized markdown plan from state.
     2→
     3→This module preserves the original import path. The full plan command
     4→(with subcommands) lives in ``desloppify.app.commands.plan.cmd``.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import argparse
    10→
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.rendering import print_agent_plan
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    14→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import warn_best_effort
    15→from desloppify.engine import planning as planning_mod
    16→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    17→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    18→from desloppify.core.tooling import check_config_staleness
    19→
    20→
    21→def cmd_plan_output(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    22→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state."""
    23→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    24→    state = runtime.state
    25→
    26→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    27→        return
    28→
    29→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(runtime.config)
    30→    if config_warning:
    31→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    32→
    33→    plan_md = planning_mod.generate_plan_md(state)
    34→    next_command = "desloppify next --count 20"
    35→
    36→    output = getattr(args, "output", None)
    37→    if output:
    38→        try:
    39→            safe_write_text(output, plan_md)
    40→            print(colorize(f"Plan written to {output}", "green"))
    41→            print_agent_plan(
    42→                ["Inspect and execute the generated plan."],
    43→                next_command=next_command,
    44→            )
    45→        except OSError as e:
    46→            warn_best_effort(f"Could not write plan to {output}: {e}")
    47→    else:
    48→        print(plan_md)
    49→        print()
    50→        print_agent_plan(
    51→            ["Start from the top-ranked action in this plan."],
    52→            next_command=next_command,
    53→        )
    54→
    55→
    56→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_output"]
    57→

    19→from desloppify.app.commands.plan_cmd import cmd_plan_output as _plan_output_impl
    20→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    21→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan, reset_plan, save_plan
    22→
    23→
    24→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    25→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
    26→    _plan_output_impl(args)
    27→
    28→
    29→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    30→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    31→    plan = load_plan()
    32→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    33→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    34→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    35→    temp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "temporary")
    36→    perm_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "permanent")
    37→    fp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "false_positive")
    38→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    39→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    40→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    41→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    42→
    43→    # Count meaningful annotations (descriptions and notes)
    44→    described = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("description"))
    45→    noted = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("note"))
    46→
    47→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
    48→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
    49→    print(f"  Queue:            {ordered} items prioritized")
    50→    if total_skipped:
    51→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
    52→    else:
    53→        print(f"  Skipped:          0")
    54→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
    55→    if clusters:
    56→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
    57→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    58→            member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    59→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
    60→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    61→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
    62→    if described or noted:
    63→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
    64→    if active:
    65→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
    66→    if superseded:
    67→        print(f"  Disappeared:      {superseded} (resolved or removed since last scan)")
    68→
    69→
    70→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    71→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
    72→    plan = load_plan()
    73→    reset_plan(plan)
    74→    save_plan(plan)
    75→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
    76→
    77→
    78→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    79→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
    80→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
    81→
    82→    if plan_action is None:
    83→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
    84→        return
    85→
    86→    if plan_action == "show":
    87→        _cmd_plan_show(args)
    88→        return
    89→
    90→    if plan_action == "queue":
    91→        cmd_plan_queue(args)
    92→        return
    93→
    94→    if plan_action == "reset":
    95→        _cmd_plan_reset(args)
    96→        return
    97→
    98→    if plan_action == "move":
    99→        cmd_plan_move(args)
   100→        return
   101→
   102→    if plan_action in (
   103→        "describe", "note", "focus",
   104→        "skip", "unskip", "reopen", "done",
   105→    ):
   106→        dispatch = {
   107→            "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   108→            "done": cmd_plan_done,
   109→            "note": cmd_plan_note,
   110→            "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   111→            "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   112→            "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   113→            "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   114→        }
   115→        dispatch[plan_action](args)
   116→        return
   117→
   118→    if plan_action == "cluster":
   119→        cmd_cluster_dispatch(args)
   120→        return
   121→
   122→    if plan_action == "synthesize":
   123→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.synthesize_handlers import cmd_plan_synthesize
   124→
   125→        cmd_plan_synthesize(args)
   126→        return
   127→
   128→    print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   129→
   130→

    37→def _scorecard_subjective(
    38→    state: dict,
    39→    dim_scores: dict,
    40→) -> list[dict]:
    41→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    42→    return next_render_mod.scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
    43→
    44→
    45→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    46→    state: dict,
    47→    dim_scores: dict,
    48→    *,
    49→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    50→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    51→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    52→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    53→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    54→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    55→            continue
    56→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    57→        if strict_val < threshold:
    58→            low.append(
    59→                (
    60→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    61→                    strict_val,
    62→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    63→                )
    64→            )
    65→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    66→    return low
    67→
    68→
    69→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    70→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    71→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    72→    state = runtime.state
    73→    config = runtime.config
    74→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    75→        return
    76→
    77→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    78→    if stale_warning:
    79→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
    80→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    81→    if skill_warning:
    82→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    83→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    84→    if config_warning:
    85→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    86→
    87→    print_synthesis_guardrail_banner()
    88→    _get_items(args, state, config)
    89→
    90→
    91→def _resolve_cluster_focus(
    92→    plan_data: dict | None,
    93→    *,
    94→    cluster_arg: str | None,
    95→    scope: str | None,
    96→) -> str | None:
    97→    effective_cluster = cluster_arg
    98→    if plan_data and not cluster_arg and not scope:
    99→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
   100→        if active_cluster:
   101→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   102→    return effective_cluster
   103→
   104→
   105→def _build_next_payload(
   106→    *,
   107→    queue: dict,
   108→    items: list[dict],
   109→    state: dict,
   110→    narrative: dict,
   111→    plan_data: dict | None,
   112→) -> dict:
   113→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   114→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative, plan=plan_data
   115→    )
   116→    payload["overall_score"] = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   117→    payload["objective_score"] = state_mod.get_objective_score(state)
   118→    payload["strict_score"] = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   119→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   120→        state,
   121→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   122→    )
   123→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   124→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   125→    ]
   126→    return payload
   127→
   128→
   129→def _emit_requested_output(
   130→    args,
   131→    payload: dict,
   132→    items: list[dict],
   133→) -> bool:
   134→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   135→    if output_file:
   136→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   137→            output_file,
   138→            payload,
   139→            len(items),
   140→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   141→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   142→        ):
   143→            return True
   144→        raise SystemExit(1)
   145→
   146→    output_format = getattr(args, "format", "terminal")
   147→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(output_format, payload, items):
   148→        return True
   149→    return False
   150→
   151→
   152→def _plan_queue_context(
   153→    *,
   154→    state: dict,
   155→    plan_data: dict | None,
   156→) -> tuple[float | None, int]:
   157→    plan_start_strict = get_plan_start_strict(plan_data)
   158→    if plan_start_strict is None:
   159→        return None, 0
   160→    try:
   161→        queue_total = plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan_data)
   162→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   163→        queue_total = 0
   164→    return plan_start_strict, queue_total
   165→
   166→
   167→def _merge_potentials_safe(raw_potentials: dict | None) -> dict | None:
   168→    try:
   169→        return merge_potentials(raw_potentials) or None
   170→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError):
   171→        return raw_potentials or None
   172→
   173→
   174→def _get_items(args, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
   175→    tier = getattr(args, "tier", None)
   176→    count = getattr(args, "count", 1) or 1
   177→    scope = getattr(args, "scope", None)
   178→    status = getattr(args, "status", "open")
   179→    group = getattr(args, "group", "item")
   180→    explain = bool(getattr(args, "explain", False))
   181→    no_tier_fallback = bool(getattr(args, "no_tier_fallback", False))
   182→    cluster_arg = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
   183→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
   184→
   185→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
   186→
   187→    # Load the living plan
   188→    plan = load_plan()
   189→    plan_data: dict | None = None
   190→    if (
   191→        plan.get("queue_order")
   192→        or plan.get("overrides")
   193→        or plan.get("clusters")
   194→    ):
   195→        plan_data = plan
   196→
   197→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster if set and no explicit scope/cluster
   198→    effective_cluster = _resolve_cluster_focus(
   199→        plan_data,
   200→        cluster_arg=cluster_arg,
   201→        scope=scope,
   202→    )
   203→
   204→    queue = build_work_queue(
   205→        state,
   206→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   207→            tier=tier,
   208→            count=count,
   209→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   210→            scope=scope,
   211→            status=status,
   212→            include_subjective=True,
   213→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   214→            no_tier_fallback=no_tier_fallback,
   215→            explain=explain,
   216→            plan=plan_data,
   217→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   218→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   219→        ),
   220→    )
   221→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   222→
   223→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   224→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   225→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   226→        state,
   227→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next", plan=plan_data),
   228→    )
   229→
   230→    payload = _build_next_payload(
   231→        queue=queue,
   232→        items=items,
   233→        state=state,
   234→        narrative=narrative,
   235→        plan_data=plan_data,
   236→    )
   237→    write_query(payload)
   238→
   239→    if _emit_requested_output(args, payload, items):
   240→        return
   241→
   242→    # Extract frozen plan-start score and queue total for lifecycle display
   243→    plan_start_strict, queue_total = _plan_queue_context(
   244→        state=state,
   245→        plan_data=plan_data,
   246→    )
   247→
   248→    next_render_mod.render_queue_header(queue, explain)
   249→    strict_score = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   250→    if next_render_mod.show_empty_queue(
   251→        queue,
   252→        tier,
   253→        strict_score,
   254→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   255→        target_strict=target_strict,
   256→    ):
   257→        return
   258→
   259→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   260→    findings_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   261→        state.get("findings", {}),
   262→        state.get("scan_path"),
   263→    )
   264→    raw_potentials = state.get("potentials", {})
   265→    potentials = _merge_potentials_safe(raw_potentials)
   266→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   267→        items, dim_scores, findings_scoped, group=group, explain=explain,
   268→        potentials=potentials, plan=plan_data,
   269→        cluster_filter=effective_cluster,
   270→    )
   271→    next_render_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   272→    next_render_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   273→        state,
   274→        dim_scores,
   275→        findings_scoped,
   276→        strict_score=strict_score,
   277→        target_strict_score=target_strict,
   278→        queue_total=queue_total,
   279→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   280→    )
   281→    print()
   282→
   283→
   284→__all__ = ["_low_subjective_dimensions", "cmd_next"]
   285→

    37→def _resolve_state_file(path: Path | None) -> Path:
    38→    return path if path is not None else state_mod.STATE_FILE

    40→
    41→def _resolve_plan_file(path: Path | None) -> Path:
    42→    return path if path is not None else PLAN_FILE
    43→
    44→
    45→def _plan_file_for_state(state_file: Path | None) -> Path | None:
    46→    if state_file is None:
    47→        return None
    48→    return plan_path_for_state(state_file)
    49→
    50→
    51→def _snapshot_file(path: Path) -> str | None:
    52→    if not path.exists():
    53→        return None
    54→    return path.read_text()
    55→
    56→
    57→def _restore_file_snapshot(path: Path, snapshot: str | None) -> None:
    58→    if snapshot is None:
    59→        try:
    60→            path.unlink()
    61→        except FileNotFoundError:
    62→            return
    63→        return
    64→    safe_write_text(path, snapshot)
    65→
    66→
    67→def _save_plan_state_transactional(
    68→    *,
    69→    plan: dict,
    70→    plan_path: Path | None,
    71→    state_data: dict,
    72→    state_path_value: Path | None,
    73→) -> None:
    74→    """Persist plan+state together; rollback both files on partial write failure."""
    75→    effective_plan_path = _resolve_plan_file(plan_path)
    76→    effective_state_path = _resolve_state_file(state_path_value)
    77→    plan_snapshot = _snapshot_file(effective_plan_path)
    78→    state_snapshot = _snapshot_file(effective_state_path)
    79→
    80→    try:
    81→        state_mod.save_state(state_data, effective_state_path)
    82→        save_plan(plan, effective_plan_path)
    83→    except Exception:
    84→        _restore_file_snapshot(effective_state_path, state_snapshot)
    85→        _restore_file_snapshot(effective_plan_path, plan_snapshot)
    86→        raise

   135→def cmd_plan_skip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   136→    """Skip findings — unified command for temporary/permanent/false-positive."""
   137→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   138→    state = runtime.state
   139→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   140→        return
   141→
   142→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   143→    reason: str | None = getattr(args, "reason", None)
   144→    review_after: int | None = getattr(args, "review_after", None)
   145→    permanent: bool = getattr(args, "permanent", False)
   146→    false_positive: bool = getattr(args, "false_positive", False)
   147→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   148→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   149→
   150→    # Determine skip kind
   151→    if false_positive:
   152→        kind = "false_positive"
   153→    elif permanent:
   154→        kind = "permanent"
   155→    else:
   156→        kind = "temporary"
   157→
   158→    # Validate requirements for permanent/false_positive
   159→    if kind in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   160→        if not validate_attestation(attestation):
   161→            show_attestation_requirement(
   162→                "Permanent skip" if kind == "permanent" else "False positive",
   163→                attestation,
   164→                ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
   165→            )
   166→            return
   167→        if kind == "permanent" and not note:
   168→            print(
   169→                colorize("  --permanent requires --note to explain the decision.", "yellow"),
   170→                file=sys.stderr,
   171→            )
   172→            return
   173→
   174→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   175→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   176→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   177→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
   178→    if not finding_ids:
   179→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
   180→        return
   181→
   182→    # For permanent/false_positive: delegate to state layer for score impact
   183→    state_data: dict | None = None
   184→    if kind in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   185→        state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   186→        status = "wontfix" if kind == "permanent" else "false_positive"
   187→        for fid in finding_ids:
   188→            state_mod.resolve_findings(
   189→                state_data, fid, status, note or "", attestation=attestation
   190→            )
   191→
   192→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   193→    count = skip_items(
   194→        plan,
   195→        finding_ids,
   196→        kind=kind,
   197→        reason=reason,
   198→        note=note,
   199→        attestation=attestation,
   200→        review_after=review_after,
   201→        scan_count=scan_count,
   202→    )
   203→
   204→    # Log the skip action
   205→    append_log_entry(
   206→        plan,
   207→        "skip",
   208→        finding_ids=finding_ids,
   209→        actor="user",
   210→        note=note,
   211→        detail={"kind": kind, "reason": reason},
   212→    )
   213→    if state_data is not None:
   214→        _save_plan_state_transactional(
   215→            plan=plan,
   216→            plan_path=plan_file,
   217→            state_data=state_data,
   218→            state_path_value=state_file,
   219→        )
   220→    else:
   221→        save_plan(plan, plan_file)
   222→
   223→    label = {"temporary": "Skipped", "permanent": "Wontfixed", "false_positive": "Marked false positive"}
   224→    print(colorize(f"  {label[kind]} {count} item(s).", "green"))
   225→    if review_after:
   226→        print(colorize(f"  Will re-surface after {review_after} scan(s).", "dim"))
   227→

   229→def cmd_plan_unskip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   230→    """Unskip findings — bring back to queue."""
   231→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   232→    state = runtime.state
   233→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   234→        return

   236→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])

   241→    # For unskip we need to match against all statuses (skipped items may be wontfix/fp)
   242→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan, status_filter="all")
   243→    if not finding_ids:
   244→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
   245→        return
   246→
   247→    count, need_reopen = unskip_items(plan, finding_ids)
   248→
   249→    # Reopen permanent/false_positive items in state
   250→    reopened: list[str] = []
   251→    if need_reopen:
   252→        state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   253→        for fid in need_reopen:
   254→            reopened.extend(state_mod.resolve_findings(state_data, fid, "open"))
   255→        _save_plan_state_transactional(
   256→            plan=plan,
   257→            plan_path=plan_file,
   258→            state_data=state_data,
   259→            state_path_value=state_file,
   260→        )
   261→        print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} finding(s) in state.", "dim"))
   262→    else:
   263→        save_plan(plan, plan_file)
   264→
   265→    print(colorize(f"  Unskipped {count} item(s) — back in queue.", "green"))

   271→
   272→def cmd_plan_reopen(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   273→    """Reopen resolved findings from plan context."""
   274→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   275→
   276→    raw_state_path = state_path(args)
   277→    state_file = raw_state_path if isinstance(raw_state_path, Path) else Path(raw_state_path) if raw_state_path else None
   278→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   279→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   280→
   281→    reopened: list[str] = []
   282→    for pattern in patterns:
   283→        reopened.extend(
   284→            state_mod.resolve_findings(state_data, pattern, "open")
   285→        )
   286→
   287→    if not reopened:
   288→        print(colorize("  No resolved findings matching: " + " ".join(patterns), "yellow"))
   289→        return
   290→
   291→    # Remove from skipped if present, and ensure all reopened IDs are in queue
   292→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   293→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   294→    count = 0
   295→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   296→    for fid in reopened:
   297→        if fid in skipped:
   298→            skipped.pop(fid)
   299→            count += 1
   300→        if fid not in order:
   301→            plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   302→            order.add(fid)
   303→            count += 1
   304→    _save_plan_state_transactional(
   305→        plan=plan,
   306→        plan_path=plan_file,
   307→        state_data=state_data,
   308→        state_path_value=state_file,
   309→    )
   310→
   311→    print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} finding(s).", "green"))
   312→    if count:
   313→        print(colorize(f"  Plan updated: items moved back to queue.", "dim"))

   316→_CLUSTER_INDIVIDUAL_THRESHOLD = 10
   317→
   318→
   319→def _check_cluster_guard(patterns: list[str], plan: dict, state: dict) -> bool:
   320→    """Return True if blocked by cluster guard, False if OK to proceed."""
   321→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   322→    for pattern in patterns:
   323→        if pattern in clusters:
   324→            cluster = clusters[pattern]
   325→            ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   326→            if 0 < len(ids) <= _CLUSTER_INDIVIDUAL_THRESHOLD:
   327→                _print_cluster_guard(pattern, ids, state)
   328→                return True  # blocked
   329→    return False  # OK
   330→
   331→
   332→def _print_cluster_guard(cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str], state: dict) -> None:
   333→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   334→    print(colorize(
   335→        f"\n  Cluster '{cluster_name}' has {len(finding_ids)} item(s) — mark them done individually first:\n",
   336→        "yellow",
   337→    ))
   338→    for fid in finding_ids:
   339→        f = findings.get(fid, {})
   340→        summary = f.get("summary", "(no summary)")[:80]
   341→        detector = f.get("detector", "?")
   342→        print(f"    {fid}  [{detector}]  {summary}")
   343→    print(colorize(
   344→        f"\n  Use: desloppify resolve <id> --status fixed --note '...' --attest '...'",
   345→        "dim",
   346→    ))
   347→    print(colorize(
   348→        f"  Or mark each done: desloppify plan done <id> --note '...' --confirm\n",
   349→        "dim",
   350→    ))
   351→

   353→def cmd_plan_done(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   354→    """Mark findings as fixed — delegates to cmd_resolve for rich UX."""
   355→    from desloppify.app.commands.resolve.cmd import cmd_resolve
   356→
   357→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   358→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   359→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   360→
   361→    # --confirm: auto-generate attestation from --note
   362→    if getattr(args, "confirm", False):
   363→        if not note:
   364→            print(colorize("  --confirm requires --note to describe what you did.", "red"))
   365→            return
   366→        attestation = f"I have actually {note} and I am not gaming the score."
   367→        args.attest = attestation
   368→
   369→    # Validate note length
   370→    if not validate_note_length(note):
   371→        show_note_length_requirement(note)
   372→        return
   373→
   374→    # Pre-validate attestation before delegating (avoids stale hint in resolve)
   375→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
   376→        show_attestation_requirement("Plan done", attestation, ATTEST_EXAMPLE)
   377→        return
   378→
   379→    # Cluster completion guard: block bulk-completing small clusters
   380→    try:
   381→        runtime = command_runtime(args)
   382→        state = runtime.state
   383→        plan = load_plan()
   384→        if _check_cluster_guard(patterns, plan, state):
   385→            return
   386→    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
   387→        plan = None
   388→
   389→    # Log the done action (best-effort)
   390→    try:
   391→        if plan is None:
   392→            plan = load_plan()
   393→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   394→        cluster_name = None
   395→        for p in patterns:
   396→            if p in clusters:
   397→                cluster_name = p
   398→                break
   399→        append_log_entry(
   400→            plan,
   401→            "done",
   402→            finding_ids=patterns,
   403→            cluster_name=cluster_name,
   404→            actor="user",
   405→            note=note,
   406→        )
   407→        save_plan(plan)
   408→    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
   409→        pass  # best-effort logging
   410→
   411→    # Build a Namespace that cmd_resolve expects
   412→    resolve_args = argparse.Namespace(
   413→        status="fixed",
   414→        patterns=patterns,
   415→        note=note,
   416→        attest=attestation,
   417→        confirm_batch_wontfix=False,
   418→        state=getattr(args, "state", None),
   419→        lang=getattr(args, "lang", None),
   420→        path=getattr(args, "path", None),
   421→        exclude=getattr(args, "exclude", None),
   422→    )
   423→
   424→    cmd_resolve(resolve_args)
   425→
   426→
   427→def cmd_plan_focus(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   428→    """Set or clear the active cluster focus."""
   429→    clear_flag = getattr(args, "clear", False)
   430→    cluster_name: str | None = getattr(args, "cluster_name", None)
   431→
   432→    plan = load_plan()
   433→    if clear_flag:
   434→        clear_focus(plan)
   435→        save_plan(plan)
   436→        print(colorize("  Focus cleared.", "green"))
   437→        return
   438→
   439→    if not cluster_name:
   440→        active = plan.get("active_cluster")
   441→        if active:
   442→            print(f"  Focused on: {active}")
   443→        else:
   444→            print("  No active focus.")
   445→        return
   446→
   447→    try:
   448→        set_focus(plan, cluster_name)
   449→    except ValueError as ex:
   450→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
   451→        return
   452→    save_plan(plan)
   453→    print(colorize(f"  Focused on: {cluster_name}", "green"))
   454→
   455→
   456→__all__ = [
   457→    "cmd_plan_describe",
   458→    "cmd_plan_done",
   459→    "cmd_plan_focus",
   460→    "cmd_plan_note",
   461→    "cmd_plan_reopen",
   462→    "cmd_plan_skip",
   463→    "cmd_plan_unskip",
   464→]
   465→

    73→def _clear_needs_rescan_flag(config: dict[str, object]) -> None:

    99→    recon = reconcile_fn(plan, state)

   148→def _seed_plan_start_scores(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   149→    """Set plan_start_scores when beginning a new queue cycle."""
   150→    if plan.get("plan_start_scores"):

   153→    if scores.strict is None:

   164→def _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(
   165→    state: state_mod.StateModel, plan: dict[str, object]
   166→) -> bool:
   167→    """Clear plan-start score snapshot once the queue is fully drained."""
   168→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   169→        return False
   170→
   171→    try:
   172→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import plan_aware_queue_count
   173→
   174→        queue_empty = plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan) == 0
   175→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:

   178→    if not queue_empty:

   181→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}

   185→def _reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: "ScanRuntime") -> None:
   186→    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
   187→    try:
   188→        plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json" if runtime.state_path else None
   189→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   190→        dirty = False
   191→
   192→        if _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan, runtime.state, reconcile_plan_after_scan):
   193→            dirty = True
   194→        if _sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, runtime.state, sync_unscored_dimensions):
   195→            dirty = True
   196→        if _sync_stale_dimensions(plan, runtime.state, sync_stale_dimensions):
   197→            dirty = True
   198→        if _sync_auto_clusters(plan, runtime.state):
   199→            dirty = True
   200→        synth_sync = sync_synthesis_needed(plan, runtime.state)
   201→        if synth_sync.changes:
   202→            dirty = True
   203→            if synth_sync.injected:
   204→                print(
   205→                    colorize(
   206→                        "  Plan: synthesis needed — review findings changed since last synthesis.",
   207→                        "cyan",
   208→                    )
   209→                )
   210→        seeded = _seed_plan_start_scores(plan, runtime.state)
   211→        if seeded:
   212→            dirty = True
   213→        # Only clear scores that existed before this reconcile pass —
   214→        # never clear scores we just seeded in the same scan.
   215→        if not seeded and _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(runtime.state, plan):
   216→            dirty = True
   217→
   218→        if dirty:
   219→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   220→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   221→        logging.debug("Plan operation skipped: %s", exc)

   267→@dataclass
   268→class ScanRuntime:
   269→    """Resolved runtime context for a single scan invocation."""
   270→
   271→    args: argparse.Namespace
   272→    state_path: Path | None
   273→    state: state_mod.StateModel

   283→    coverage_warnings: list[DetectorCoverageRecord] = field(default_factory=list)
   284→

   308→
   309→def _configure_lang_runtime(
   310→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   311→    config: dict[str, object],
   312→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   313→    lang: LangRun | None,
   314→) -> LangRun | None:
   315→    """Populate runtime context and threshold overrides for a selected language."""
   316→    if not lang:
   317→        return None
   318→
   319→    lang_options = resolve_lang_runtime_options(args, lang)
   320→    lang_settings = resolve_lang_settings(config, lang)
   321→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(
   322→        lang,
   323→        overrides=LangRunOverrides(
   324→            review_cache=state.get("review_cache", {}),
   325→            review_max_age_days=config.get("review_max_age_days", 30),
   326→            runtime_settings=lang_settings,
   327→            runtime_options=lang_options,
   328→            large_threshold_override=config.get("large_files_threshold", 0),
   329→            props_threshold_override=config.get("props_threshold", 0),
   330→        ),
   331→    )
   332→
   333→    lang_capabilities = _state_lang_capabilities(state)
   334→    lang_capabilities[runtime_lang.name] = {
   335→        "fixers": sorted(runtime_lang.fixers.keys()),
   336→        "typecheck_cmd": runtime_lang.typecheck_cmd,
   337→    }
   338→    return runtime_lang
   339→
   340→
   341→def _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   342→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   343→    *,
   344→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   345→) -> int:
   346→    """Reset known subjective dimensions to 0 so the next scan starts fresh."""
   347→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   348→
   349→    reset_keys = {
   350→        key.strip()
   351→        for key in assessments
   352→        if isinstance(key, str) and key.strip()
   353→    }
   354→    reset_keys.update(_subjective_reset_dimensions(lang_name=lang_name))
   355→
   356→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   357→    for key in sorted(reset_keys):
   358→        payload = assessments.get(key)
   359→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   360→            payload["score"] = 0.0
   361→            payload["source"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   362→            payload["assessed_at"] = now
   363→            payload["reset_by"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   364→            payload["placeholder"] = True
   365→            payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   366→            payload.pop("components", None)
   367→            payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   368→            continue
   369→        assessments[key] = {
   370→            "score": 0.0,
   371→            "source": "scan_reset_subjective",
   372→            "assessed_at": now,
   373→            "reset_by": "scan_reset_subjective",
   374→            "placeholder": True,
   375→        }
   376→    return len(reset_keys)
   377→
   378→
   379→def _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   380→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   381→) -> int:
   382→    """Expire provisional manual-override assessments at scan start."""
   383→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   384→
   385→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   386→    expired = 0
   387→    for payload in assessments.values():
   388→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   389→            continue
   390→        if payload.get("provisional_override") is not True:
   391→            continue
   392→        payload["score"] = 0.0
   393→        payload["source"] = "manual_override_expired"
   394→        payload["assessed_at"] = now
   395→        payload["reset_by"] = "manual_override_expired"
   396→        payload["placeholder"] = True
   397→        payload.pop("provisional_override", None)
   398→        payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   399→        payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   400→        payload.pop("components", None)
   401→        payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   402→        expired += 1
   403→    return expired
   404→
   405→
   406→def prepare_scan_runtime(args) -> ScanRuntime:
   407→    """Resolve state/config/language and apply scan-time runtime settings."""
   408→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   409→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   410→    state = runtime.state if isinstance(runtime.state, dict) else {}
   411→    state_mod.ensure_state_defaults(state)
   412→    path = Path(args.path)
   413→    config = runtime.config if isinstance(runtime.config, dict) else {}
   414→    lang_config = resolve_lang(args)
   415→    reset_subjective_count = 0
   416→    expired_manual_override_count = _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   417→        state
   418→    )
   419→    if getattr(args, "reset_subjective", False):
   420→        reset_subjective_count = _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   421→            state,
   422→            lang_name=getattr(lang_config, "name", None),
   423→        )
   424→
   425→    include_slow = not getattr(args, "skip_slow", False)
   426→    profile = _resolve_scan_profile(getattr(args, "profile", None), lang_config)
   427→    effective_include_slow = _effective_include_slow(include_slow, profile)
   428→
   429→    lang = _configure_lang_runtime(args, config, state, lang_config)
   430→    coverage_warnings = _seed_runtime_coverage_warnings(lang)
   431→    zone_overrides_raw = config.get("zone_overrides")
   432→    zone_overrides = zone_overrides_raw if isinstance(zone_overrides_raw, dict) else None
   433→
   434→    return ScanRuntime(
   435→        args=args,
   436→        state_path=state_file,
   437→        state=state,
   438→        path=path,
   439→        config=config,
   440→        lang=lang,
   441→        lang_label=f" ({lang.name})" if lang else "",
   442→        profile=profile,
   443→        effective_include_slow=effective_include_slow,
   444→        zone_overrides=zone_overrides,
   445→        reset_subjective_count=reset_subjective_count,
   446→        expired_manual_override_count=expired_manual_override_count,
   447→        coverage_warnings=coverage_warnings,
   448→    )
   449→
   450→
   451→def _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(
   452→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   453→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   454→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   455→    """Append stale exclude findings when excluded dirs are unreferenced."""
   456→    extra_exclusions = get_exclusions()
   457→    if not (extra_exclusions and runtime.lang and runtime.lang.file_finder):
   458→        return findings
   459→
   460→    scanned_files = runtime.lang.file_finder(runtime.path)
   461→    stale = _audit_excluded_dirs(
   462→        extra_exclusions, scanned_files, PROJECT_ROOT
   463→    )
   464→    if not stale:
   465→        return findings
   466→
   467→    augmented = list(findings)
   468→    augmented.extend(stale)
   469→    for stale_finding in stale:
   470→        print(colorize(f"  ℹ {stale_finding['summary']}", "dim"))
   471→    return augmented
   472→
   473→
   474→def _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   475→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   476→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   477→    *,
   478→    decay_scans: int,
   479→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], int]:
   480→    """Append re-triage findings for stale/worsening wontfix debt."""
   481→    return _augment_stale_wontfix_impl(
   482→        findings,
   483→        state=runtime.state,
   484→        scan_path=runtime.path,
   485→        project_root=PROJECT_ROOT,
   486→        decay_scans=decay_scans,
   487→    )
   488→
   489→
   490→def run_scan_generation(
   491→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   492→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None]:
   493→    """Run detector pipeline and return findings, potentials, and codebase metrics."""
   494→    enable_file_cache()
   495→    enable_parse_cache()
   496→    try:
   497→        findings, potentials = plan_mod.generate_findings(
   498→            runtime.path,
   499→            lang=runtime.lang,
   500→            options=PlanScanOptions(
   501→                include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   502→                zone_overrides=runtime.zone_overrides,
   503→                profile=runtime.profile,
   504→            ),
   505→        )
   506→    finally:
   507→        disable_parse_cache()
   508→        disable_file_cache()
   509→
   510→    codebase_metrics = _collect_codebase_metrics(runtime.lang, runtime.path)
   511→    _warn_explicit_lang_with_no_files(
   512→        runtime.args, runtime.lang, runtime.path, codebase_metrics
   513→    )
   514→    findings = _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(findings, runtime)
   515→    decay_scans = _coerce_int(
   516→        runtime.config.get("wontfix_decay_scans"),
   517→        default=_WONTFIX_DECAY_SCANS_DEFAULT,
   518→    )
   519→    findings, monitored_wontfix = _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   520→        findings,
   521→        runtime,
   522→        decay_scans=max(decay_scans, 0),
   523→    )
   524→    potentials["stale_wontfix"] = monitored_wontfix
   525→    return findings, potentials, codebase_metrics
   526→
   527→
   528→def merge_scan_results(
   529→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   530→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   531→    potentials: dict[str, object],
   532→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, object] | None,
   533→) -> ScanMergeResult:
   534→    """Merge findings into persistent state and return diff + previous score snapshot."""
   535→    scan_path_rel = rel(str(runtime.path))
   536→    prev_scan_path = runtime.state.get("scan_path")
   537→    path_changed = prev_scan_path is not None and prev_scan_path != scan_path_rel
   538→
   539→    if not path_changed:
   540→        prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(runtime.state)
   541→    else:
   542→        prev = state_mod.ScoreSnapshot(None, None, None, None)
   543→    prev_dim_scores = (
   544→        runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {}) if not path_changed else {}
   545→    )
   546→
   547→    if runtime.lang and runtime.lang.zone_map is not None:
   548→        runtime.state["zone_distribution"] = runtime.lang.zone_map.counts()
   549→    _persist_scan_coverage(runtime.state, runtime.lang)
   550→
   551→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   552→
   553→    diff = state_mod.merge_scan(
   554→        runtime.state,
   555→        findings,
   556→        options=state_mod.MergeScanOptions(
   557→            lang=runtime.lang.name if runtime.lang else None,
   558→            scan_path=scan_path_rel,
   559→            force_resolve=getattr(runtime.args, "force_resolve", False),
   560→            exclude=get_exclusions(),
   561→            potentials=potentials,
   562→            codebase_metrics=codebase_metrics,
   563→            include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   564→            ignore=runtime.config.get("ignore", []),
   565→            subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   566→        ),
   567→    )
   568→
   569→    issues_mod.expire_stale_holistic(
   570→        runtime.state, runtime.config.get("holistic_max_age_days", 30)
   571→    )
   572→    state_mod.save_state(
   573→        runtime.state,
   574→        runtime.state_path,
   575→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   576→    )
   577→
   578→    _clear_needs_rescan_flag(runtime.config)
   579→    _reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime)
   580→
   581→    return ScanMergeResult(
   582→        diff=diff,
   583→        prev_overall=prev.overall,
   584→        prev_objective=prev.objective,
   585→        prev_strict=prev.strict,
   586→        prev_verified=prev.verified,
   587→        prev_dim_scores=prev_dim_scores,
   588→    )
   589→
   590→
   591→def resolve_noise_snapshot(
   592→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   593→    config: dict[str, object],
   594→) -> ScanNoiseSnapshot:
   595→    """Resolve noise budget settings and hidden finding counters."""
   596→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   597→        state_mod.resolve_finding_noise_settings(config)
   598→    )
   599→    findings_by_id = _state_findings(state)
   600→    open_findings = [
   601→        finding
   602→        for finding in state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   603→            findings_by_id, state.get("scan_path")
   604→        ).values()
   605→        if finding.get("status") == "open"
   606→    ]
   607→    _, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_finding_noise_budget(
   608→        open_findings,
   609→        budget=noise_budget,
   610→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   611→    )
   612→
   613→    return ScanNoiseSnapshot(
   614→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   615→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   616→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   617→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   618→        hidden_total=sum(hidden_by_detector.values()),
   619→    )
   620→
   621→
   622→def persist_reminder_history(
   623→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   624→    narrative: dict[str, object],
   625→) -> None:
   626→    """Persist reminder history emitted by narrative computation."""
   627→    if not (narrative and "reminder_history" in narrative):
   628→        return
   629→
   630→    runtime.state["reminder_history"] = narrative["reminder_history"]
   631→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   632→    state_mod.save_state(
   633→        runtime.state,
   634→        runtime.state_path,
   635→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   636→    )
   637→
   638→
   639→__all__ = [
   640→    "ScanStateContractError",
   641→    "ScanMergeResult",
   642→    "ScanNoiseSnapshot",
   643→    "ScanRuntime",
   644→    "merge_scan_results",
   645→    "persist_reminder_history",
   646→    "prepare_scan_runtime",
   647→    "resolve_noise_snapshot",
   648→    "run_scan_generation",
   649→]
   650→

    27→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    28→class QueueBuildOptions:
    29→    """Configuration for queue construction and tier selection behavior."""
    30→
    31→    tier: int | None = None
    32→    count: int | None = 1
    33→    scan_path: str | None = None
    34→    scope: str | None = None
    35→    status: str = "open"
    36→    include_subjective: bool = True
    37→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    38→    chronic: bool = False
    39→    no_tier_fallback: bool = False
    40→    explain: bool = False
    41→    plan: dict | None = None
    42→    include_skipped: bool = False
    43→    cluster: str | None = None
    44→    collapse_clusters: bool = True
    45→
    46→
    47→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    48→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    49→
    50→    items: list[dict]
    51→    total: int
    52→    tier_counts: dict[int, int]
    53→    requested_tier: int | None
    54→    selected_tier: int | None
    55→    fallback_reason: str | None
    56→    available_tiers: list[int]
    57→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    58→
    59→
    60→def _is_subjective_queue_item(item: dict) -> bool:
    61→    """Return True for queue items that represent subjective work."""
    62→    return item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
    63→
    64→
    65→def _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(
    66→    items: list[dict],
    67→    *,
    68→    objective_burst: int = 3,
    69→    subjective_burst: int = 1,
    70→) -> list[dict]:
    71→    """Interleave subjective work with objective work to avoid starvation."""
    72→    if objective_burst < 1 or subjective_burst < 1:
    73→        return items
    74→
    75→    subjective_items = deque(
    76→        item for item in items if _is_subjective_queue_item(item)
    77→    )
    78→    objective_items = deque(
    79→        item for item in items if not _is_subjective_queue_item(item)
    80→    )
    81→    if not subjective_items or not objective_items:
    82→        return items
    83→
    84→    interleaved: list[dict] = []
    85→    while objective_items and subjective_items:
    86→        for _ in range(objective_burst):
    87→            if not objective_items:
    88→                break
    89→            interleaved.append(objective_items.popleft())
    90→        for _ in range(subjective_burst):
    91→            if not subjective_items:
    92→                break
    93→            interleaved.append(subjective_items.popleft())
    94→
    95→    interleaved.extend(objective_items)
    96→    interleaved.extend(subjective_items)
    97→    return interleaved
    98→
    99→
   100→def _apply_plan_order(
   101→    items: list[dict],
   102→    plan: dict,
   103→    *,
   104→    include_skipped: bool = False,
   105→    cluster: str | None = None,
   106→) -> list[dict]:
   107→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
   108→
   109→    1. Items in ``queue_order`` appear first, in that order.
   110→    2. Remaining items keep their mechanical sort.
   111→    3. Skipped items are appended last (or excluded).
   112→    4. Each item is annotated with plan metadata.
   113→    """
   114→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   115→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   116→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
   117→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
   118→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
   119→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
   120→
   121→    # Build lookup
   122→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
   123→    for item in items:
   124→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
   125→
   126→    # Annotate items with plan metadata
   127→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
   128→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
   129→        if override.get("description"):
   130→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
   131→        if override.get("note"):
   132→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
   133→        if override.get("cluster"):
   134→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
   135→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   136→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
   137→                "name": cluster_name,
   138→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
   139→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
   140→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
   141→            }
   142→
   143→    # Split into ordered, remaining, skipped
   144→    ordered: list[dict] = []
   145→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
   146→    for fid in queue_order:
   147→        if fid in by_id and fid not in skipped_ids:
   148→            ordered.append(by_id[fid])
   149→            ordered_ids.add(fid)
   150→

    19→
    20→@dataclass
    21→class PlanScanOptions:
    22→    """Config object for scan execution behavior."""
    23→
    24→    include_slow: bool = True
    25→    zone_overrides: dict[str, str] | None = None
    26→    profile: str = "full"
    27→
    28→
    29→def _stderr(msg: str) -> None:
    30→    print(colorize(msg, "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
    31→
    32→
    33→def _resolve_lang(
    34→    lang: LangConfig | LangRun | None, project_root: Path
    35→) -> LangConfig | LangRun:
    36→    if lang is not None:
    37→        return lang
    38→
    39→    detected = auto_detect_lang(project_root)
    40→    if detected is None:
    41→        langs = available_langs()
    42→        if not langs:
    43→            raise ValueError("No language plugins available")
    44→        detected = langs[0]
    45→    return get_lang(detected)
    46→
    47→
    48→def _build_zone_map(path: Path, lang: LangRun, zone_overrides: dict[str, str] | None) -> None:
    49→    if not (lang.zone_rules and lang.file_finder):
    50→        return
    51→
    52→    files = lang.file_finder(path)
    53→    lang.zone_map = FileZoneMap(
    54→        files, lang.zone_rules, rel_fn=rel, overrides=zone_overrides
    55→    )
    56→    counts = lang.zone_map.counts()
    57→    zone_str = ", ".join(
    58→        f"{zone}: {count}" for zone, count in sorted(counts.items()) if count > 0
    59→    )
    60→    _stderr(f"  Zones: {zone_str}")
    61→
    62→    from desloppify.languages._framework.generic import capability_report
    63→
    64→    report = capability_report(lang)
    65→    if report is not None:
    66→        present, missing = report
    67→        if present:
    68→            _stderr(f"  Capabilities: {', '.join(present)}")
    69→        if missing:
    70→            _stderr(f"  Not available: {', '.join(missing)}")
    71→
    72→
    73→def _select_phases(lang: LangRun, *, include_slow: bool, profile: str) -> list[DetectorPhase]:
    74→    active_profile = profile if profile in {"objective", "full", "ci"} else "full"
    75→    phases = lang.phases
    76→    if not include_slow or active_profile == "ci":
    77→        phases = [phase for phase in phases if not phase.slow]
    78→    if active_profile in {"objective", "ci"}:
    79→        phases = [phase for phase in phases if not is_subjective_phase(phase)]
    80→    return phases
    81→
    82→
    83→def _run_phases(path: Path, lang: LangRun, phases: list[DetectorPhase]) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
    84→    findings: list[Finding] = []
    85→    all_potentials: dict[str, int] = {}
    86→
    87→    total = len(phases)
    88→    for idx, phase in enumerate(phases, start=1):
    89→        _stderr(f"  [{idx}/{total}] {phase.label}...")
    90→        phase_findings, phase_potentials = phase.run(path, lang)
    91→        all_potentials.update(phase_potentials)
    92→        findings.extend(phase_findings)
    93→
    94→    return findings, all_potentials
    95→
    96→
    97→def _stamp_finding_context(findings: list[Finding], lang: LangRun) -> None:
    98→    if not findings:
    99→        return
   100→

    79→def synthesis_phase_banner(plan: PlanModel) -> str:
    80→    """Return a banner string when ``synthesis::pending`` is in the queue."""
    81→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    82→    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
    83→        return ""
    84→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    85→    stages = meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})
    86→    completed = [s for s in ("observe", "reflect", "organize") if s in stages]
    87→    if completed:
    88→        return (
    89→            f"Synthesis in progress ({len(completed)}/4 stages complete). "
    90→            "Run: desloppify plan synthesize"
    91→        )
    92→    return (
    93→        "Synthesis needed — review findings require analysis before fixing. "
    94→        "Run: desloppify plan synthesize"
    95→    )

   155→        if item_id in ordered_ids:

   157→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
   158→            skipped_items.append(item)
   159→        else:
   160→            remaining.append(item)
   161→
   162→    # Assign queue positions
   163→    result = ordered + remaining
   164→    if include_skipped:
   165→        result = result + skipped_items
   166→
   167→    for pos, item in enumerate(result):
   168→        item["queue_position"] = pos + 1
   169→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   170→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   171→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   172→            if skip_entry:
   173→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   174→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   175→                if skip_reason:
   176→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   177→
   178→    # Filter to cluster if requested
   179→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   180→    if effective_cluster:
   181→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   182→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   183→        if cluster_member_ids:
   184→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   185→
   186→    return result
   187→
   188→
   189→def _item_matches_tier(item: dict, tier: int) -> bool:
   190→    """Check if an item (or any of its members for clusters) matches a tier."""
   191→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   192→        return any(
   193→            int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   194→            for m in item.get("members", [])
   195→        )
   196→    return int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   197→
   198→
   199→def _action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   200→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   201→    try:
   202→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   203→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   204→        if meta:
   205→            return meta.action_type
   206→    except ImportError as exc:
   207→        _ = exc
   208→    return "manual_fix"
   209→
   210→
   211→_ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
   212→
   213→
   214→def _collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   215→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items."""
   216→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   217→    if not clusters:
   218→        return items
   219→
   220→    # Build mapping: finding_id → auto-cluster name
   221→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   222→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   223→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   224→            continue
   225→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   226→            fid_to_cluster[fid] = name
   227→
   228→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   229→        return items
   230→
   231→    # Collect members for each cluster, preserving order
   232→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   233→    non_cluster_items: list[dict] = []
   234→
   235→    for item in items:
   236→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   237→        if cname:
   238→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   239→        else:
   240→            non_cluster_items.append(item)
   241→
   242→    # Build cluster meta-items
   243→    result: list[dict] = list(non_cluster_items)
   244→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   245→        # Don't collapse singletons — show them as individual findings
   246→        if len(members) < 2:
   247→            result.extend(members)
   248→            continue
   249→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cname, {})
   250→        detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   251→        action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   252→        # Derive action_type from the actual cluster action, not just the detector
   253→        if "desloppify fix" in action:
   254→            action_type = "auto_fix"
   255→        elif "desloppify move" in action:
   256→            action_type = "reorganize"
   257→        else:
   258→            action_type = _action_type_for_detector(detector)
   259→            # If detector says auto_fix but cluster has no fix command, it's really refactor
   260→            if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify fix" not in action:
   261→                action_type = "refactor"
   262→
   263→        # Use stored description but patch the count if it differs from visible members
   264→        stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   265→        total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   266→        if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   267→            # Replace the stored count with the visible count
   268→            summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   269→        else:
   270→            summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   271→
   272→        primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   273→        if not primary_command:
   274→            primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cname} --count 10"
   275→
   276→        meta_item: dict = {
   277→            "id": cname,
   278→            "kind": "cluster",
   279→            "action_type": action_type,
   280→            "summary": summary,
   281→            "members": members,
   282→            "member_count": len(members),
   283→            "primary_command": primary_command,
   284→            "cluster_name": cname,
   285→            "cluster_auto": True,
   286→            "confidence": "high",
   287→            "detector": detector,
   288→            "file": "",
   289→        }
   290→        result.append(meta_item)
   291→
   292→    result.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   293→    return result
   294→
   295→
   296→def build_work_queue(
   297→    state: StateModel,
   298→    *,
   299→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   300→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   301→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   302→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   303→
   304→    status = resolved_options.status
   305→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   306→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   307→    try:
   308→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   309→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   310→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   311→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   312→
   313→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   314→        state,
   315→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   316→        status_filter=status,
   317→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   318→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   319→    )
   320→
   321→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   322→    if (
   323→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   324→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   325→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   326→    ):
   327→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   328→            state,
   329→            state.get("findings", {}),
   330→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   331→        )
   332→        for item in subjective_items:
   333→            if scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   334→                all_items.append(item)
   335→
   336→    # Inject synthesis::pending synthetic item when plan requires it
   337→    if resolved_options.plan and status in {"open", "all"}:
   338→        synth_item = build_synthesis_item(resolved_options.plan, state)
   339→        if synth_item is not None:
   340→            all_items.append(synth_item)
   341→
   342→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   343→
   344→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   345→    if resolved_options.plan:
   346→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   347→            all_items,
   348→            resolved_options.plan,
   349→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
