```
1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — handle finding churn."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import EPIC_PREFIX, PlanModel, SupersededEntry, ensure_plan_defaults
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX, SYNTHESIS_ID
    10→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    11→
    12→
    13→SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS = 90
    14→
    15→
    16→@dataclass
    17→class ReconcileResult:
    18→    """Summary of changes made during reconciliation."""
    19→
    20→    superseded: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    21→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    22→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    23→    changes: int = 0
    24→
    25→
    26→def _find_candidates(
    27→    state: StateModel, detector: str, file: str
    28→) -> list[str]:
    29→    """Find open findings that could be remaps for a disappeared finding."""
    30→    candidates: list[str] = []
    31→    for fid, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items():
    32→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    33→            continue
    34→        if finding.get("detector") == detector and finding.get("file") == file:
    35→            candidates.append(fid)
    36→    return candidates
    37→
    38→
    39→def _is_finding_alive(state: StateModel, finding_id: str) -> bool:
    40→    """Return True if the finding exists and is open."""
    41→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    42→    if finding is None:
    43→        return False
    44→    return finding.get("status") == "open"
    45→
    46→
    47→def _supersede_id(
    48→    plan: PlanModel,
    49→    state: StateModel,
    50→    finding_id: str,
    51→    now: str,
    52→) -> bool:
    53→    """Move a disappeared finding to superseded. Returns True if changed."""
    54→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    55→    detector = ""
    56→    file = ""
    57→    summary = ""
    58→    if finding:
    59→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    60→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    61→        summary = finding.get("summary", "")
    62→
    63→    candidates = _find_candidates(state, detector, file) if detector else []
    64→    # Don't include the original in candidates
    65→    candidates = [c for c in candidates if c != finding_id]
    66→
    67→    entry: SupersededEntry = {
    68→        "original_id": finding_id,
    69→        "original_detector": detector,
    70→        "original_file": file,
    71→        "original_summary": summary,
    72→        "status": "superseded",
    73→        "superseded_at": now,
    74→        "remapped_to": None,
    75→        "candidates": candidates[:5],
    76→    }
    77→
    78→    # Preserve any existing override note
    79→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(finding_id)
    80→    if override and override.get("note"):
    81→        entry["note"] = override["note"]
    82→
    83→    plan["superseded"][finding_id] = entry
    84→
    85→    # Remove from queue_order, skipped, cluster finding_ids
    86→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    87→    skipped: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    88→    if finding_id in order:
    89→        order.remove(finding_id)
    90→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    91→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    92→        ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
    93→        if finding_id in ids:
    94→            ids.remove(finding_id)
    95→
    96→    # Clear stale cluster reference from override
    97→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(finding_id)
    98→    if override and override.get("cluster"):
    99→        override["cluster"] = None
   100→        override["updated_at"] = now
   101→
   102→    return True
   103→
   104→
   105→def _prune_old_superseded(plan: PlanModel, now_dt: datetime) -> list[str]:
   106→    """Remove superseded entries older than TTL. Returns pruned IDs."""
   107→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   108→    cutoff = now_dt - timedelta(days=SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS)
   109→    to_prune: list[str] = []
   110→
   111→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   112→        ts = entry.get("superseded_at", "")
   113→        try:
   114→            entry_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)
   115→            if entry_dt.tzinfo is None:
   116→                entry_dt = entry_dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
   117→            if entry_dt < cutoff:
   118→                to_prune.append(fid)
   119→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   120→            to_prune.append(fid)
   121→
   122→    for fid in to_prune:
   123→        superseded.pop(fid, None)
   124→        # Also clean up stale overrides
   125→        plan.get("overrides", {}).pop(fid, None)
   126→
   127→    return to_prune
   128→
   129→
   130→def reconcile_plan_after_scan(
   131→    plan: PlanModel,
   132→    state: StateModel,
   133→) -> ReconcileResult:
   134→    """Reconcile plan against current state after a scan.
   135→
   136→    Finds IDs referenced in the plan that no longer exist or are no longer
   137→    open, moves them to superseded, and prunes old superseded entries.
   138→    """
   139→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   140→    result = ReconcileResult()
   141→    now = utc_now()
   142→    now_dt = datetime.now(UTC)
   143→
   144→    # Collect all finding IDs referenced by the plan
   145→    referenced_ids: set[str] = set()
   146→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   147→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   148→    for override_id in plan.get("overrides", {}):
   149→        referenced_ids.add(override_id)
   150→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   151→        referenced_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   152→
   153→    # Exclude already-superseded IDs and synthetic IDs (managed by stale_dimensions)
   154→    already_superseded = set(plan.get("superseded", {}).keys())
   155→    referenced_ids -= already_superseded
   156→    referenced_ids = {
   157→        fid for fid in referenced_ids
   158→        if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   159→        and fid != SYNTHESIS_ID
   160→    }
   161→
   162→    # Check each referenced ID
   163→    for fid in sorted(referenced_ids):
   164→        if not _is_finding_alive(state, fid):
   165→            if _supersede_id(plan, state, fid, now):
   166→                result.superseded.append(fid)
   167→                result.changes += 1
   168→
   169→    # Reconcile epic clusters: remove dead findings, delete empty epics
   170→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   171→    epic_names_to_delete: list[str] = []
   172→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   173→        if not name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX):
   174→            continue
   175→        finding_ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   176→        alive_ids = [fid for fid in finding_ids if _is_finding_alive(state, fid)]
   177→        if alive_ids != finding_ids:
   178→            cluster["finding_ids"] = alive_ids
   179→            result.changes += 1
   180→        if not alive_ids:
   181→            epic_names_to_delete.append(name)
   182→    for name in epic_names_to_delete:
   183→        clusters.pop(name, None)
   184→        result.changes += 1
   185→
   186→    # Resurface stale temporary skips
   187→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   188→    from desloppify.engine._plan.operations import resurface_stale_skips
   189→
   190→    resurfaced = resurface_stale_skips(plan, scan_count)
   191→    if resurfaced:
   192→        result.resurfaced = resurfaced
   193→        result.changes += len(resurfaced)
   194→
   195→    # Prune old superseded entries
   196→    pruned = _prune_old_superseded(plan, now_dt)
   197→    result.pruned = pruned
   198→    result.changes += len(pruned)
   199→
   200→    # Log reconciliation if any changes were made
   201→    if result.changes > 0:
   202→        from desloppify.engine._plan.operations import append_log_entry
   203→
   204→        append_log_entry(
   205→            plan,
   206→            "reconcile",
   207→            finding_ids=result.superseded,
   208→            actor="system",
   209→            detail={
   210→                "superseded_count": len(result.superseded),
   211→                "pruned_count": len(result.pruned),
   212→                "resurfaced_count": len(result.resurfaced),
   213→            },
   214→        )
   215→
   216→    return result
   217→
   218→
   219→__all__ = [
   220→    "ReconcileResult",
   221→    "reconcile_plan_after_scan",
   222→]
   223→
```

```
1→"""Auto-clustering algorithm — groups findings into task clusters."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import os
     6→from collections import defaultdict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS, DetectorMeta
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import Cluster, PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
    11→    SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    12→    _current_unscored_ids,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    15→
    16→AUTO_PREFIX = "auto/"
    17→_MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE = 2
    18→_STALE_KEY = "subjective::stale"
    19→_STALE_NAME = "auto/stale-review"
    20→_UNSCORED_KEY = "subjective::unscored"
    21→_UNSCORED_NAME = "auto/initial-review"
    22→_MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE = 1
    23→
    24→
    25→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    26→# Grouping key computation
    27→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    28→
    29→def _extract_subtype(finding: dict) -> str | None:
    30→    """Extract the subtype/kind from a finding.
    31→
    32→    Checks detail.kind first, then falls back to parsing the finding ID
    33→    (format: ``detector::file::subtype`` or ``detector::file::symbol::subtype``).
    34→    """
    35→    detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    36→    kind = detail.get("kind")
    37→    if kind:
    38→        return kind
    39→
    40→    # Parse from finding ID — third segment is often the subtype
    41→    fid = finding.get("id", "")
    42→    parts = fid.split("::")
    43→    if len(parts) >= 3:
    44→        # For IDs like smells::file.py::silent_except, the subtype is parts[2]
    45→        # For IDs like dict_keys::file.py::key::phantom_read, subtype is last part
    46→        candidate = parts[-1]
    47→        # Skip if it looks like a file path or symbol name (contains / or .)
    48→        if "/" not in candidate and "." not in candidate:
    49→            return candidate
    50→    return None
    51→
    52→
    53→def _grouping_key(finding: dict, meta: DetectorMeta | None) -> str | None:
    54→    """Compute a deterministic grouping key for a finding.
    55→
    56→    Returns None if the finding should not be auto-clustered.
    57→    """
    58→    detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    59→
    60→    if meta is None:
    61→        # Unknown detector — group by detector name
    62→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    63→
    64→    # Review findings → group by dimension
    65→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
    66→        detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    67→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
    68→        if dimension:
    69→            return f"review::{dimension}"
    70→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    71→
    72→    # Per-file detectors (structural, responsibility_cohesion)
    73→    if meta.needs_judgment and detector in (
    74→        "structural", "responsibility_cohesion",
    75→    ):
    76→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    77→        if file:
    78→            basename = os.path.basename(file)
    79→            return f"file::{detector}::{basename}"
    80→
    81→    # Needs-judgment detectors — group by subtype when available
    82→    if meta.needs_judgment:
    83→        subtype = _extract_subtype(finding)
    84→        if subtype:
    85→            return f"typed::{detector}::{subtype}"
    86→
    87→    # Pure auto-fix (no judgment needed) → all findings by detector
    88→    if meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
    89→        return f"auto::{detector}"
    90→
    91→    # Everything else → by detector
    92→    return f"detector::{detector}"
    93→
    94→
    95→def _cluster_name_from_key(key: str) -> str:
    96→    """Convert a grouping key to a cluster name with auto/ prefix."""
    97→    # auto::unused → auto/unused
    98→    # typed::dict_keys::phantom_read → auto/dict_keys-phantom_read
    99→    # file::structural::big_file.py → auto/structural-big_file.py
   100→    # review::abstraction_fitness → auto/review-abstraction_fitness
```

```
   394→                    item
   395→                    for item in all_items
   396→                    if _item_matches_tier(item, chosen)
   397→                ]
   398→                fallback_reason = (
   399→                    f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open -> showing T{chosen} "
   400→                    "(nearest non-empty)."
   401→                )
   402→        elif not filtered:
   403→            fallback_reason = f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open."
   404→
   405→    total = len(filtered)
   406→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   407→        filtered = filtered[: resolved_options.count]
   408→
   409→    if resolved_options.explain:
   410→        for item in filtered:
   411→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   412→
   413→    available_tiers = [tier for tier, value in counts.items() if value > 0]
   414→    return {
   415→        "items": filtered,
   416→        "total": total,
   417→        "tier_counts": counts,
   418→        "requested_tier": requested_tier,
   419→        "selected_tier": selected_tier,
   420→        "fallback_reason": fallback_reason,
   421→        "available_tiers": available_tiers,
   422→        "grouped": group_queue_items(filtered, "item"),
   423→    }
   424→
   425→
   426→__all__ = [
   427→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   428→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   429→    "WorkQueueResult",
   430→    "build_work_queue",
   431→    "group_queue_items",
   432→]
   433→
```

```
1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — prepend never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *front* of the queue unconditionally (onboarding priority).
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import hashlib
    14→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    15→
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    17→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    18→
    19→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    20→SYNTHESIS_ID = "synthesis::pending"
    21→
    22→
    23→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    24→# Result dataclasses
    25→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    26→
    27→@dataclass
    28→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    29→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    30→
    31→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    32→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    33→
    34→    @property
    35→    def changes(self) -> int:
    36→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    37→
    38→
    39→@dataclass
    40→class UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    41→    """What changed during an unscored-dimension sync."""
    42→
    43→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    44→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    45→
    46→    @property
    47→    def changes(self) -> int:
    48→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    49→
    50→
    51→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    52→# ID helpers
    53→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    54→
    55→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    56→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    57→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    58→    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    59→        scorecard_subjective_entries,
    60→    )
    61→
    62→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    63→    if not dim_scores:
    64→        return set()
    65→
    66→    stale: set[str] = set()
    67→    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    68→        if not entry.get("stale"):
    69→            continue
    70→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    71→        if dim_key:
    72→            stale.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    73→    return stale
    74→
    75→
    76→def _current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    77→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    78→
    79→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` directly because
    80→    ``scorecard_subjective_entries`` filters out placeholder dimensions
    81→    (they are hidden from the scorecard display).
    82→    """
    83→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    84→
    85→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    86→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    87→        return set()
    88→
    89→    unscored: set[str] = set()
    90→    for dim_key, payload in assessments.items():
    91→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    92→            continue
    93→        if not payload.get("placeholder"):
    94→            continue
    95→        if dim_key:
    96→            unscored.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    97→    return unscored
    98→
    99→
   100→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   101→# Unscored dimension sync (front of queue, unconditional)
   102→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   103→
   104→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   105→    plan: PlanModel,
   106→    state: StateModel,
   107→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
   108→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   109→
   110→    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
   111→       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
   112→       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
   113→    2. **Inject** — unconditionally prepend currently-unscored IDs to the
   114→       *front* of ``queue_order`` so initial reviews are the first priority.
   115→    """
   116→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   117→    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
   118→    unscored_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   119→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   120→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
```

```
1→"""Compact queue table renderer for ``plan queue``."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
     9→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize, print_table
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    11→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    12→
    13→
    14→def _truncate(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    15→    if len(text) <= width:
    16→        return text
    17→    return text[: width - 1] + "\u2026"
    18→
    19→
    20→def _cluster_tier_label(item: dict) -> str:
    21→    """Compute a tier label from cluster members, e.g. 'T2' or 'T1-T3'."""
    22→    members = item.get("members", [])
    23→    if not members:
    24→        return ""
    25→    tiers = {int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) for m in members}
    26→    lo, hi = min(tiers), max(tiers)
    27→    if lo == hi:
    28→        return f"T{lo}"
    29→    return f"T{lo}-T{hi}"
    30→
    31→
    32→def _resolve_plan_context(plan: dict, cluster_filter: str | None) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None]:
    33→    plan_data: dict | None = None
    34→    if plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("overrides") or plan.get("clusters"):
    35→        plan_data = plan
    36→
    37→    effective_cluster = cluster_filter
    38→    if plan_data and not cluster_filter:
    39→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
    40→        if active_cluster:
    41→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
    42→    return plan_data, effective_cluster
    43→
    44→
    45→def _print_queue_header(
    46→    *,
    47→    items: list[dict],
    48→    tier_counts: dict,
    49→    include_skipped: bool,
    50→    plan: dict,
    51→    plan_data: dict | None,
    52→) -> None:
    53→    total = len(items)
    54→    skipped_count = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("plan_skipped"))
    55→    non_skipped = total - skipped_count
    56→    tc_parts = " ".join(f"T{t}:{tier_counts.get(t, 0)}" for t in (1, 2, 3, 4))
    57→    finding_total = sum(tier_counts.get(t, 0) for t in (1, 2, 3, 4))
    58→    if finding_total != non_skipped:
    59→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {non_skipped} entries  ({finding_total} findings: {tc_parts})", "bold"))
    60→    else:
    61→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {non_skipped} items  ({tc_parts})", "bold"))
    62→
    63→    focus = plan.get("active_cluster") if plan_data else None
    64→    if focus:
    65→        print(colorize(f"  Focus: {focus}", "cyan"))
    66→
    67→    if include_skipped or skipped_count != 0:
    68→        return
    69→    plan_skipped_count = len(plan.get("skipped", {})) if plan_data else 0
    70→    if not plan_skipped_count:
    71→        return
    72→    print(
    73→        colorize(
    74→            f"  ({plan_skipped_count} skipped item{'s' if plan_skipped_count != 1 else ''}"
    75→            " hidden — use --include-skipped)",
    76→            "dim",
    77→        )
    78→    )
    79→
    80→
    81→def _queue_display_items(items: list[dict], *, top: int) -> list[dict]:
    82→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
    83→        return items[:top]
    84→    return items
    85→
    86→
    87→def _build_rows(display_items: list[dict]) -> list[list[str]]:
    88→    rows: list[list[str]] = []
    89→    for idx, item in enumerate(display_items, 1):
    90→        pos = str(idx)
    91→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
    92→
    93→        if kind == "cluster":
    94→            tier_str = _cluster_tier_label(item)
    95→            member_count = item.get("member_count", 0)
    96→            detector = item.get("detector", "cluster")
    97→            summary = f"[{member_count} items] {item.get('summary', '')}"
    98→            cluster_name = item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id", ""))
    99→        else:
   100→            tier_val = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
```

```
     7→import logging
     8→from dataclasses import dataclass
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→from typing import Any
    11→
    12→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    13→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    14→from desloppify.core.paths_api import get_project_root
    15→
    16→
    17→def _default_config_file() -> Path:
    18→    """Resolve config path from the active runtime project root."""
    19→    return get_project_root() / ".desloppify" / "config.json"
    20→
    21→
    22→# Legacy export for call sites/tests that introspect this module constant.
    23→CONFIG_FILE = _default_config_file()
    24→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    25→MIN_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE = 0
    26→MAX_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE = 100
    27→
    28→
    29→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    30→class ConfigKey:
    31→    type: type
    32→    default: object
    33→    description: str
    34→
    35→
    36→CONFIG_SCHEMA: dict[str, ConfigKey] = {
    37→    "target_strict_score": ConfigKey(
    38→        int, 95, "North-star strict score target used to prioritize guidance"
    39→    ),
    40→    "review_max_age_days": ConfigKey(
    41→        int, 30, "Days before a file review is considered stale (0 = never)"
    42→    ),
    43→    "review_batch_max_files": ConfigKey(
    44→        int,
    45→        80,
    46→        "Max files assigned to each holistic review batch (0 = unlimited)",
    47→    ),
    48→    "holistic_max_age_days": ConfigKey(
    49→        int, 30, "Days before a holistic review is considered stale (0 = never)"
    50→    ),
    51→    "generate_scorecard": ConfigKey(
    52→        bool, True, "Generate scorecard image after each scan"
    53→    ),
    54→    "badge_path": ConfigKey(
    55→        str, "scorecard.png", "Output path for scorecard image"
    56→    ),
    57→    "exclude": ConfigKey(list, [], "Path patterns to exclude from scanning"),
    58→    "ignore": ConfigKey(list, [], "Finding patterns to suppress"),
    59→    "ignore_metadata": ConfigKey(dict, {}, "Ignore metadata {pattern: {note, added_at}}"),
    60→    "zone_overrides": ConfigKey(
    61→        dict, {}, "Manual zone overrides {rel_path: zone_name}"
    62→    ),
    63→    "review_dimensions": ConfigKey(
    64→        list,
    65→        [],
    66→        "Override default per-file review dimensions (empty = built-in defaults)",
    67→    ),
    68→    "large_files_threshold": ConfigKey(
    69→        int,
    70→        0,
    71→        "Override LOC threshold for large file detection (0 = use language default)",
    72→    ),
    73→    "props_threshold": ConfigKey(
    74→        int,
    75→        0,
    76→        "Override prop count threshold for bloated interface detection (0 = default 14)",
    77→    ),
    78→    "finding_noise_budget": ConfigKey(
    79→        int,
    80→        10,
    81→        "Max findings surfaced per detector in show/scan summaries (0 = unlimited)",
    82→    ),
    83→    "finding_noise_global_budget": ConfigKey(
    84→        int,
    85→        0,
    86→        "Global cap for surfaced findings after per-detector budget (0 = unlimited)",
    87→    ),
    88→    "needs_rescan": ConfigKey(
    89→        bool, False, "Set when config changes may have invalidated cached scores"
    90→    ),
    91→    "languages": ConfigKey(
    92→        dict, {}, "Language-specific settings {lang_name: {key: value}}"
    93→    ),
    94→}
    95→
    96→
    97→def default_config() -> dict[str, Any]:
    98→    """Return a config dict with all keys set to their defaults."""
    99→    return {k: copy.deepcopy(v.default) for k, v in CONFIG_SCHEMA.items()}
   100→
   101→
   102→def _coerce_target_strict_score(value: object) -> tuple[int, bool]:
   103→    """Coerce target strict score and report whether it is in range."""
   104→    try:
   105→        parsed = int(value)
   106→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   107→        return MIN_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE, False
   108→    valid = MIN_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE <= parsed <= MAX_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
   109→    return parsed, valid
   110→
   111→
   112→def load_config(path: Path | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
   113→    """Load config from disk, auto-migrating from state files if needed.
   114→
   115→    Fills missing keys with defaults. If no config.json exists, attempts
   116→    migration from state-*.json files.
   117→    """
   118→    p = path or _default_config_file()
   119→    if p.exists():
   120→        try:
   121→            config = json.loads(p.read_text())
   122→        except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError):
   123→            config = {}
   124→    else:
   125→        # First run — try migrating from state files
   126→        config = _migrate_from_state_files(p)
   127→
   128→    changed = False
   129→
   130→    # Fill missing keys with defaults
   131→    for key, schema in CONFIG_SCHEMA.items():
   132→        if key not in config:
   133→            config[key] = copy.deepcopy(schema.default)
   134→            changed = True
   135→        elif key == "badge_path":
   136→            try:
   137→                normalized = _validate_badge_path(str(config[key]))
   138→                if normalized != config[key]:
   139→                    config[key] = normalized
   140→                    changed = True
   141→            except ValueError:
   142→                config[key] = copy.deepcopy(schema.default)
   143→                changed = True
   144→
   145→    if changed and p.exists():
   146→        try:
   147→            save_config(config, p)
   148→        except OSError as exc:
   149→            log_best_effort_failure(logger, f"persist migrated config to {p}", exc)
   150→
   151→    return config
   152→
   153→
   154→def save_config(config: dict, path: Path | None = None) -> None:
   155→    """Save config to disk atomically."""
   156→    p = path or _default_config_file()
   157→    safe_write_text(p, json.dumps(config, indent=2) + "\n")
   158→
   159→
   160→def add_ignore_pattern(config: dict, pattern: str) -> None:
   161→    """Append a pattern to the ignore list (deduplicates)."""
   162→    ignores = config.setdefault("ignore", [])
   163→    if pattern not in ignores:
   164→        ignores.append(pattern)
   165→
   166→
   167→def add_exclude_pattern(config: dict, pattern: str) -> None:
   168→    """Append a pattern to the exclude list (deduplicates)."""
   169→    excludes = config.setdefault("exclude", [])
   170→    if pattern not in excludes:
   171→        excludes.append(pattern)
   172→
   173→
   174→def set_ignore_metadata(config: dict, pattern: str, *, note: str, added_at: str) -> None:
   175→    """Record note + timestamp for an ignore pattern."""
   176→    meta = config.setdefault("ignore_metadata", {})
   177→    if not isinstance(meta, dict):
   178→        meta = {}
   179→        config["ignore_metadata"] = meta
   180→    meta[pattern] = {"note": note, "added_at": added_at}
   181→
   182→
   183→def _validate_badge_path(raw: str) -> str:
   184→    """Require badge_path to point to a filename (root or nested path)."""
   185→    value = raw.strip()
   186→    path = Path(value)
   187→    if (
   188→        not value
   189→        or value.endswith(("/", "\\"))
   190→        or path.name in {"", ".", ".."}
   191→    ):
   192→        raise ValueError(
   193→            "Expected file path for badge_path "
   194→            f"(example: scorecard.png or assets/scorecard.png), got: {raw}"
   195→        )
   196→    return value
   197→
   198→
   199→def _set_int_config_value(config: dict, key: str, raw: str) -> None:
   200→    if raw.lower() == "never":
   201→        config[key] = 0
   202→    else:
   203→        config[key] = int(raw)
   204→    if key != "target_strict_score":
   205→        return
   206→    target_strict_score, target_valid = _coerce_target_strict_score(config[key])
   207→    if not target_valid:
   208→        raise ValueError(
   209→            f"Expected integer {MIN_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE}-{MAX_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE} "
   210→            f"for {key}, got: {raw}"
   211→        )
   212→    config[key] = target_strict_score
   213→
   214→
   215→def _set_bool_config_value(config: dict, key: str, raw: str) -> None:
   216→    normalized = raw.lower()
   217→    if normalized in ("true", "1", "yes"):
   218→        config[key] = True
   219→        return
   220→    if normalized in ("false", "0", "no"):
   221→        config[key] = False
   222→        return
   223→    raise ValueError(f"Expected true/false for {key}, got: {raw}")
   224→
   225→
   226→def _set_str_config_value(config: dict, key: str, raw: str) -> None:
   227→    if key == "badge_path":
   228→        config[key] = _validate_badge_path(raw)
   229→        return
   230→    config[key] = raw
   231→
   232→
   233→def _set_list_config_value(config: dict, key: str, raw: str) -> None:
   234→    config.setdefault(key, [])
   235→    if raw not in config[key]:
   236→        config[key].append(raw)
   237→
   238→
   239→def set_config_value(config: dict, key: str, raw: str) -> None:
   240→    """Parse and set a config value from a raw string.
   241→
   242→    Handles special cases:
   243→    - "never" → 0 for age keys
   244→    - "true"/"false" for bools
   245→    """
   246→    if key not in CONFIG_SCHEMA:
   247→        raise KeyError(f"Unknown config key: {key}")
   248→
   249→    schema = CONFIG_SCHEMA[key]
   250→
   251→    if schema.type is int:
   252→        _set_int_config_value(config, key, raw)
   253→        return
   254→    if schema.type is bool:
   255→        _set_bool_config_value(config, key, raw)
   256→        return
   257→    if schema.type is str:
   258→        _set_str_config_value(config, key, raw)
   259→        return
   260→    if schema.type is list:
   261→        _set_list_config_value(config, key, raw)
   262→        return
   263→    if schema.type is dict:
   264→        raise ValueError(f"Cannot set dict key '{key}' via CLI — use subcommands")
   265→    config[key] = raw
   266→
   267→
   268→def unset_config_value(config: dict, key: str) -> None:
   269→    """Reset a config key to its default value."""
   270→    if key not in CONFIG_SCHEMA:
   271→        raise KeyError(f"Unknown config key: {key}")
   272→    config[key] = copy.deepcopy(CONFIG_SCHEMA[key].default)
   273→
   274→
   275→def config_for_query(config: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   276→    """Return a sanitized config dict suitable for query.json."""
   277→    return {k: config.get(k, schema.default) for k, schema in CONFIG_SCHEMA.items()}
   278→
   279→
   280→def _merge_config_value(config: dict, key: str, value: object) -> None:
   281→    """Merge a config value into the target dict."""
   282→    if key not in config:
   283→        config[key] = copy.deepcopy(value)
   284→        return
   285→    if isinstance(value, list) and isinstance(config[key], list):
   286→        for item in value:
   287→            if item not in config[key]:
   288→                config[key].append(item)
   289→        return
   290→    if isinstance(value, dict) and isinstance(config[key], dict):
   291→        for dk, dv in value.items():
   292→            if dk not in config[key]:
   293→                config[key][dk] = copy.deepcopy(dv)
   294→        return
   295→
   296→
   297→def _load_state_file_payload(path: Path) -> dict | None:
   298→    try:
   299→        payload = json.loads(path.read_text())
   300→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError) as exc:
   301→        logger.debug("Skipping unreadable state file %s: %s", path, exc)
   302→        return None
   303→    if isinstance(payload, dict):
   304→        return payload
   305→    return None
   306→
   307→
   308→def _merge_legacy_state_config(config: dict, old_config: dict) -> None:
   309→    for key, value in old_config.items():
   310→        if key not in CONFIG_SCHEMA:
   311→            continue
   312→        _merge_config_value(config, key, value)
   313→
   314→
   315→def _strip_config_from_state_file(path: Path, state_data: dict) -> None:
   316→    if "config" not in state_data:
   317→        return
   318→    del state_data["config"]
   319→    try:
   320→        safe_write_text(path, json.dumps(state_data, indent=2) + "\n")
   321→    except OSError as exc:
   322→        log_best_effort_failure(
   323→            logger,
   324→            f"rewrite state file {path} after config migration",
   325→            exc,
   326→        )
   327→
   328→
   329→def _migrate_single_state_file(config: dict, path: Path) -> bool:
   330→    state_data = _load_state_file_payload(path)
   331→    if state_data is None:
   332→        return False
   333→    old_config = state_data.get("config")
   334→    if not isinstance(old_config, dict) or not old_config:
   335→        return False
   336→
   337→    _merge_legacy_state_config(config, old_config)
   338→    _strip_config_from_state_file(path, state_data)
   339→    return True
   340→
   341→
   342→def _migrate_from_state_files(config_path: Path) -> dict:
   343→    """Migrate config keys from state-*.json files into config.json.
   344→
   345→    Reads state["config"] from all state files, merges them (union for
   346→    lists, merge for dicts), writes config.json, and strips "config" from
   347→    the state files.
   348→    """
   349→    config: dict = {}
   350→    state_dir = config_path.parent
   351→    if not state_dir.exists():
   352→        return config
   353→
   354→    state_files = list(state_dir.glob("state-*.json")) + list(
   355→        state_dir.glob("state.json")
   356→    )
   357→    migrated_any = False
   358→    for sf in state_files:
   359→        migrated_any = _migrate_single_state_file(config, sf) or migrated_any
   360→
   361→    if migrated_any and config:
   362→        try:
   363→            save_config(config, config_path)
   364→        except OSError as exc:
   365→            log_best_effort_failure(
   366→                logger, f"save migrated config to {config_path}", exc
   367→            )
   368→
   369→    return config
   370→
```

```
1→"""Runtime context helpers for command handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    11→from desloppify.core.config import load_config
    12→
    13→
    14→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    15→class CommandRuntime:
    16→    """Explicit runtime dependencies shared by command handlers."""
    17→
    18→    config: dict[str, Any]
    19→    state: dict[str, Any]
    20→    state_path: Path | None
    21→
    22→
    23→def command_runtime(args) -> CommandRuntime:
    24→    """Return runtime context from explicit args.runtime or construct one."""
    25→    runtime = getattr(args, "runtime", None)
    26→    if isinstance(runtime, CommandRuntime):
    27→        return runtime
    28→
    29→    config = load_config()
    30→    state_file = state_path(args)
    31→    if isinstance(state_file, str):
    32→        state_file = Path(state_file)
    33→
    34→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
    35→
    36→    return CommandRuntime(config=config, state=state, state_path=state_file)
    37→
    38→
    39→__all__ = ["CommandRuntime", "command_runtime"]
    40→
```

```
1→"""config command: show/set/unset project configuration."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import sys
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     9→from desloppify.core.config import (
    10→    CONFIG_SCHEMA,
    11→    save_config,
    12→    set_config_value,
    13→    unset_config_value,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import print_error
    16→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    17→
    18→
    19→def cmd_config(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    20→    """Handle config subcommands: show, set, unset."""
    21→    action = getattr(args, "config_action", None)
    22→    if action == "set":
    23→        _config_set(args)
    24→    elif action == "unset":
    25→        _config_unset(args)
    26→    else:
    27→        _config_show(args)
    28→
    29→
    30→def _config_show(args):
    31→    """Print all config keys with current values and descriptions."""
    32→    config = command_runtime(args).config
    33→
    34→    print(colorize("\n  Desloppify Configuration\n", "bold"))
    35→    for key, schema in CONFIG_SCHEMA.items():
    36→        value = config.get(key, schema.default)
    37→        is_default = value == schema.default
    38→
    39→        # Format display value
    40→        if schema.type is int and key.endswith("_days") and value == 0:
    41→            display = "never (0)"
    42→        elif isinstance(value, list):
    43→            display = ", ".join(value) if value else "(empty)"
    44→        elif isinstance(value, dict):
    45→            display = f"{len(value)} entries" if value else "(empty)"
    46→        else:
    47→            display = str(value)
    48→
    49→        default_tag = colorize(" (default)", "dim") if is_default else ""
    50→        print(f"  {key:<25} {display}{default_tag}")
    51→        print(colorize(f"  {'':25} {schema.description}", "dim"))
    52→    print()
    53→
    54→
    55→def _config_set(args):
    56→    """Set a config key to a value."""
    57→    config = command_runtime(args).config
    58→    key = args.config_key
    59→    value = args.config_value
    60→
    61→    try:
    62→        set_config_value(config, key, value)
    63→    except (KeyError, ValueError) as e:
    64→        print_error(str(e))
    65→        sys.exit(1)
    66→
    67→    try:
    68→        save_config(config)
    69→    except OSError as e:
    70→        print_error(f"could not save config: {e}")
    71→        sys.exit(1)
    72→    display = config[key]
    73→    if isinstance(display, int) and key.endswith("_days") and display == 0:
    74→        display = "never (0)"
    75→    print(colorize(f"  Set {key} = {display}", "green"))
    76→
    77→
    78→def _config_unset(args):
    79→    """Reset a config key to its default."""
    80→    config = command_runtime(args).config
    81→    key = args.config_key
    82→
    83→    try:
    84→        unset_config_value(config, key)
    85→    except KeyError as e:
    86→        print_error(str(e))
    87→        sys.exit(1)
    88→
    89→    try:
    90→        save_config(config)
    91→    except OSError as e:
    92→        print_error(f"could not save config: {e}")
    93→        sys.exit(1)
    94→    default = CONFIG_SCHEMA[key].default
    95→    print(colorize(f"  Reset {key} to default ({default})", "green"))
    96→
```

```
1→"""Pure mutation operations on the plan model."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     6→    Cluster,
     7→    ExecutionLogEntry,
     8→    PlanModel,
     9→    SkipEntry,
    10→    empty_plan,
    11→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    14→
    15→
    16→_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES = 500
    17→_CAP_LOG = True
    18→
    19→
    20→def append_log_entry(
    21→    plan: PlanModel,
    22→    action: str,
    23→    *,
    24→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    25→    cluster_name: str | None = None,
    26→    actor: str = "user",
    27→    note: str | None = None,
    28→    detail: dict | None = None,
    29→) -> None:
    30→    """Append a structured entry to the plan's execution log."""
    31→    log = plan.get("execution_log", [])
    32→    entry: ExecutionLogEntry = {
    33→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
    34→        "action": action,
    35→        "finding_ids": finding_ids or [],
    36→        "cluster_name": cluster_name,
    37→        "actor": actor,
    38→        "note": note,
    39→        "detail": detail or {},
    40→    }
    41→    log.append(entry)
    42→    if _CAP_LOG and len(log) > _MAX_LOG_ENTRIES:
    43→        plan["execution_log"] = log[-_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES:]
    44→
    45→
    46→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    47→# Helpers
    48→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    49→
    50→def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str) -> None:
    51→    """Remove a finding ID from queue_order and skipped."""
    52→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    53→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
    54→    if finding_id in order:
    55→        order.remove(finding_id)
    56→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    57→
    58→
    59→def _resolve_position(
    60→    order: list[str],
    61→    position: str,
    62→    target: str | None = None,
    63→    offset: int | None = None,
    64→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    65→) -> int:
    66→    """Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.
    67→
    68→    *finding_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative
    69→    positions when they already exist in the list.
    70→    """
    71→    moving = set(finding_ids or [])
    72→
    73→    if position == "top":
    74→        return 0
    75→    if position == "bottom":
    76→        return len(order)
    77→
    78→    if position in {"before", "after"} and target:
    79→        return _resolve_relative_position(order, moving, position=position, target=target)
    80→
    81→    if position in {"up", "down"} and offset is not None:
    82→        return _resolve_offset_position(
    83→            order,
    84→            moving,
    85→            position=position,
    86→            offset=offset,
    87→            finding_ids=finding_ids,
    88→        )
    89→
    90→    return len(order)
    91→
    92→
    93→def _resolve_relative_position(
    94→    order: list[str],
    95→    moving: set[str],
    96→    *,
    97→    position: str,
    98→    target: str,
    99→) -> int:
   100→    for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
   101→        if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
   102→            return i if position == "before" else i + 1
   103→    return 0 if position == "before" else len(order)
   104→
   105→
   106→def _find_index(items: list[str], target: str) -> int | None:
   107→    for i, item_id in enumerate(items):
   108→        if item_id == target:
   109→            return i
   110→    return None
   111→
   112→
   113→def _resolve_offset_position(
   114→    order: list[str],
   115→    moving: set[str],
   116→    *,
   117→    position: str,
   118→    offset: int,
   119→    finding_ids: list[str] | None,
   120→) -> int:
   121→    if not finding_ids:
   122→        return 0 if position == "up" else len(order)
   123→    first_id = finding_ids[0]
   124→    clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
   125→    current_idx = _find_index(clean_order, first_id)
   126→    if current_idx is None:
   127→        if position == "up":
   128→            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)
   129→        return len(clean_order)
   130→    if position == "up":
   131→        return max(0, current_idx - offset)
   132→    return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)
   133→
   134→
   135→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   136→# Move items
   137→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   138→
   139→def move_items(
   140→    plan: PlanModel,
   141→    finding_ids: list[str],
   142→    position: str,
   143→    target: str | None = None,
   144→    offset: int | None = None,
   145→) -> int:
   146→    """Move finding IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved."""
   147→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   148→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   149→
   150→    # Remove from skipped if present
   151→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
   152→    for fid in finding_ids:
   153→        skipped.pop(fid, None)
   154→
   155→    # Remove from current position in order
   156→    for fid in finding_ids:
   157→        if fid in order:
   158→            order.remove(fid)
   159→
   160→    # Resolve insertion point
   161→    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, finding_ids)
   162→
   163→    # Insert in original order
   164→    for i, fid in enumerate(finding_ids):
   165→        order.insert(idx + i, fid)
   166→
   167→    return len(finding_ids)
   168→
   169→
   170→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   171→# Skip / unskip
   172→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   173→
   174→def skip_items(
   175→    plan: PlanModel,
   176→    finding_ids: list[str],
   177→    *,
   178→    kind: str = "temporary",
   179→    reason: str | None = None,
   180→    note: str | None = None,
   181→    attestation: str | None = None,
   182→    review_after: int | None = None,
   183→    scan_count: int = 0,
   184→) -> int:
   185→    """Move finding IDs to the skipped dict. Returns count skipped."""
   186→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   187→    now = utc_now()
   188→    count = 0
   189→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   190→    for fid in finding_ids:
   191→        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)
   192→        skipped[fid] = {
   193→            "finding_id": fid,
   194→            "kind": kind,
   195→            "reason": reason,
   196→            "note": note,
   197→            "attestation": attestation,
   198→            "created_at": now,
   199→            "review_after": review_after,
   200→            "skipped_at_scan": scan_count,
   201→        }
   202→        count += 1
   203→    return count
   204→
   205→
   206→def unskip_items(
   207→    plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]
   208→) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
   209→    """Bring finding IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.
   210→
   211→    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)``
   212→    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive
   213→    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller.
   214→    """
   215→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   216→    count = 0
   217→    need_reopen: list[str] = []
   218→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   219→    for fid in finding_ids:
   220→        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)
   221→        if entry is not None:
   222→            if entry.get("kind") in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   223→                need_reopen.append(fid)
   224→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   225→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   226→            count += 1
   227→    return count, need_reopen
   228→
   229→
   230→def resurface_stale_skips(
   231→    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int
   232→) -> list[str]:
   233→    """Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.
   234→
   235→    Returns list of resurfaced finding IDs.
   236→    """
   237→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   238→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   239→    resurfaced: list[str] = []
   240→    for fid in list(skipped):
   241→        entry = skipped[fid]
   242→        if entry.get("kind") != "temporary":
   243→            continue
   244→        review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   245→        if review_after is None:
   246→            continue
   247→        skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   248→        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:
   249→            skipped.pop(fid)
   250→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   251→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   252→            resurfaced.append(fid)
   253→    return resurfaced
   254→
   255→
   256→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   257→# Describe / annotate
   258→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   259→
   260→def describe_finding(
   261→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, description: str | None
   262→) -> None:
   263→    """Set or clear an augmented description on a finding."""
   264→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   265→    now = utc_now()
   266→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   267→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   268→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   269→    overrides[finding_id]["description"] = description
   270→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   271→
   272→
   273→def annotate_finding(
   274→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, note: str | None
   275→) -> None:
   276→    """Set or clear a note on a finding."""
   277→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   278→    now = utc_now()
   279→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   280→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   281→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   282→    overrides[finding_id]["note"] = note
   283→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   284→
   285→
   286→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   287→# Clusters
   288→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   289→
   290→def create_cluster(
   291→    plan: PlanModel,
   292→    name: str,
   293→    description: str | None = None,
   294→    action: str | None = None,
   295→) -> Cluster:
   296→    """Create a named cluster. Raises ValueError if it already exists."""
   297→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   298→    if name.startswith("auto/"):
   299→        raise ValueError(
   300→            f"Cluster names starting with 'auto/' are reserved for auto-clusters: {name!r}"
   301→        )
   302→    if name.startswith("epic/"):
   303→        raise ValueError(
   304→            f"Cluster names starting with 'epic/' are reserved for synthesis epics: {name!r}"
   305→        )
   306→    if name in plan["clusters"]:
   307→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} already exists")
   308→    now = utc_now()
   309→    cluster: Cluster = {
   310→        "name": name,
   311→        "description": description,
   312→        "finding_ids": [],
   313→        "created_at": now,
   314→        "updated_at": now,
   315→        "auto": False,
   316→        "cluster_key": "",
   317→        "action": action,
   318→        "user_modified": False,
   319→    }
   320→    plan["clusters"][name] = cluster
   321→    return cluster
   322→
   323→
   324→def add_to_cluster(
   325→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   326→) -> int:
   327→    """Add finding IDs to a cluster. Returns count added."""
   328→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   329→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   330→    if cluster is None:
   331→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   332→
   333→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   334→    count = 0
   335→    now = utc_now()
   336→    for fid in finding_ids:
   337→        if fid not in member_ids:
   338→            member_ids.append(fid)
   339→            count += 1
   340→        # Update override to track cluster membership
   341→        overrides = plan["overrides"]
   342→        if fid not in overrides:
   343→            overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   344→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = cluster_name
   345→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   346→
   347→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   348→    return count
   349→
   350→
   351→def remove_from_cluster(
   352→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   353→) -> int:
   354→    """Remove finding IDs from a cluster. Returns count removed."""
   355→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   356→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   357→    if cluster is None:
   358→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   359→
   360→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   361→    now = utc_now()
   362→    count = 0
   363→    for fid in finding_ids:
   364→        if fid in member_ids:
   365→            member_ids.remove(fid)
   366→            count += 1
   367→        # Clear cluster from override
   368→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   369→        if override and override.get("cluster") == cluster_name:
   370→            override["cluster"] = None
   371→            override["updated_at"] = now
   372→
   373→    # Mark auto-clusters as user_modified when items are manually removed
   374→    if count > 0 and cluster.get("auto"):
   375→        cluster["user_modified"] = True
   376→
   377→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   378→    return count
   379→
   380→
   381→def delete_cluster(plan: PlanModel, name: str) -> list[str]:
   382→    """Delete a cluster and clear cluster refs from overrides. Returns orphaned IDs."""
   383→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   384→    cluster = plan["clusters"].pop(name, None)
   385→    if cluster is None:
   386→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} does not exist")
   387→
   388→    orphaned = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   389→    now = utc_now()
   390→    for fid in orphaned:
   391→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   392→        if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   393→            override["cluster"] = None
   394→            override["updated_at"] = now
   395→
   396→    if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   397→        plan["active_cluster"] = None
   398→
   399→    return orphaned
   400→
   401→
   402→def move_cluster(
   403→    plan: PlanModel,
   404→    cluster_name: str,
   405→    position: str,
   406→    target: str | None = None,
   407→    offset: int | None = None,
   408→) -> int:
   409→    """Move all cluster members as a contiguous block. Returns count moved."""
   410→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   411→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   412→    if cluster is None:
   413→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   414→
   415→    member_ids = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   416→    if not member_ids:
   417→        return 0
   418→
   419→    return move_items(plan, member_ids, position, target, offset)
   420→
   421→
   422→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   423→# Focus
   424→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   425→
   426→def set_focus(plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str) -> None:
   427→    """Set the active cluster focus."""
   428→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   429→    if cluster_name not in plan["clusters"]:
   430→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   431→    plan["active_cluster"] = cluster_name
   432→
   433→
   434→def clear_focus(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   435→    """Clear the active cluster focus."""
   436→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   437→    plan["active_cluster"] = None
   438→
   439→
   440→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   441→# Reset
   442→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   443→
   444→def reset_plan(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   445→    """Reset plan to empty state, preserving version and created timestamp."""
   446→    created = plan.get("created", utc_now())
   447→    plan.clear()
   448→    for k, v in empty_plan().items():
   449→        plan[k] = v
   450→    plan["created"] = created
   451→
   452→
   453→def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]) -> int:
   454→    """Remove finding IDs from the plan entirely.
   455→
   456→    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.
   457→    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).
   458→    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.
   459→    """
   460→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   461→    found = 0
   462→
   463→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   464→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   465→    for fid in finding_ids:
   466→        was_present = False
   467→        if fid in order:
   468→            order.remove(fid)
   469→            was_present = True
   470→        if fid in skipped:
   471→            skipped.pop(fid)
   472→            was_present = True
   473→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   474→            ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   475→            if fid in ids:
   476→                ids.remove(fid)
   477→                was_present = True
   478→        # Clear stale cluster reference from override (preserve notes/descriptions)
   479→        override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
   480→        if override and override.get("cluster"):
   481→            override["cluster"] = None
   482→            override["updated_at"] = utc_now()
   483→        if was_present:
   484→            found += 1
   485→
   486→    return found
   487→
   488→
   489→__all__ = [
   490→    "add_to_cluster",
   491→    "annotate_finding",
   492→    "append_log_entry",
   493→    "clear_focus",
   494→    "create_cluster",
   495→    "delete_cluster",
   496→    "describe_finding",
   497→    "move_cluster",
   498→    "move_items",
   499→    "purge_ids",
   500→    "remove_from_cluster",
   501→    "reset_plan",
   502→    "resurface_stale_skips",
   503→    "set_focus",
   504→    "skip_items",
   505→    "unskip_items",
   506→]
   507→
```

```
    37→def _scorecard_subjective(

    43→
    44→
    45→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    46→    state: dict,
    47→    dim_scores: dict,
    48→    *,
    49→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    50→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    51→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    52→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    53→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    54→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    55→            continue
    56→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    57→        if strict_val < threshold:
    58→            low.append(
    59→                (
    60→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    61→                    strict_val,
    62→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    63→                )
    64→            )
    65→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    66→    return low
    67→

    78→    if stale_warning:
```

<summary>
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user asked to implement a multi-part plan to fix bugs and add guardrails to desloppify's plan execution engine. The core motivation: Claude Code executed a desloppify-generated plan over multiple sessions, producing 329 changed files of mostly-sloppy code — with no way to detect the plan engine had a bug (cluster finding_ids desync) or reconstruct what happened (no execution log). The specific changes requested were:
   - **Fix cluster finding_ids desync bug** — when findings are superseded, override `cluster` references aren't cleared, causing ghost clusters
   - **Add structured execution log** — append timestamped entries to plan.json for every mutating operation
   - **Add cluster completion guard** — prevent bulk-completing small clusters without resolving items individually
   - **Add minimum note quality** — 50-character minimum for attestation notes
   
   After the initial implementation, the user made several follow-up requests:
   - Make the log cap configurable via the settings/config system (not just a Python flag)
   - Change default log cap from 500 to 10,000
   - Instrument ALL remaining mutating plan operations (not just the initial 4)
   - Add queue order enforcement — items must be resolved in plan order (position 0 first)
   - Block marking empty clusters as done — must add items first
   - The user's final question was about whether the planning phase lifecycle is now fully logged

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **PlanModel** — TypedDict in schema.py representing the living plan (queue_order, clusters, overrides, skipped, superseded, execution_log)
   - **Plan reconciliation** — post-scan process that supersedes disappeared findings, resurfaces stale skips, prunes old entries
   - **Auto-clustering** — groups findings by detector/subtype into named clusters with actions
   - **Work queue** — ranked list of items built from state findings + plan ordering, with cluster collapsing
   - **Override cluster refs** — each finding override tracks which cluster it belongs to; these can become orphaned (the bug)
   - **CONFIG_SCHEMA** — dict of ConfigKey(type, default, description) in config.py; auto-populates on load, settable via CLI
   - **Attestation system** — required keywords ("I have actually", "not gaming") to prevent score gaming
   - **Queue position enforcement** — items must be at queue position 0 (or in front cluster) to be resolved
   - **ExecutionLogEntry** — new TypedDict tracking timestamp, action, finding_ids, cluster_name, actor, note, detail

3. Files and Code Sections:

     - Added ExecutionLogEntry TypedDict
     - Added `execution_log: list[ExecutionLogEntry]` to PlanModel
     - Added `"execution_log": []` to empty_plan()
     - Added list type check to _ensure_container_types()
     - Added ExecutionLogEntry to __all__

     ```python
         timestamp: Required[str]
         action: Required[str]  # "done", "skip", "unskip", "resolve", "reconcile", "cluster_done", "focus", "reset"
         finding_ids: list[str]
         cluster_name: str | None
     ```

     ```python
     # Clear stale cluster reference from override

     if override and override.get("cluster"):
         override["cluster"] = None
     ```

     ```python
     if result.changes > 0:
         from desloppify.engine._plan.operations import append_log_entry
         append_log_entry(
             plan, "reconcile", finding_ids=result.superseded, actor="system",
             detail={"superseded_count": len(result.superseded), "pruned_count": len(result.pruned), "resurfaced_count": len(result.resurfaced)},
         )
     ```

   - **`desloppify/engine/_plan/operations.py`** — Bug fix + execution log helper
     - In `purge_ids()`: clears stale override cluster ref after removing from cluster finding_ids
     - Added `_DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES = 10000`
     - Added `_get_log_cap()` that reads from config (`execution_log_max_entries`), returns 0 for unlimited
     - Added `append_log_entry()` function with config-driven cap
     ```python
     def _get_log_cap() -> int:
         try:
             from desloppify.core.config import load_config
             config = load_config()
             value = config.get("execution_log_max_entries", _DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES)
             return max(0, int(value))
         except (ImportError, OSError, ValueError, TypeError):
             return _DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES

     def append_log_entry(plan, action, *, finding_ids=None, cluster_name=None, actor="user", note=None, detail=None) -> None:
         log = plan.get("execution_log", [])
         entry: ExecutionLogEntry = {
             "timestamp": utc_now(), "action": action, "finding_ids": finding_ids or [],
             "cluster_name": cluster_name, "actor": actor, "note": note, "detail": detail or {},
         }
         log.append(entry)
         cap = _get_log_cap()
         if cap > 0 and len(log) > cap:
             plan["execution_log"] = log[-cap:]
     ```

   - **`desloppify/engine/_plan/auto_cluster.py`** — Ghost ref repair
     - Added `_repair_ghost_cluster_refs()` function before `auto_cluster_findings()`
     - Called at end of `auto_cluster_findings()` before return
     ```python
     def _repair_ghost_cluster_refs(plan: PlanModel, now: str) -> int:
         clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
         overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
         repaired = 0
         for override in overrides.values():
             cluster_name = override.get("cluster")
             if cluster_name and cluster_name not in clusters:
                 override["cluster"] = None
                 override["updated_at"] = now
                 repaired += 1
         return repaired
     ```

   - **`desloppify/engine/plan.py`** — Facade updates

     - Added both to __all__

     ```python
     def validate_note_length(note: str | None) -> bool:

     def show_note_length_requirement(note: str | None) -> None:
         current = len((note or "").strip())

     ```

     ```python
     def _check_queue_order_guard(state, patterns, status) -> bool:
         if status != "fixed": return False
         # ... loads plan, builds work queue, gets front item
         # Collects front_ids (item + cluster members if collapsed)
         # Expands patterns (cluster names → member IDs)
         out_of_order = resolved_ids - front_ids
         if not out_of_order: return False
         # Prints: queue order violation, current next item, plan move/skip/next commands
         return True
     ```

   - **`desloppify/app/commands/plan/override_handlers.py`** — Cluster guard + note validation + log instrumentation for describe/note/skip/unskip/reopen/focus/done
     - Added imports: `append_log_entry`, `validate_note_length`, `show_note_length_requirement`
     - Added `_CLUSTER_INDIVIDUAL_THRESHOLD = 10`
     - Added `_check_cluster_guard()` — blocks empty clusters AND clusters with 1-10 items
     - Added `_print_cluster_guard()` — prints items with detector/summary
     - `cmd_plan_done()`: note validation → attestation validation → cluster guard → log "done" → delegate to cmd_resolve
     - `cmd_plan_skip()`: log "skip" entry with kind/reason before save
     - `cmd_plan_unskip()`: log "unskip" entry with need_reopen detail
     - `cmd_plan_reopen()`: log "reopen" entry with reopened IDs
     - `cmd_plan_focus()`: log "focus" entry for both set and clear paths
     - `cmd_plan_describe()`: log "describe" entry with text
     - `cmd_plan_note()`: log "note" entry with text
     ```python
     def _check_cluster_guard(patterns, plan, state) -> bool:
         clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
         for pattern in patterns:
             if pattern in clusters:
                 ids = clusters[pattern].get("finding_ids", [])
                 if len(ids) == 0:
                     print(colorize(f"\n  Cluster '{pattern}' is empty — add items before marking it done.\n", "yellow"))
                     print(colorize(f"  Use: desloppify plan cluster add {pattern} <finding-id>", "dim"))
                     return True
                 if len(ids) <= _CLUSTER_INDIVIDUAL_THRESHOLD:
                     _print_cluster_guard(pattern, ids, state)
                     return True
         return False
     ```

```
1→"""plan command: generate prioritized markdown plan from state.
     2→
     3→This module preserves the original import path. The full plan command
     4→(with subcommands) lives in ``desloppify.app.commands.plan.cmd``.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import argparse
    10→
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.rendering import print_agent_plan
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    14→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import warn_best_effort
    15→from desloppify.engine import planning as planning_mod
    16→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    17→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    18→from desloppify.core.tooling import check_config_staleness
    19→
    20→
    21→def cmd_plan_output(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    22→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state."""
    23→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    24→    state = runtime.state
    25→
    26→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    27→        return
    28→
    29→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(runtime.config)
    30→    if config_warning:
    31→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    32→
    33→    plan_md = planning_mod.generate_plan_md(state)
    34→    next_command = "desloppify next --count 20"
    35→
    36→    output = getattr(args, "output", None)
    37→    if output:
    38→        try:
    39→            safe_write_text(output, plan_md)
    40→            print(colorize(f"Plan written to {output}", "green"))
    41→            print_agent_plan(
    42→                ["Inspect and execute the generated plan."],
    43→                next_command=next_command,
    44→            )
    45→        except OSError as e:
    46→            warn_best_effort(f"Could not write plan to {output}: {e}")
    47→    else:
    48→        print(plan_md)
    49→        print()
    50→        print_agent_plan(
    51→            ["Start from the top-ranked action in this plan."],
    52→            next_command=next_command,
    53→        )
    54→
    55→
    56→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_output"]
    57→
```

```
    22→

    29→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    30→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    31→    plan = load_plan()
    32→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    33→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    34→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    35→    temp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "temporary")
    36→    perm_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "permanent")
    37→    fp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "false_positive")
    38→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    39→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    40→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    41→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    42→
    43→    # Count meaningful annotations (descriptions and notes)
    44→    described = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("description"))
    45→    noted = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("note"))
    46→
    47→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
    48→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
    49→    print(f"  Queue:            {ordered} items prioritized")
    50→    if total_skipped:
    51→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
    52→    else:
    53→        print(f"  Skipped:          0")
    54→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
    55→    if clusters:
    56→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
    57→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    58→            member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    59→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
    60→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    61→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
    62→    if described or noted:
    63→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
    64→    if active:
    65→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
    66→    if superseded:
    67→        print(f"  Disappeared:      {superseded} (resolved or removed since last scan)")
    68→
    69→
    70→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    71→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
    72→    plan = load_plan()
    73→    queue_len = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    74→    cluster_count = len(plan.get("clusters", {}))
    75→    reset_plan(plan)
    76→    append_log_entry(
    77→        plan, "reset", actor="user",
    78→        detail={"previous_queue_size": queue_len, "previous_cluster_count": cluster_count},
    79→    )
    80→    save_plan(plan)
    81→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
    82→
    83→
    84→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    85→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
    86→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
    87→
    88→    if plan_action is None:
    89→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
    90→        return
    91→
    92→    if plan_action == "show":
    93→        _cmd_plan_show(args)
    94→        return
    95→
    96→    if plan_action == "queue":
    97→        cmd_plan_queue(args)
    98→        return
    99→
   100→    if plan_action == "reset":
   101→        _cmd_plan_reset(args)
   102→        return
   103→
   104→    if plan_action == "move":
   105→        cmd_plan_move(args)
   106→        return
   107→
   108→    if plan_action in (
   109→        "describe", "note", "focus",
   110→        "skip", "unskip", "reopen", "done",
   111→    ):
   112→        dispatch = {
   113→            "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   114→            "done": cmd_plan_done,
   115→            "note": cmd_plan_note,
   116→            "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   117→            "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   118→            "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   119→            "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   120→        }
   121→        dispatch[plan_action](args)
   122→        return
   123→
   124→    if plan_action == "cluster":
   125→        cmd_cluster_dispatch(args)
   126→        return
   127→
   128→    if plan_action == "synthesize":
   129→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.synthesize_handlers import cmd_plan_synthesize
   130→
   131→        cmd_plan_synthesize(args)
   132→        return
   133→
   134→    print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   135→
   136→
   137→# Backwards-compatible alias
   138→cmd_plan_output = cmd_plan
   139→
   140→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]
   141→
```
