1→"""Finding generation pipeline (phase execution and normalization)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import sys
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import PROJECT_ROOT
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import is_subjective_phase
    11→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import ZONE_POLICIES, FileZoneMap
    12→from desloppify.core.file_paths import rel
    13→from desloppify.languages import auto_detect_lang, available_langs, get_lang
    14→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import DetectorPhase, LangConfig
    15→from desloppify.languages._framework.runtime import LangRun, make_lang_run
    16→from desloppify.state import Finding
    17→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    18→
    19→
    20→@dataclass
    21→class PlanScanOptions:
    22→    """Config object for scan execution behavior."""
    23→
    24→    include_slow: bool = True
    25→    zone_overrides: dict[str, str] | None = None
    26→    profile: str = "full"
    27→
    28→
    29→def _stderr(msg: str) -> None:
    30→    print(colorize(msg, "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
    31→
    32→
    33→def _resolve_lang(
    34→    lang: LangConfig | LangRun | None, project_root: Path
    35→) -> LangConfig | LangRun:
    36→    if lang is not None:
    37→        return lang
    38→
    39→    detected = auto_detect_lang(project_root)
    40→    if detected is None:
    41→        langs = available_langs()
    42→        if not langs:
    43→            raise ValueError("No language plugins available")
    44→        detected = langs[0]
    45→    return get_lang(detected)
    46→
    47→
    48→def _build_zone_map(path: Path, lang: LangRun, zone_overrides: dict[str, str] | None) -> None:
    49→    if not (lang.zone_rules and lang.file_finder):
    50→        return
    51→
    52→    files = lang.file_finder(path)
    53→    lang.zone_map = FileZoneMap(
    54→        files, lang.zone_rules, rel_fn=rel, overrides=zone_overrides
    55→    )
    56→    counts = lang.zone_map.counts()
    57→    zone_str = ", ".join(
    58→        f"{zone}: {count}" for zone, count in sorted(counts.items()) if count > 0
    59→    )
    60→    _stderr(f"  Zones: {zone_str}")
    61→
    62→    from desloppify.languages._framework.generic import capability_report
    63→
    64→    report = capability_report(lang)
    65→    if report is not None:
    66→        present, missing = report
    67→        if present:
    68→            _stderr(f"  Capabilities: {', '.join(present)}")
    69→        if missing:
    70→            _stderr(f"  Not available: {', '.join(missing)}")
    71→
    72→
    73→def _select_phases(lang: LangRun, *, include_slow: bool, profile: str) -> list[DetectorPhase]:
    74→    active_profile = profile if profile in {"objective", "full", "ci"} else "full"
    75→    phases = lang.phases
    76→    if not include_slow or active_profile == "ci":
    77→        phases = [phase for phase in phases if not phase.slow]
    78→    if active_profile in {"objective", "ci"}:
    79→        phases = [phase for phase in phases if not is_subjective_phase(phase)]
    80→    return phases
    81→
    82→
    83→def _run_phases(path: Path, lang: LangRun, phases: list[DetectorPhase]) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
    84→    findings: list[Finding] = []
    85→    all_potentials: dict[str, int] = {}
    86→
    87→    total = len(phases)
    88→    for idx, phase in enumerate(phases, start=1):
    89→        _stderr(f"  [{idx}/{total}] {phase.label}...")
    90→        phase_findings, phase_potentials = phase.run(path, lang)
    91→        all_potentials.update(phase_potentials)
    92→        findings.extend(phase_findings)
    93→
    94→    return findings, all_potentials
    95→
    96→
    97→def _stamp_finding_context(findings: list[Finding], lang: LangRun) -> None:
    98→    if not findings:
    99→        return
   100→
   101→    zone_policies = None
   102→    if lang.zone_map is not None:
   103→        zone_policies = ZONE_POLICIES
   104→
   105→    for finding in findings:
   106→        finding["lang"] = lang.name
   107→        if lang.zone_map is None:
   108→            continue
   109→
   110→        zone = lang.zone_map.get(finding.get("file", ""))
   111→        finding["zone"] = zone.value
   112→        policy = zone_policies.get(zone) if zone_policies else None
   113→        if policy and finding.get("detector") in policy.downgrade_detectors:
   114→            finding["confidence"] = "low"
   115→
   116→
   117→def _generate_findings_from_lang(
   118→    path: Path,
   119→    lang: LangRun,
   120→    *,
   121→    include_slow: bool = True,
   122→    zone_overrides: dict[str, str] | None = None,
   123→    profile: str = "full",
   124→) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   125→    """Run detector phases from a LangRun."""
   126→    _build_zone_map(path, lang, zone_overrides)
   127→    phases = _select_phases(lang, include_slow=include_slow, profile=profile)
   128→    findings, all_potentials = _run_phases(path, lang, phases)
   129→    _stamp_finding_context(findings, lang)
   130→    _stderr(f"\n  Total: {len(findings)} findings")
   131→    return findings, all_potentials
   132→
   133→
   134→def generate_findings(
   135→    path: Path,
   136→    lang: LangConfig | LangRun | None = None,
   137→    *,
   138→    options: PlanScanOptions | None = None,
   139→) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   140→    """Run all detectors and convert results to normalized findings."""
   141→    resolved_options = options or PlanScanOptions()
   142→
   143→    resolved_lang = _resolve_lang(lang, PROJECT_ROOT)
   144→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(resolved_lang)
   145→    return _generate_findings_from_lang(
   146→        path,
   147→        runtime_lang,
   148→        include_slow=resolved_options.include_slow,
   149→        zone_overrides=resolved_options.zone_overrides,
   150→        profile=resolved_options.profile,
   151→    )
   152→
```

   247→def _tier_summary_lines(stats: dict) -> list[str]:
   248→    lines: list[str] = []
   249→    by_tier = stats.get("by_tier", {})
   250→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:

   252→        open_count = tier_stats.get("open", 0)

   254→        addressed = total - open_count
   255→        pct = round(addressed / total * 100) if total else 100
   256→        label = TIER_LABELS.get(tier_num, f"Tier {tier_num}")
   257→        lines.append(
   258→            f"- **Tier {tier_num}** ({label}): {open_count} open / {total} total ({pct}% addressed)"
   259→        )

   264→def _addressed_section(findings: dict) -> list[str]:

   272→    by_status: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
   273→    for finding in addressed:
   274→        by_status[finding["status"]] += 1
   275→    for status, count in sorted(by_status.items()):
   276→        lines.append(f"- **{status}**: {count}")
   277→
   278→    wontfix = [
   279→        finding
   280→        for finding in addressed
   281→        if finding["status"] == "wontfix" and finding.get("note")
   282→    ]
   283→    if wontfix:
   284→        lines.extend(["", "### Wontfix (with explanations)", ""])
   285→        for finding in wontfix[:30]:
   286→            lines.append(f"- `{finding['id']}` — {finding['note']}")
   287→
   288→    lines.append("")
   289→    return lines
   290→
   291→
   292→def _plan_user_ordered_section(
   293→    items: list[dict],
   294→    plan: dict,
   295→) -> list[str]:
   296→    """Render the user-ordered queue section, grouped by cluster."""
   297→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   298→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   299→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
   300→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
   301→
   302→    ordered_ids = set(queue_order) - skipped_ids
   303→    if not ordered_ids:
   304→        return []
   305→
   306→    by_id = {item.get("id"): item for item in items}
   307→
   308→    lines: list[str] = [
   309→        "---",
   310→        f"## User-Ordered Queue ({len(ordered_ids)} items)",
   311→        "",
   312→    ]
   313→
   314→    # Group by cluster: clustered items first, then unclustered
   315→    emitted: set[str] = set()
   316→    for cluster_name, cluster in clusters.items():
   317→        member_ids = [
   318→            fid for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   319→            if fid in ordered_ids and fid in by_id
   320→        ]
   321→        if not member_ids:
   322→            continue
   323→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   324→        lines.append(f"### Cluster: {cluster_name}")
   325→        if desc:
   326→            lines.append(f"> {desc}")
   327→        lines.append("")
   328→        for fid in member_ids:
   329→            item = by_id.get(fid)
   330→            if item:
   331→                lines.extend(_render_plan_item(item, overrides.get(fid, {})))
   332→                emitted.add(fid)
   333→        lines.append("")
   334→
   335→    # Unclustered ordered items
   336→    unclustered = [
   337→        fid for fid in queue_order
   338→        if fid in ordered_ids and fid not in emitted and fid in by_id
   339→    ]
   340→    if unclustered:
   341→        if any(c.get("finding_ids") for c in clusters.values()):
   342→            lines.append("### (unclustered ordered items)")
   343→            lines.append("")
   344→        for fid in unclustered:
   345→            item = by_id.get(fid)
   346→            if item:
   347→                lines.extend(_render_plan_item(item, overrides.get(fid, {})))
   348→        lines.append("")
   349→
   350→    return lines
   351→
   352→
   353→def _plan_skipped_section(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   354→    """Render the skipped items section, grouped by kind."""
   355→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   356→    if not skipped:
   357→        return []
   358→
   359→    by_id = {item.get("id"): item for item in items}
   360→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   361→
   362→    # Group by kind
   363→    by_kind: dict[str, list[str]] = {"temporary": [], "permanent": [], "false_positive": []}
   364→    for fid, entry in skipped.items():
   365→        kind = entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   366→        by_kind.setdefault(kind, []).append(fid)
   367→
   368→    kind_labels = {
   369→        "temporary": "Skipped Temporarily",
   370→        "permanent": "Wontfix (permanent)",
   371→        "false_positive": "False Positives",
   372→    }
   373→
   374→    lines: list[str] = [
   375→        "---",
   376→        f"## Skipped ({len(skipped)} items)",
   377→        "",
   378→    ]
   379→
   380→    for kind in ("temporary", "permanent", "false_positive"):
   381→        ids = by_kind.get(kind, [])
   382→        if not ids:
   383→            continue
   384→        lines.append(f"### {kind_labels[kind]} ({len(ids)})")
   385→        lines.append("")
   386→        for fid in ids:
   387→            entry = skipped.get(fid, {})
   388→            item = by_id.get(fid)
   389→            if item:
   390→                lines.extend(_render_plan_item(item, overrides.get(fid, {})))
   391→            else:
   392→                lines.append(f"- ~~{fid}~~ (not in current queue)")
   393→            reason = entry.get("reason")
   394→            if reason:
   395→                lines.append(f"      Reason: {reason}")
   396→            note = entry.get("note")
   397→            if note and not overrides.get(fid, {}).get("note"):
   398→                lines.append(f"      Note: {note}")
   399→            review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   400→            if review_after:
   401→                skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   402→                lines.append(f"      Review after: scan {skipped_at + review_after}")
   403→        lines.append("")
   404→
   405→    return lines
   406→
   407→
   408→def _plan_superseded_section(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   409→    """Render the superseded items section."""
   410→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   411→    if not superseded:
   412→        return []
   413→
   414→    lines: list[str] = [
   415→        "---",
   416→        f"## Superseded ({len(superseded)} items — may need remap)",
   417→        "",
   418→    ]
   419→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   420→        summary = entry.get("original_summary", "")
   421→        summary_str = f" — {summary}" if summary else ""
   422→        lines.append(f"- ~~{fid}~~{summary_str}")
   423→        candidates = entry.get("candidates", [])
   424→        if candidates:
   425→            lines.append(f"  Candidates: {', '.join(candidates[:3])}")
   426→        note = entry.get("note")
   427→        if note:
   428→            lines.append(f"  Note: {note}")
   429→    lines.append("")
   430→    return lines
   431→
   432→
   433→def _render_plan_item(item: dict, override: dict) -> list[str]:
   434→    """Render a single plan item as markdown lines."""
   435→    tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   436→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   437→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
   438→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
   439→
   440→    lines = [f"- [ ] [T{tier}/{confidence}] {summary}"]
   441→    desc = override.get("description")
   442→    if desc:
   443→        lines.append(f"      → {desc}")
   444→    lines.append(f"      `{item_id}`")
   445→    note = override.get("note")
   446→    if note:
   447→        lines.append(f"      Note: {note}")
   448→    return lines
   449→
```

    79→def synthesis_phase_banner(plan: PlanModel) -> str:
    80→    """Return a banner string when ``synthesis::pending`` is in the queue."""
    81→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    82→    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
    83→        return ""
    84→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    85→    stages = meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})
    86→    completed = [s for s in ("observe", "reflect", "organize") if s in stages]
    87→    if completed:
    88→        return (
    89→            f"Synthesis in progress ({len(completed)}/4 stages complete). "
    90→            "Run: desloppify plan synthesize"
    91→        )
    92→    return (
    93→        "Synthesis needed — review findings require analysis before fixing. "
    94→        "Run: desloppify plan synthesize"
    95→    )
    96→

1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — handle finding churn."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import EPIC_PREFIX, PlanModel, SupersededEntry, ensure_plan_defaults
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX, SYNTHESIS_ID
    10→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    11→
    12→
    13→SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS = 90
    14→
    15→
    16→@dataclass
    17→class ReconcileResult:
    18→    """Summary of changes made during reconciliation."""
    19→
    20→    superseded: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    21→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    22→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    23→    changes: int = 0
    24→
    25→
    26→def _find_candidates(
    27→    state: StateModel, detector: str, file: str
    28→) -> list[str]:
    29→    """Find open findings that could be remaps for a disappeared finding."""
    30→    candidates: list[str] = []
    31→    for fid, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items():
    32→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    33→            continue
    34→        if finding.get("detector") == detector and finding.get("file") == file:
    35→            candidates.append(fid)
    36→    return candidates
    37→
    38→
    39→def _is_finding_alive(state: StateModel, finding_id: str) -> bool:
    40→    """Return True if the finding exists and is open."""
    41→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    42→    if finding is None:
    43→        return False
    44→    return finding.get("status") == "open"
    45→
    46→
    47→def _supersede_id(
    48→    plan: PlanModel,
    49→    state: StateModel,
    50→    finding_id: str,
    51→    now: str,
    52→) -> bool:
    53→    """Move a disappeared finding to superseded. Returns True if changed."""
    54→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    55→    detector = ""
    56→    file = ""
    57→    summary = ""
    58→    if finding:
    59→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    60→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    61→        summary = finding.get("summary", "")
    62→
    63→    candidates = _find_candidates(state, detector, file) if detector else []
    64→    # Don't include the original in candidates
    65→    candidates = [c for c in candidates if c != finding_id]
    66→
    67→    entry: SupersededEntry = {
    68→        "original_id": finding_id,
    69→        "original_detector": detector,
    70→        "original_file": file,
    71→        "original_summary": summary,
    72→        "status": "superseded",
    73→        "superseded_at": now,
    74→        "remapped_to": None,
    75→        "candidates": candidates[:5],
    76→    }
    77→
    78→    # Preserve any existing override note
    79→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(finding_id)
    80→    if override and override.get("note"):
    81→        entry["note"] = override["note"]
    82→
    83→    plan["superseded"][finding_id] = entry
    84→
    85→    # Remove from queue_order, skipped, cluster finding_ids
    86→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    87→    skipped: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    88→    if finding_id in order:
    89→        order.remove(finding_id)
    90→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    91→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    92→        ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
    93→        if finding_id in ids:
    94→            ids.remove(finding_id)
    95→
    96→    # Clear stale cluster reference from override
    97→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(finding_id)
    98→    if override and override.get("cluster"):
    99→        override["cluster"] = None
   100→        override["updated_at"] = now
   101→
   102→    return True
   103→
   104→
   105→def _prune_old_superseded(plan: PlanModel, now_dt: datetime) -> list[str]:
   106→    """Remove superseded entries older than TTL. Returns pruned IDs."""
   107→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   108→    cutoff = now_dt - timedelta(days=SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS)
   109→    to_prune: list[str] = []
   110→
   111→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   112→        ts = entry.get("superseded_at", "")
   113→        try:
   114→            entry_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)
   115→            if entry_dt.tzinfo is None:
   116→                entry_dt = entry_dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
   117→            if entry_dt < cutoff:
   118→                to_prune.append(fid)
   119→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   120→            to_prune.append(fid)
   121→
   122→    for fid in to_prune:
   123→        superseded.pop(fid, None)
   124→        # Also clean up stale overrides
   125→        plan.get("overrides", {}).pop(fid, None)
   126→
   127→    return to_prune
   128→
   129→
   130→def reconcile_plan_after_scan(
   131→    plan: PlanModel,
   132→    state: StateModel,
   133→) -> ReconcileResult:
   134→    """Reconcile plan against current state after a scan.
   135→
   136→    Finds IDs referenced in the plan that no longer exist or are no longer
   137→    open, moves them to superseded, and prunes old superseded entries.
   138→    """
   139→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   140→    result = ReconcileResult()
   141→    now = utc_now()
   142→    now_dt = datetime.now(UTC)
   143→
   144→    # Collect all finding IDs referenced by the plan
   145→    referenced_ids: set[str] = set()
   146→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   147→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   148→    for override_id in plan.get("overrides", {}):
   149→        referenced_ids.add(override_id)
   150→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
```

1→"""Pure mutation operations on the plan model."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     6→    Cluster,
     7→    ExecutionLogEntry,
     8→    PlanModel,
     9→    SkipEntry,
    10→    empty_plan,
    11→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    14→
    15→
    16→_DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES = 10000
    17→
    18→
    19→def _get_log_cap() -> int:
    20→    """Read execution_log_max_entries from config. Returns 0 for unlimited."""
    21→    try:
    22→        from desloppify.core.config import load_config
    23→
    24→        config = load_config()
    25→        value = config.get("execution_log_max_entries", _DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES)
    26→        return max(0, int(value))
    27→    except (ImportError, OSError, ValueError, TypeError):
    28→        return _DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES
    29→
    30→
    31→def append_log_entry(
    32→    plan: PlanModel,
    33→    action: str,
    34→    *,
    35→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    36→    cluster_name: str | None = None,
    37→    actor: str = "user",
    38→    note: str | None = None,
    39→    detail: dict | None = None,
    40→) -> None:
    41→    """Append a structured entry to the plan's execution log."""
    42→    log = plan.get("execution_log", [])
    43→    entry: ExecutionLogEntry = {
    44→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
    45→        "action": action,
    46→        "finding_ids": finding_ids or [],
    47→        "cluster_name": cluster_name,
    48→        "actor": actor,
    49→        "note": note,
    50→        "detail": detail or {},
    51→    }
    52→    log.append(entry)
    53→    cap = _get_log_cap()
    54→    if cap > 0 and len(log) > cap:
    55→        plan["execution_log"] = log[-cap:]
    56→
    57→
    58→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    59→# Helpers
    60→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    61→
    62→def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str) -> None:
    63→    """Remove a finding ID from queue_order and skipped."""
    64→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    65→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
    66→    if finding_id in order:
    67→        order.remove(finding_id)
    68→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    69→
    70→
    71→def _resolve_position(
    72→    order: list[str],
    73→    position: str,
    74→    target: str | None = None,
    75→    offset: int | None = None,
    76→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    77→) -> int:
    78→    """Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.
    79→
    80→    *finding_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative
    81→    positions when they already exist in the list.
    82→    """
    83→    moving = set(finding_ids or [])
    84→
    85→    if position == "top":
    86→        return 0
    87→    if position == "bottom":
    88→        return len(order)
    89→
    90→    if position in {"before", "after"} and target:
    91→        return _resolve_relative_position(order, moving, position=position, target=target)
    92→
    93→    if position in {"up", "down"} and offset is not None:
    94→        return _resolve_offset_position(
    95→            order,
    96→            moving,
    97→            position=position,
    98→            offset=offset,
    99→            finding_ids=finding_ids,
   100→        )

    73→def _clear_needs_rescan_flag(config: dict[str, object]) -> None:

    88→        plan.get("queue_order")
    89→        or plan.get("overrides")

    97→    if not _plan_has_user_content(plan):

    99→    recon = reconcile_fn(plan, state)

   104→                "cyan",

   146→
   147→
   148→def _seed_plan_start_scores(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   149→    """Set plan_start_scores when beginning a new queue cycle."""
   150→    if plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   151→        return False
   152→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   153→    if scores.strict is None:
   154→        return False
   155→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
   156→        "strict": scores.strict,
   157→        "overall": scores.overall,
   158→        "objective": scores.objective,
   159→        "verified": scores.verified,
   160→    }
   161→    return True
   162→
   163→
   164→def _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(
   165→    state: state_mod.StateModel, plan: dict[str, object]
   166→) -> bool:
   167→    """Clear plan-start score snapshot once the queue is fully drained."""
   168→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   169→        return False
   170→
   171→    try:
   172→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import plan_aware_queue_count
   173→
   174→        queue_empty = plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan) == 0
   175→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   176→        logging.debug("Plan operation skipped: %s", exc)
   177→        return False
   178→    if not queue_empty:
   179→        return False
   180→    state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"] = dict(plan["plan_start_scores"])
   181→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
   182→    return True
   183→
   184→
   185→def _reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: "ScanRuntime") -> None:
   186→    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
   187→    try:
   188→        plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json" if runtime.state_path else None
   189→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   190→        dirty = False
   191→
   192→        if _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan, runtime.state, reconcile_plan_after_scan):
   193→            dirty = True
   194→        if _sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, runtime.state, sync_unscored_dimensions):
   195→            dirty = True
   196→        if _sync_stale_dimensions(plan, runtime.state, sync_stale_dimensions):
   197→            dirty = True
   198→        if _sync_auto_clusters(plan, runtime.state):
   199→            dirty = True
   200→        synth_sync = sync_synthesis_needed(plan, runtime.state)
   201→        if synth_sync.changes:
   202→            dirty = True
   203→            if synth_sync.injected:
   204→                print(
   205→                    colorize(
   206→                        "  Plan: synthesis needed — review findings changed since last synthesis.",
   207→                        "cyan",
   208→                    )
   209→                )
   210→        seeded = _seed_plan_start_scores(plan, runtime.state)
   211→        if seeded:
   212→            dirty = True
   213→        # Only clear scores that existed before this reconcile pass —
   214→        # never clear scores we just seeded in the same scan.
   215→        if not seeded and _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(runtime.state, plan):
   216→            dirty = True
   217→
   218→        if dirty:
   219→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   220→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   221→        logging.debug("Plan operation skipped: %s", exc)
   222→
   223→
   224→def _state_subjective_assessments(
   225→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   226→) -> dict[str, object]:
   227→    """Return normalized subjective assessment store from state."""
   228→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
   229→    if isinstance(assessments, dict):
   230→        return assessments
   231→    raise ScanStateContractError(
   232→        "state.subjective_assessments must be an object; rerun with a valid state file"
   233→    )
   234→
   235→
   236→def _state_lang_capabilities(
   237→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   238→) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
   239→    """Return normalized language capability map from state."""
   240→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities")
   241→    if capabilities is None:
   242→        normalized: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {}
   243→        state["lang_capabilities"] = normalized
   244→        return normalized
   245→    if isinstance(capabilities, dict):
   246→        return capabilities

   248→        "state.lang_capabilities must be an object when present"

1→"""Queue-policy helpers shared by planning render/select modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
     9→    QueueBuildOptions,
    10→    WorkQueueResult,
    11→    build_work_queue,
    12→)
    13→
    14→
    15→def _subjective_threshold(state: dict[str, Any], *, default: float = 95.0) -> float:
    16→    config = state.get("config", {})
    17→    raw_target = default
    18→    if isinstance(config, dict):
    19→        raw_target = config.get("target_strict_score", default)
    20→    try:
    21→        value = float(raw_target)
    22→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    23→        value = default
    24→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, value))
    25→
    26→
    27→@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
    28→class OpenPlanQueuePolicy:
    29→    tier: int | None = None
    30→    count: int | None = None
    31→    scan_path: str | None = None
    32→    include_subjective: bool = True
    33→    no_tier_fallback: bool = True
    34→
    35→
    36→def build_open_plan_queue(
    37→    state: dict[str, Any],
    38→    policy: OpenPlanQueuePolicy | None = None,
    39→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    40→    """Build one open-status queue with consistent planning policy defaults."""
    41→    policy = policy or OpenPlanQueuePolicy()
    42→    resolved_scan_path: str | None
    43→    if isinstance(policy.scan_path, str):
    44→        resolved_scan_path = policy.scan_path
    45→    else:
    46→        raw_state_path = state.get("scan_path")
    47→        resolved_scan_path = raw_state_path if isinstance(raw_state_path, str) else None
    48→    return build_work_queue(
    49→        state,
    50→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    51→            tier=policy.tier,
    52→            count=policy.count,
    53→            scan_path=resolved_scan_path,
    54→            status="open",
    55→            include_subjective=policy.include_subjective,
    56→            subjective_threshold=_subjective_threshold(state),
    57→            no_tier_fallback=policy.no_tier_fallback,
    58→        ),
    59→    )
    60→
    61→
    62→__all__ = ["OpenPlanQueuePolicy", "build_open_plan_queue"]
    63→

1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — prepend never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *front* of the queue unconditionally (onboarding priority).
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import hashlib
    14→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    15→
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    17→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    18→
    19→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    20→SYNTHESIS_ID = "synthesis::pending"
    21→
    22→
    23→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    24→# Result dataclasses
    25→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    26→
    27→@dataclass
    28→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    29→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    30→
    31→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    32→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    33→
    34→    @property
    35→    def changes(self) -> int:
    36→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    37→
    38→
    39→@dataclass
    40→class UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    41→    """What changed during an unscored-dimension sync."""
    42→
    43→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    44→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    45→
    46→    @property
    47→    def changes(self) -> int:
    48→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    49→
    50→
    51→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    52→# ID helpers
    53→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    54→
    55→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    56→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    57→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    58→    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    59→        scorecard_subjective_entries,
    60→    )
    61→
    62→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    63→    if not dim_scores:
    64→        return set()
    65→
    66→    stale: set[str] = set()
    67→    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    68→        if not entry.get("stale"):
    69→            continue
    70→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    71→        if dim_key:
    72→            stale.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    73→    return stale
    74→
    75→
    76→def _current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    77→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    78→
    79→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` directly because
    80→    ``scorecard_subjective_entries`` filters out placeholder dimensions
    81→    (they are hidden from the scorecard display).
    82→    """
    83→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    84→
    85→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    86→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    87→        return set()
    88→
    89→    unscored: set[str] = set()
    90→    for dim_key, payload in assessments.items():
    91→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    92→            continue
    93→        if not payload.get("placeholder"):
    94→            continue
    95→        if dim_key:
    96→            unscored.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    97→    return unscored
    98→
    99→
   100→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   101→# Unscored dimension sync (front of queue, unconditional)
   102→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   103→
   104→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   105→    plan: PlanModel,
   106→    state: StateModel,
   107→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
   108→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   109→
   110→    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
   111→       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
   112→       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
   113→    2. **Inject** — unconditionally prepend currently-unscored IDs to the
   114→       *front* of ``queue_order`` so initial reviews are the first priority.
   115→    """
   116→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   117→    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
   118→    unscored_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   119→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   120→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   121→
   122→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   123→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   124→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   125→        fid for fid in order
   126→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   127→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   128→        and fid not in stale_ids
   129→    ]
   130→    for fid in to_remove:
   131→        order.remove(fid)
   132→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   133→
   134→    # --- Inject: prepend unscored IDs to front ----------------------------
   135→    existing = set(order)
   136→    for uid in reversed(sorted(unscored_ids)):
   137→        if uid not in existing:
   138→            order.insert(0, uid)
   139→            result.injected.append(uid)
   140→
   141→    return result
   142→
   143→
   144→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   145→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   146→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   147→
   148→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   149→    plan: PlanModel,
   150→    state: StateModel,
   151→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
   152→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale subjective dimensions.
   153→
   154→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   155→       longer stale and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned by
   156→       ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   157→    2. If no objective items remain after cleanup, inject all currently-stale
   158→       dimension IDs so the plan surfaces them as actionable work.
   159→    """
   160→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   161→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
   162→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   163→    unscored_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   164→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   165→
   166→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   167→    # Only prune IDs that are neither stale nor unscored.
   168→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   169→        fid for fid in order
   170→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   171→        and fid not in stale_ids
   172→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   173→    ]
   174→    for fid in to_remove:
   175→        order.remove(fid)
   176→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   177→
   178→    # --- Inject: populate when no objective items remain -----------------
   179→    has_real_items = any(fid for fid in order if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX))
   180→    if not has_real_items and stale_ids:

   183→            if sid not in existing:
   184→                order.append(sid)
   185→                result.injected.append(sid)
   186→
   187→    return result
   188→

190→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   191→# Synthesis snapshot hash + sync
   192→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   193→
   194→def review_finding_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   195→    """Hash open review finding IDs to detect changes.
   196→
   197→    Returns empty string when there are no open review findings.
   198→    """
   199→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   200→    review_ids = sorted(
   201→        fid for fid, f in findings.items()
   202→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   203→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   204→    )
   205→    if not review_ids:
   206→        return ""
   207→    return hashlib.sha256("|".join(review_ids).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
   208→
   209→
   210→@dataclass
   211→class SynthesisSyncResult:
   212→    """What changed during a synthesis sync."""
   213→
   214→    injected: bool = False
   215→    pruned: bool = False
   216→
   217→    @property
   218→    def changes(self) -> int:
   219→        return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)
   220→
   221→
   222→def sync_synthesis_needed(
   223→    plan: PlanModel,
   224→    state: StateModel,
   225→) -> SynthesisSyncResult:
   226→    """Inject ``synthesis::pending`` at front of queue when review findings change.
   227→
   228→    Never auto-prunes — only explicit completion (``_apply_completion``) removes it.
   229→    """
   230→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   231→    result = SynthesisSyncResult()
   232→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   233→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   234→    already_present = SYNTHESIS_ID in order
   235→
   236→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   237→    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
   238→
   239→    # If hash changed and not already present, inject at front
   240→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash and not already_present:
   241→        order.insert(0, SYNTHESIS_ID)
   242→        result.injected = True
   243→
   244→    # Never auto-prune: synthesis::pending is only removed by explicit completion
   245→
   246→    return result
   247→
   248→
   249→__all__ = [
   250→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   251→    "SYNTHESIS_ID",
   252→    "StaleDimensionSyncResult",
   253→    "SynthesisSyncResult",
   254→    "UnscoredDimensionSyncResult",
   255→    "_current_unscored_ids",
   256→    "review_finding_snapshot_hash",
   257→    "sync_stale_dimensions",
   258→    "sync_synthesis_needed",
   259→    "sync_unscored_dimensions",
   260→]
   261→

   100→def _apply_plan_order(
   101→    items: list[dict],
   102→    plan: dict,
   103→    *,
   104→    include_skipped: bool = False,
   105→    cluster: str | None = None,
   106→) -> list[dict]:
   107→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
   108→
   109→    1. Items in ``queue_order`` appear first, in that order.
   110→    2. Remaining items keep their mechanical sort.
   111→    3. Skipped items are appended last (or excluded).
   112→    4. Each item is annotated with plan metadata.
   113→    """
   114→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   115→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   116→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
   117→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
   118→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
   119→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
   120→
   121→    # Build lookup
   122→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
   123→    for item in items:
   124→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
   125→
   126→    # Annotate items with plan metadata
   127→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
   128→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
   129→        if override.get("description"):
   130→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
   131→        if override.get("note"):
   132→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
   133→        if override.get("cluster"):
   134→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
   135→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   136→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
   137→                "name": cluster_name,
   138→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
   139→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
   140→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
   141→            }
   142→
   143→    # Split into ordered, remaining, skipped
   144→    ordered: list[dict] = []
   145→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
   146→    for fid in queue_order:
   147→        if fid in by_id and fid not in skipped_ids:
   148→            ordered.append(by_id[fid])
   149→            ordered_ids.add(fid)
   150→
   151→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   152→    remaining: list[dict] = []
   153→    for item in items:
   154→        item_id = item["id"]
   155→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
   156→            continue
   157→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
   158→            skipped_items.append(item)
   159→        else:
   160→            remaining.append(item)
   161→
   162→    # Assign queue positions
   163→    result = ordered + remaining
   164→    if include_skipped:
   165→        result = result + skipped_items
   166→
   167→    for pos, item in enumerate(result):
   168→        item["queue_position"] = pos + 1
   169→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   170→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   171→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   172→            if skip_entry:
   173→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   174→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   175→                if skip_reason:
   176→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   177→
   178→    # Filter to cluster if requested
   179→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   180→    if effective_cluster:
   181→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   182→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   183→        if cluster_member_ids:
   184→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   185→
   186→    return result
   187→
   188→
   189→def _item_matches_tier(item: dict, tier: int) -> bool:
   190→    """Check if an item (or any of its members for clusters) matches a tier."""
   191→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   192→        return any(
   193→            int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   194→            for m in item.get("members", [])
   195→        )
   196→    return int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   197→
   198→
   199→def _action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   200→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   201→    try:
   202→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   203→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   204→        if meta:
   205→            return meta.action_type
   206→    except ImportError as exc:
   207→        _ = exc
   208→    return "manual_fix"
   209→
   210→
   211→_ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
   212→
   213→
   214→def _collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   215→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items."""
   216→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   217→    if not clusters:
   218→        return items
   219→
   220→    # Build mapping: finding_id → auto-cluster name
   221→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   222→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   223→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   224→            continue
   225→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   226→            fid_to_cluster[fid] = name
   227→
   228→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   229→        return items
   230→
   231→    # Collect members for each cluster, preserving order
   232→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   233→    non_cluster_items: list[dict] = []
   234→
   235→    for item in items:
   236→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   237→        if cname:
   238→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   239→        else:
   240→            non_cluster_items.append(item)
   241→
   242→    # Build cluster meta-items
   243→    result: list[dict] = list(non_cluster_items)
   244→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   245→        # Don't collapse singletons — show them as individual findings
   246→        if len(members) < 2:
   247→            result.extend(members)
   248→            continue
   249→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cname, {})
   250→        detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   251→        action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   252→        # Derive action_type from the actual cluster action, not just the detector
   253→        if "desloppify fix" in action:
   254→            action_type = "auto_fix"
   255→        elif "desloppify move" in action:
   256→            action_type = "reorganize"
   257→        else:
   258→            action_type = _action_type_for_detector(detector)
   259→            # If detector says auto_fix but cluster has no fix command, it's really refactor
   260→            if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify fix" not in action:
   261→                action_type = "refactor"
   262→
   263→        # Use stored description but patch the count if it differs from visible members
   264→        stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   265→        total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   266→        if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   267→            # Replace the stored count with the visible count
   268→            summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   269→        else:
   270→            summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   271→
   272→        primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   273→        if not primary_command:
   274→            primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cname} --count 10"

   408→def _plan_superseded_section(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   409→    """Render the superseded items section."""

   433→def _render_plan_item(item: dict, override: dict) -> list[str]:
   434→    """Render a single plan item as markdown lines."""
   435→    tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   436→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   437→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
   438→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
   439→
   440→    lines = [f"- [ ] [T{tier}/{confidence}] {summary}"]
   441→    desc = override.get("description")
   442→    if desc:
   443→        lines.append(f"      → {desc}")
   444→    lines.append(f"      `{item_id}`")
   445→    note = override.get("note")
   446→    if note:
   447→        lines.append(f"      Note: {note}")

   451→def generate_plan_md(state: PlanState, plan: dict | None = None) -> str:
   452→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state.
   453→
   454→    When *plan* is provided (or auto-loaded from disk), user-ordered
   455→    items, clusters, skipped, and superseded sections are rendered.
   456→    When no plan exists, output is identical to the previous behavior.
   457→    """
   458→    findings = state["findings"]
   459→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   460→
   461→    # Auto-load plan if not provided
   462→    if plan is None:
   463→        try:
   464→            from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
   465→            plan = load_plan()
   466→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   467→            plan = {}
   468→
   469→    has_plan = bool(
   470→        plan
   471→        and (
   472→            plan.get("queue_order")
   473→            or plan.get("skipped")
   474→            or plan.get("clusters")
   475→        )
   476→    )
   477→
   478→    lines = _plan_header(state, stats)
   479→    lines.extend(_plan_dimension_table(state))
   480→    lines.extend(_tier_summary_lines(stats))
   481→
   482→    if has_plan:
   483→        # Build full queue for item lookup
   484→        queue = build_work_queue(
   485→            state,
   486→            options=QueueBuildOptions(
   487→                count=None,
   488→                scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   489→                status="open",
   490→                include_subjective=True,
   491→                no_tier_fallback=True,
   492→            ),
   493→        )
   494→        all_items = queue.get("items", [])
   495→        lines.extend(_plan_user_ordered_section(all_items, plan))
   496→
   497→        # Remaining: items NOT in queue_order or skipped
   498→        ordered_ids = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   499→        skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
```

## Planning Phase in Desloppify: Comprehensive Overview

I've thoroughly explored the planning phase architecture. Here's a detailed breakdown of the distinct stages, decisions, and logging mechanisms.

---

### **HIGH-LEVEL FLOW: When is a Plan Generated/Regenerated?**

The planning phase happens in **two contexts**:

1. **Interactive markdown generation** (`desloppify plan`) — reads state & existing plan, renders markdown
2. **Post-scan reconciliation** (`scan_workflow.py:_reconcile_plan_post_scan`) — auto-updates plan after scan

---

### **DISTINCT STAGES IN PLAN GENERATION/SYNTHESIS**

#### **Stage 1: Finding Normalization & Collection (Computational)**
**File:** `desloppify/engine/planning/scan.py:generate_findings()`
- Runs all detector phases in order
- Stamps findings with language, zone, confidence
- **NOT executing log entries** — purely computational

---

#### **Stage 2: Post-Scan Plan Reconciliation (Mutating, Auto)**
**File:** `desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_workflow.py:_reconcile_plan_post_scan()`

**Sub-stages (in sequence):**

1. **Plan Reconciliation** (if plan has user content)
   - **Module:** `desloppify/engine/_plan/reconcile.py:reconcile_plan_after_scan()`
   - **Action:** Marks disappeared findings as `superseded`, prunes old superseded entries (90-day TTL)
   - **Result type:** `ReconcileResult` → `superseded`, `pruned`, `resurfaced`, `changes` count
   - **Logging:** ❌ **No append_log_entry calls**

2. **Unscored Dimension Sync** (unconditional, front of queue)
   - **Module:** `desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py:sync_unscored_dimensions()`
   - **Action:** Injects `subjective::<slug>` IDs for placeholder dimensions at **front** of queue
   - **Result type:** `UnscoredDimensionSyncResult` → `injected`, `pruned`, `changes` count
   - **Logging:** ❌ **No append_log_entry calls**

3. **Stale Dimension Sync** (conditional, back of queue)
   - **Module:** `desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py:sync_stale_dimensions()`
   - **Action:** Injects `subjective::<slug>` IDs for stale dimensions at **back** of queue (only if no objective items remain)
   - **Result type:** `StaleDimensionSyncResult` → `injected`, `pruned`, `changes` count
   - **Logging:** ❌ **No append_log_entry calls**

4. **Auto-Clustering** (regenerative)
   - **Module:** `desloppify/engine/_plan/auto_cluster.py:auto_cluster_findings()`
   - **Action:** Groups open findings into task clusters by detector/subtype/dimension
   - **Creates/updates:** `auto/*` clusters, annotations to `overrides`
   - **Result:** Change count (clusters created/updated/deleted)
   - **Logging:** ❌ **No append_log_entry calls**

5. **Synthesis Trigger Sync**
   - **Module:** `desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py:sync_synthesis_needed()`
   - **Action:** Injects `synthesis::pending` at front of queue if review findings hash changed
   - **Result type:** `SynthesisSyncResult` → `injected` bool, `pruned` bool, `changes` count
   - **Logging:** ❌ **No append_log_entry calls**

6. **Plan-Start Scores Seeding** (optional)
   - **Module:** `desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_workflow.py:_seed_plan_start_scores()`
   - **Action:** Captures current health scores when beginning a new queue cycle
   - **Logging:** ❌ **No append_log_entry calls**

7. **Plan-Start Scores Clearing** (optional)
   - **Module:** `desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_workflow.py:_clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty()`
   - **Action:** Clears plan-start scores once queue is fully drained
   - **Logging:** ❌ **No append_log_entry calls**

**All these use:** `save_plan(plan, plan_path)` at the end

---

#### **Stage 3: Interactive Synthesis (Manual, User-Driven)**
**File:** `desloppify/app/commands/plan/synthesize_handlers.py`

**Orchestrated as 4 sequential stages with interdependencies:**

```
OBSERVE → REFLECT → ORGANIZE → COMMIT
```

**Dependency graph (from `synthesis_playbook.py`):**
- `observe`: depends on nothing
- `reflect`: depends on `observe`
- `organize`: depends on `reflect`
- `commit`: depends on `organize`

**Each stage:**
- Validates user input (report length: 100 char min for observe/reflect, 200 char min for commit, or "same")
- Records stage payload to `plan.epic_synthesis_meta.synthesis_stages[stage_name]`
- Updates `stage_snapshot_hash` to match current review-finding snapshot
- Saves plan

**Sub-handlers (in `desloppify/app/commands/plan/synthesis/`):**

1. **Observe** (`_cmd_stage_observe`)
   - Collects synthesis input
   - Validates report (100 char min)
   - Records: `stage`, `report`, `cited_ids`, `timestamp`, `finding_count`
   - **Logging:** ❌ **No append_log_entry**

2. **Reflect** (`_cmd_stage_reflect`)
   - Calls `collect_synthesis_input()` + `detect_recurring_patterns()`
   - Renders completed clusters, resolved findings, recurring patterns
   - Validates report (100 char min)
   - Records: `stage`, `report`, `cited_ids`, `timestamp`, `finding_count`, `patterns`
   - **Logging:** ❌ **No append_log_entry**

3. **Organize** (`_cmd_stage_organize`)
   - Validates all non-auto clusters have `description` + `action_steps`
   - Records: `stage`, `report`, `cited_ids`, `timestamp`, `finding_count`
   - **Logging:** ❌ **No append_log_entry**

4. **Commit** (`_cmd_stage_complete` / `_apply_completion`)
   - Removes `synthesis::pending` from queue_order
   - Writes `epic_synthesis_meta`: `finding_snapshot_hash`, `strategy_summary`, `trigger: "manual_synthesis"`, `last_completed_at`
   - Clears `synthesis_stages` dict
   - **Logging:** ❌ **No append_log_entry**

---

#### **Stage 4: Epic Synthesis LLM (Computational → Mutating)**
**File:** `desloppify/engine/_plan/epic_synthesis.py`

**Sub-steps:**

1. **Input Collection** (`collect_synthesis_input`)
   - Gathers open review findings, mechanical findings, existing epics, dimension scores
   - Detects new/resolved findings since last synthesis
   - Retrieves completed clusters from history
   - Returns: `SynthesisInput` dataclass

2. **Prompt Building** (`build_synthesis_prompt`)
   - Formats all input into structured text for LLM
   - Sections: existing epics, what changed, all open findings, dimension scores, previously dismissed
   - **Purely computational** — no state mutation

3. **LLM Call** (via `deps.llm_call` if available)
   - Calls system prompt + user prompt
   - Parses JSON response
   - **Purely computational** — no state mutation

4. **Result Parsing** (`parse_synthesis_result`)
   - Validates JSON structure
   - Filters finding IDs against valid set
   - Extracts: `strategy_summary`, `epics[]`, `dismissed_findings[]`, `contradiction_notes[]`, `priority_rationale`
   - Returns: `SynthesisResult` dataclass
   - **Purely computational** — no state mutation

5. **Plan Mutation** (`apply_synthesis_to_plan`)
   - **Creates/updates synthesis clusters** in `plan["clusters"]`
   - **Dismisses findings** → moves to `plan["skipped"]` with `kind: "synthesized_out"`
   - **Reorders queue_order** → epic items first (by `dependency_order`), then non-epic items
   - **Updates epic_synthesis_meta**:
     - `synthesized_ids`, `dismissed_ids`, `version`, `last_run`, `finding_snapshot_hash`, `strategy_summary`, `trigger`
   - Returns: `SynthesisMutationResult` with counters
   - **Mutating** — modifies plan in-place
   - **Logging:** ❌ **No append_log_entry**

---

#### **Stage 5: Plan Markdown Rendering (Read-only)**
**File:** `desloppify/engine/planning/render.py:generate_plan_md()`

**Sections (in order):**

1. **Header:** Score, stats (open/fixed/wontfix)
2. **Dimension Health Table:** Per-dimension scores, tiers, actions
3. **Tier Summary:** Progress by tier
4. **User-Ordered Queue:** Items from `plan["queue_order"]`, grouped by cluster
5. **Skipped Items:** Grouped by kind (temporary/permanent/false_positive)
6. **Superseded Items:** Disappeared findings with remapping candidates
7. **Remaining Tier Sections:** Items NOT in `queue_order` (or `skipped`), grouped by tier

**Plan-aware processing:**
- `_apply_plan_order()` annotates items with plan metadata (description, note, cluster info)
- Clusters are rendered via `_collapse_clusters()` as meta-items
- Auto-clusters are hidden; manual/epic clusters are shown

**Logging:** ❌ **No append_log_entry calls**

---

### **WHICH OPERATIONS HAVE EXECUTION LOG ENTRIES?**

**Yes — Only User-Facing Plan Mutations:**

1. **Plan reset** (`desloppify plan reset`)
   - `append_log_entry(plan, "reset", actor="user", detail={"previous_queue_size": ..., "previous_cluster_count": ...})`

2. **Plan move** (`desloppify plan move <id> <position>`)
   - `append_log_entry(plan, "move", finding_ids=[...], actor="user", note=...)`

3. **Cluster operations** (create/delete/add/update)
   - `append_log_entry(plan, "cluster_create", cluster_name=..., actor="user")`
   - `append_log_entry(plan, "cluster_delete", cluster_name=..., actor="user")`
   - `append_log_entry(plan, "cluster_add", cluster_name=..., finding_ids=[...], actor="user")`
   - etc.

4. **Override operations** (describe/note/focus/skip/unskip/reopen/done)
   - `append_log_entry(plan, "describe", finding_ids=[...], actor="user", detail={"description": ...})`
   - `append_log_entry(plan, "done", finding_ids=[...], actor="user", note=...)`
   - etc.

**No — Automated Plan Reconciliation/Synthesis:**
- ❌ Reconciliation (supersede, prune)
- ❌ Dimension sync (unscored, stale, synthesis)
- ❌ Auto-clustering
- ❌ Epic synthesis LLM + mutation

---

### **DISTINCTLY READ-ONLY VS MUTATING STEPS**

| Step | Type | Mutates Plan | Executes Phase |
|------|------|--------------|----------------|
| Finding normalization | Computational | ❌ | ❌ |
| Plan reconciliation | Mutating | ✅ (post-scan) | ❌ |
| Unscored dimension sync | Mutating | ✅ (post-scan) | ❌ |
| Stale dimension sync | Mutating | ✅ (post-scan) | ❌ |
| Auto-clustering | Mutating | ✅ (post-scan) | ❌ |
| Synthesis trigger sync | Mutating | ✅ (post-scan) | ❌ |
| Plan-start score seeding | Mutating | ✅ (post-scan) | ❌ |
| Synthesis stages (observe/reflect/organize) | Mutating | ✅ (user) | ❌ |
| Epic synthesis LLM | Computational | ❌ (up to result parsing) | ❌ |
| Epic synthesis apply | Mutating | ✅ (user, via complete) | ❌ |
| Plan reordering (work queue) | Computational | ❌ | ❌ |
| Plan markdown rendering | Computational | ❌ | ❌ |

---

### **KEY ARCHITECTURAL INSIGHTS**

1. **Execution Log is User-Centric:** Only explicit user actions (`plan move`, `plan cluster create`, etc.) create log entries. Automated sync steps do NOT log.

2. **Post-Scan Reconciliation is "Best-Effort":** All reconciliation steps are wrapped in `try/except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS`, so failures don't break the scan.

3. **Plan Order Application is Deferred:** `plan["queue_order"]` only affects output when rendering/selecting items via `_apply_plan_order()` in the work queue. The queue itself is always built from findings + tiers; the plan just reorders.

4. **Synthesis is a User Flow:** Unlike auto-clustering (which happens silently), synthesis is a 4-stage interactive process requiring human input at each gate. The LLM only runs when explicitly invoked.

5. **Two Independent Dimension Syncs:** Unscored (front, unconditional) and stale (back, conditional) are separate to prioritize onboarding while deferring refresh work.

6. **Finding Snapshot Hash:** Drives synthesis triggering — any change to open review finding IDs injects `synthesis::pending` at queue front.

---

### **FILE LOCATIONS QUICK REFERENCE**

- **Post-scan reconciliation orchestrator:** `/desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_workflow.py:_reconcile_plan_post_scan`
- **Plan reconciliation:** `/desloppify/engine/_plan/reconcile.py`
- **Dimension syncs:** `/desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py`
- **Auto-clustering:** `/desloppify/engine/_plan/auto_cluster.py`
- **Epic synthesis engine:** `/desloppify/engine/_plan/epic_synthesis.py`
- **Synthesis interactive handlers:** `/desloppify/app/commands/plan/synthesize_handlers.py`
- **Plan markdown rendering:** `/desloppify/engine/planning/render.py`
- **Work queue + plan order:** `/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py:_apply_plan_order`
- **User plan operations (logging):** `/desloppify/app/commands/plan/{cmd,cluster_handlers,override_handlers,move_handlers}.py`
- **Plan operations API:** `/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations.py`

1→"""Epic synthesis engine — LLM-driven meta-plan for review findings.
     2→
     3→Clusters review findings by root cause, dismisses false positives,
     4→resolves contradictions, and orders by dependency. The synthesis is incremental:
     5→it updates existing synthesis-clusters rather than recreating from scratch.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→import json
    11→import logging
    12→import re
    13→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    14→from typing import Any
    15→
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    17→    EPIC_PREFIX,
    18→    VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS,
    19→    Cluster,
    20→    PlanModel,
    21→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    22→    synthesis_clusters,
    23→)

    25→    SYNTHESIS_ID,
    26→    review_finding_snapshot_hash,
    27→)
