```
    15→from desloppify.core.output_api import LOC_COMPACT_THRESHOLD
    16→
    17→
    18→def _plan_header(state: PlanState, stats: dict) -> list[str]:
    19→    """Build the plan header: title, score line, and codebase metrics."""
    20→    overall_score = get_overall_score(state)
    21→    objective_score = get_objective_score(state)
    22→    strict_score = get_strict_score(state)
    23→
    24→    if (
    25→        overall_score is not None
    26→        and objective_score is not None
    27→        and strict_score is not None
    28→    ):
    29→        header_score = (
    30→            f"**Health:** overall {overall_score:.1f}/100 | "
    31→            f"objective {objective_score:.1f}/100 | "
    32→            f"strict {strict_score:.1f}/100"
    33→        )
    34→    elif overall_score is not None:
    35→        header_score = f"**Score: {overall_score:.1f}/100**"
    36→    else:
    37→        header_score = "**Scores unavailable**"
    38→
    39→    metrics = state.get("codebase_metrics", {})
    40→    total_files = sum(metric.get("total_files", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    41→    total_loc = sum(metric.get("total_loc", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    42→    total_dirs = sum(metric.get("total_directories", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    43→
    44→    lines = [
    45→        f"# Desloppify Plan — {date.today().isoformat()}",
    46→        "",
    47→        f"{header_score} | "
    48→        f"{stats.get('open', 0)} open | "
    49→        f"{stats.get('fixed', 0)} fixed | "
    50→        f"{stats.get('wontfix', 0)} wontfix | "
    51→        f"{stats.get('auto_resolved', 0)} auto-resolved",
    52→        "",
    53→    ]
    54→
    55→    if total_files:
    56→        loc_str = (
    57→            f"{total_loc:,}"
    58→            if total_loc < LOC_COMPACT_THRESHOLD
    59→            else f"{total_loc // 1000}K"
    60→        )
    61→        lines.append(
    62→            f"\n{total_files} files · {loc_str} LOC · {total_dirs} directories\n"
    63→        )
    64→
    65→    return lines
    66→
    67→
    68→def _plan_dimension_table(state: PlanState) -> list[str]:
    69→    """Build the dimension health table rows (empty list when no data)."""
    70→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
    71→    if not dim_scores:
    72→        return []
    73→
    74→    lines = [
    75→        "## Health by Dimension",
    76→        "",
    77→        "| Dimension | Tier | Checks | Issues | Health | Strict | Action |",
    78→        "|-----------|------|--------|--------|--------|--------|--------|",
    79→    ]
    80→    static_names: set[str] = set()
    81→    rendered_names: set[str] = set()
    82→    subjective_display_names = {
    83→        display.lower() for display in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()
    84→    }
    85→
    86→    def _looks_subjective(name: str, data: dict) -> bool:
    87→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    88→        if "subjective_assessment" in detectors:
    89→            return True
    90→        lowered = name.strip().lower()
    91→        return lowered in subjective_display_names or lowered.startswith("elegance")
    92→
    93→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
    94→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
    95→        if not ds:
    96→            continue
    97→        static_names.add(dim.name)
    98→        rendered_names.add(dim.name)
    99→        checks = ds.get("checks", 0)
   100→        issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   101→        score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   102→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   103→        bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   104→        action = dimension_action_type(dim.name)
   105→        lines.append(
   106→            f"| {bold}{dim.name}{bold} | T{dim.tier} | "
   107→            f"{checks:,} | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | {action} |"
   108→        )
   109→
   110→    from desloppify.engine.planning.dimension_rows import scorecard_dimension_rows
   111→
   112→    scorecard_rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state)
   113→    scorecard_subjective_rows = [
   114→        (name, ds) for name, ds in scorecard_rows if _looks_subjective(name, ds)
   115→    ]
   116→    scorecard_subjective_names = {name for name, _ in scorecard_subjective_rows}
   117→
   118→    # Show custom dimensions not present in scorecard.png in the main table.
   119→    custom_non_subjective_rows: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
   120→    for name, ds in sorted(dim_scores.items(), key=lambda item: str(item[0]).lower()):
   121→        if name in rendered_names or not isinstance(ds, dict):
   122→            continue
   123→        if _looks_subjective(name, ds):
   124→            continue
   125→        custom_non_subjective_rows.append((name, ds))
   126→        rendered_names.add(name)
   127→
   128→    for name, ds in custom_non_subjective_rows:
   129→        checks = ds.get("checks", 0)
   130→        issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   131→        score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   132→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   133→        tier = int(ds.get("tier", 3) or 3)
   134→        bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   135→        action = dimension_action_type(name)
   136→        lines.append(
   137→            f"| {bold}{name}{bold} | T{tier} | "
   138→            f"{checks:,} | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | {action} |"
   139→        )
   140→
   141→    extra_subjective_rows = [
   142→        (name, ds)
   143→        for name, ds in sorted(
   144→            dim_scores.items(), key=lambda item: str(item[0]).lower()
   145→        )
   146→        if (
   147→            isinstance(ds, dict)
   148→            and name not in scorecard_subjective_names
   149→            and name.strip().lower() not in subjective_display_names
   150→            and name.strip().lower() not in {"elegance", "elegance (combined)"}
   151→            and _looks_subjective(name, ds)
   152→        )
   153→    ]
   154→    subjective_rows = [*scorecard_subjective_rows, *extra_subjective_rows]
   155→
   156→    if subjective_rows:
   157→        lines.append("| **Subjective Measures (matches scorecard.png)** | | | | | | |")
   158→        for name, ds in subjective_rows:
   159→            issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   160→            score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   161→            strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   162→            tier = ds.get("tier", 4)
   163→            bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   164→            lines.append(
   165→                f"| {bold}{name}{bold} | T{tier} | "
   166→                f"— | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | review |"
   167→            )
   168→
   169→    lines.append("")
   170→    return lines
   171→
   172→
   173→def _plan_tier_sections(findings: dict, *, state: PlanState | None = None) -> list[str]:
   174→    """Build per-tier sections from the shared work-queue backend."""
   175→
   176→    queue_state: PlanState | dict = state or {"findings": findings}
   177→    scan_path = state.get("scan_path") if state else None
   178→    raw_target = (
   179→        (state or {}).get("config", {}).get("target_strict_score", 95)
   180→        if isinstance(state, dict)
   181→        else 95
   182→    )
   183→    try:
   184→        subjective_threshold = float(raw_target)
   185→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   186→        subjective_threshold = 95.0
   187→    subjective_threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold))
   188→    if "findings" not in queue_state:
   189→        queue_state = {**queue_state, "findings": findings}
   190→
   191→    queue = build_work_queue(
   192→        queue_state,
   193→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   194→            count=None,
   195→            scan_path=scan_path,
   196→            status="open",
   197→            include_subjective=True,
   198→            subjective_threshold=subjective_threshold,
   199→            no_tier_fallback=True,
   200→        ),
   201→    )
   202→    open_items = queue.get("items", [])
   203→    by_tier_file: dict[int, dict[str, list]] = defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(list))
   204→    for item in open_items:
   205→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   206→        by_tier_file[tier][item.get("file", ".")].append(item)
   207→
   208→    lines: list[str] = []
   209→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
   210→        tier_files = by_tier_file.get(tier_num, {})
   211→        if not tier_files:
   212→            continue
   213→
   214→        label = TIER_LABELS.get(tier_num, f"Tier {tier_num}")
   215→        tier_count = sum(len(file_findings) for file_findings in tier_files.values())
   216→        lines.extend(
   217→            [
   218→                "---",
   219→                f"## Tier {tier_num}: {label} ({tier_count} open)",
   220→                "",
   221→            ]
   222→        )
   223→
   224→        sorted_files = sorted(
   225→            tier_files.items(), key=lambda item: (-len(item[1]), item[0])
   226→        )
   227→        for filepath, file_items in sorted_files:
   228→            display_path = "Codebase-wide" if filepath == "." else filepath
   229→            lines.append(f"### `{display_path}` ({len(file_items)} findings)")
   230→            lines.append("")
   231→            for item in file_items:
   232→                if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   233→                    lines.append(f"- [ ] [subjective] {item.get('summary', '')}")
   234→                    lines.append(f"      `{item.get('id', '')}`")
   235→                    if item.get("primary_command"):
   236→                        lines.append(f"      action: `{item['primary_command']}`")
   237→                    continue
   238→
   239→                conf_badge = f"[{item.get('confidence', 'medium')}]"
   240→                lines.append(f"- [ ] {conf_badge} {item.get('summary', '')}")
   241→                lines.append(f"      `{item.get('id', '')}`")
   242→            lines.append("")
   243→
   244→    return lines
   245→
   246→
   247→def _tier_summary_lines(stats: dict) -> list[str]:
   248→    lines: list[str] = []
   249→    by_tier = stats.get("by_tier", {})
   250→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
   251→        tier_stats = by_tier.get(str(tier_num), {})
   252→        open_count = tier_stats.get("open", 0)
   253→        total = sum(tier_stats.values())
   254→        addressed = total - open_count
   255→        pct = round(addressed / total * 100) if total else 100
   256→        label = TIER_LABELS.get(tier_num, f"Tier {tier_num}")
   257→        lines.append(
   258→            f"- **Tier {tier_num}** ({label}): {open_count} open / {total} total ({pct}% addressed)"
   259→        )
   260→    lines.append("")
   261→    return lines
   262→
   263→
   264→def _addressed_section(findings: dict) -> list[str]:
   265→    addressed = [
   266→        finding for finding in findings.values() if finding["status"] != "open"
   267→    ]
   268→    if not addressed:
   269→        return []
   270→
   271→    lines: list[str] = ["---", "## Addressed", ""]
   272→    by_status: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
   273→    for finding in addressed:
   274→        by_status[finding["status"]] += 1
   275→    for status, count in sorted(by_status.items()):
   276→        lines.append(f"- **{status}**: {count}")
   277→
   278→    wontfix = [
   279→        finding
   280→        for finding in addressed
   281→        if finding["status"] == "wontfix" and finding.get("note")
   282→    ]
   283→    if wontfix:
   284→        lines.extend(["", "### Wontfix (with explanations)", ""])
   285→        for finding in wontfix[:30]:
   286→            lines.append(f"- `{finding['id']}` — {finding['note']}")
   287→
   288→    lines.append("")
   289→    return lines
   290→
   291→
   292→def _plan_user_ordered_section(
   293→    items: list[dict],
   294→    plan: dict,
   295→) -> list[str]:
   296→    """Render the user-ordered queue section, grouped by cluster."""
   297→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   298→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   299→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
   300→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
   301→
   302→    ordered_ids = set(queue_order) - skipped_ids
   303→    if not ordered_ids:
   304→        return []
   305→
   306→    by_id = {item.get("id"): item for item in items}
   307→
   308→    lines: list[str] = [
   309→        "---",
   310→        f"## User-Ordered Queue ({len(ordered_ids)} items)",
   311→        "",
   312→    ]
   313→
   314→    # Group by cluster: clustered items first, then unclustered
   315→    emitted: set[str] = set()
   316→    for cluster_name, cluster in clusters.items():
   317→        member_ids = [
   318→            fid for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   319→            if fid in ordered_ids and fid in by_id
   320→        ]
   321→        if not member_ids:
   322→            continue
   323→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   324→        lines.append(f"### Cluster: {cluster_name}")
   325→        if desc:
   326→            lines.append(f"> {desc}")
   327→        lines.append("")
   328→        for fid in member_ids:
   329→            item = by_id.get(fid)
   330→            if item:
   331→                lines.extend(_render_plan_item(item, overrides.get(fid, {})))
   332→                emitted.add(fid)
   333→        lines.append("")
   334→
   335→    # Unclustered ordered items
   336→    unclustered = [
   337→        fid for fid in queue_order
   338→        if fid in ordered_ids and fid not in emitted and fid in by_id
   339→    ]
   340→    if unclustered:
   341→        if any(c.get("finding_ids") for c in clusters.values()):
   342→            lines.append("### (unclustered ordered items)")
   343→            lines.append("")
   344→        for fid in unclustered:
   345→            item = by_id.get(fid)
   346→            if item:
   347→                lines.extend(_render_plan_item(item, overrides.get(fid, {})))
   348→        lines.append("")
   349→
   350→    return lines
   351→
   352→
   353→def _plan_skipped_section(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   354→    """Render the skipped items section, grouped by kind."""
   355→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   356→    if not skipped:
   357→        return []
   358→
   359→    by_id = {item.get("id"): item for item in items}
   360→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   361→
   362→    # Group by kind
   363→    by_kind: dict[str, list[str]] = {"temporary": [], "permanent": [], "false_positive": []}
   364→    for fid, entry in skipped.items():
   365→        kind = entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   366→        by_kind.setdefault(kind, []).append(fid)
   367→
   368→    kind_labels = {
   369→        "temporary": "Skipped Temporarily",
   370→        "permanent": "Wontfix (permanent)",
   371→        "false_positive": "False Positives",
   372→    }
   373→
   374→    lines: list[str] = [
   375→        "---",
   376→        f"## Skipped ({len(skipped)} items)",
   377→        "",
   378→    ]
   379→
   380→    for kind in ("temporary", "permanent", "false_positive"):
   381→        ids = by_kind.get(kind, [])
   382→        if not ids:
   383→            continue
   384→        lines.append(f"### {kind_labels[kind]} ({len(ids)})")
   385→        lines.append("")
   386→        for fid in ids:
   387→            entry = skipped.get(fid, {})
   388→            item = by_id.get(fid)
   389→            if item:
   390→                lines.extend(_render_plan_item(item, overrides.get(fid, {})))
   391→            else:
   392→                lines.append(f"- ~~{fid}~~ (not in current queue)")
   393→            reason = entry.get("reason")
   394→            if reason:
   395→                lines.append(f"      Reason: {reason}")
   396→            note = entry.get("note")
   397→            if note and not overrides.get(fid, {}).get("note"):
   398→                lines.append(f"      Note: {note}")
   399→            review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   400→            if review_after:
   401→                skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   402→                lines.append(f"      Review after: scan {skipped_at + review_after}")
   403→        lines.append("")
   404→
   405→    return lines
   406→
   407→
   408→def _plan_superseded_section(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   409→    """Render the superseded items section."""
   410→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   411→    if not superseded:
   412→        return []
   413→
   414→    lines: list[str] = [
   415→        "---",
   416→        f"## Superseded ({len(superseded)} items — may need remap)",
   417→        "",
   418→    ]
   419→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   420→        summary = entry.get("original_summary", "")
   421→        summary_str = f" — {summary}" if summary else ""
   422→        lines.append(f"- ~~{fid}~~{summary_str}")
   423→        candidates = entry.get("candidates", [])
   424→        if candidates:
   425→            lines.append(f"  Candidates: {', '.join(candidates[:3])}")
   426→        note = entry.get("note")
   427→        if note:
   428→            lines.append(f"  Note: {note}")
   429→    lines.append("")
   430→    return lines
   431→
   432→
   433→def _render_plan_item(item: dict, override: dict) -> list[str]:
   434→    """Render a single plan item as markdown lines."""
   435→    tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   436→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   437→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
   438→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
   439→
   440→    lines = [f"- [ ] [T{tier}/{confidence}] {summary}"]
   441→    desc = override.get("description")
   442→    if desc:
   443→        lines.append(f"      → {desc}")
   444→    lines.append(f"      `{item_id}`")
   445→    note = override.get("note")
   446→    if note:
   447→        lines.append(f"      Note: {note}")
   448→    return lines
   449→
   450→
   451→def generate_plan_md(state: PlanState, plan: dict | None = None) -> str:
   452→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state.
   453→
   454→    When *plan* is provided (or auto-loaded from disk), user-ordered
   455→    items, clusters, skipped, and superseded sections are rendered.
   456→    When no plan exists, output is identical to the previous behavior.
   457→    """
   458→    findings = state["findings"]
   459→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   460→
   461→    # Auto-load plan if not provided
   462→    if plan is None:
   463→        try:
   464→            from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
   465→            plan = load_plan()
   466→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   467→            plan = {}
   468→
   469→    has_plan = bool(
   470→        plan
   471→        and (
   472→            plan.get("queue_order")
   473→            or plan.get("skipped")
   474→            or plan.get("clusters")
   475→        )
   476→    )
   477→
   478→    lines = _plan_header(state, stats)
   479→    lines.extend(_plan_dimension_table(state))
   480→    lines.extend(_tier_summary_lines(stats))
   481→
   482→    if has_plan:
   483→        # Build full queue for item lookup
   484→        queue = build_work_queue(
   485→            state,
   486→            options=QueueBuildOptions(
   487→                count=None,
   488→                scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   489→                status="open",
   490→                include_subjective=True,
   491→                no_tier_fallback=True,
   492→            ),
   493→        )
   494→        all_items = queue.get("items", [])
   495→        lines.extend(_plan_user_ordered_section(all_items, plan))
   496→
   497→        # Remaining: items NOT in queue_order or skipped
   498→        ordered_ids = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   499→        skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   500→        plan_ids = ordered_ids | skipped_ids
   501→        remaining = [item for item in all_items if item.get("id") not in plan_ids]
   502→        if remaining:
   503→            lines.append("---")
   504→            lines.append(f"## Remaining (mechanical order, {len(remaining)} items)")
   505→            lines.append("")
   506→
   507→        lines.extend(_plan_tier_sections(findings, state=state))
   508→        lines.extend(_plan_skipped_section(all_items, plan))
   509→        lines.extend(_plan_superseded_section(plan))
   510→    else:
   511→        lines.extend(_plan_tier_sections(findings, state=state))
   512→
   513→    lines.extend(_addressed_section(findings))
   514→
   515→    return "\n".join(lines)
   516→
```

```
    22→def _short_id(fid: str) -> str:
    23→    """Extract the 8-char hash suffix from a finding ID for compact display."""
    24→    if "::" in fid:
    25→        suffix = fid.rsplit("::", 1)[-1]
    26→        if len(suffix) >= 8:
    27→            return suffix[:8]
    28→    return fid
    29→
    30→
    31→def _require_synthesis_pending(plan: dict, *, action: str) -> bool:
    32→    """Require ``synthesis::pending`` to be present in queue for an action."""
    33→    if SYNTHESIS_ID in plan.get("queue_order", []):
    34→        return True
    35→    print(colorize(f"  synthesis::pending is not in the queue — nothing to {action}.", "yellow"))
    36→    return False
    37→
    38→
    39→def _validate_stage_report(
    40→    report: str | None,
    41→    *,
    42→    stage: str,
    43→    min_chars: int,
    44→    missing_guidance: list[str] | None = None,
    45→    short_guidance: list[str] | None = None,
    46→) -> str | None:
    47→    """Validate staged report presence/length and print consistent guidance."""
    48→    if not report:
    49→        print(colorize(f"  --report is required for --stage {stage}.", "red"))
    50→        for line in missing_guidance or []:
    51→            print(colorize(f"  {line}", "dim"))
    52→        return None
    53→    cleaned = report.strip()
    54→    if len(cleaned) < min_chars:
    55→        print(
    56→            colorize(
    57→                f"  Report too short: {len(cleaned)} chars (minimum {min_chars}).",
    58→                "red",
    59→            )
    60→        )
    61→        for line in short_guidance or []:
    62→            print(colorize(f"  {line}", "dim"))
    63→        return None
    64→    return cleaned
    65→
    66→
    67→def _synthesis_coverage(plan: dict) -> tuple[int, int, dict]:
    68→    """Return (organized, total, clusters) for synthesis progress."""
    69→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    70→    all_cluster_ids: set[str] = set()
    71→    for c in clusters.values():
    72→        all_cluster_ids.update(c.get("finding_ids", []))
    73→    queue_ids = [fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", []) if fid != SYNTHESIS_ID]
    74→    organized = sum(1 for fid in queue_ids if fid in all_cluster_ids)
    75→    return organized, len(queue_ids), clusters
    76→
    77→
    78→def _unenriched_clusters(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, list[str]]]:
    79→    """Return clusters with findings that are missing required enrichment."""
    80→    gaps: list[tuple[str, list[str]]] = []
    81→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    82→        if not cluster.get("finding_ids"):
    83→            continue
    84→        if cluster.get("auto"):
    85→            continue
    86→        missing: list[str] = []
    87→        if not cluster.get("description"):
    88→            missing.append("description")
    89→        if not cluster.get("action_steps"):
    90→            missing.append("action_steps")
    91→        if missing:
    92→            gaps.append((name, missing))
    93→    return gaps
    94→
    95→
    96→def _manual_clusters_with_findings(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
    97→    """Return names of non-auto clusters that have findings."""
    98→    return [
    99→        name
   100→        for name, c in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   101→        if c.get("finding_ids") and not c.get("auto")
   102→    ]

   105→def _print_stage_progress(stages: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> None:
   106→    """Print the 4-stage progress indicator."""
   107→    print(colorize("  Stages:", "dim"))
   108→    for stage_name, label in SYNTHESIS_STAGE_LABELS:
   109→        if stage_name in stages:
   110→            print(colorize(f"    ✓ {label}", "green"))
   111→        elif SYNTHESIS_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES[stage_name].issubset(stages):
   112→            print(colorize(f"    → {label} (current)", "yellow"))
   113→        else:
   114→            print(colorize(f"    ○ {label}", "dim"))
   115→
   116→    if plan and "reflect" in stages and "organize" not in stages:
   117→        gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
   118→        manual = _manual_clusters_with_findings(plan)
   119→        if not manual:
   120→            print(

   123→                    "yellow",

   125→            )

   128→            for name, missing in gaps:

   130→            print(

   133→                    "dim",
   134→                )
   135→            )
   136→        else:
   137→            print(colorize(f"\n    All {len(manual)} manual cluster(s) enriched.", "green"))
   138→
   139→
   140→def _print_progress(plan: dict, open_findings: dict) -> None:
   141→    """Show cluster state and unclustered findings."""
   142→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})

   145→    _print_unclustered_findings(plan, open_findings, unclustered)

   148→def _print_active_clusters(clusters: dict[str, dict]) -> None:
   149→    """Print current clusters that contain findings."""
   150→    active_clusters = {name: c for name, c in clusters.items() if c.get("finding_ids")}
   151→    if not active_clusters:

   153→    print(colorize("\n  Current clusters:", "cyan"))
   154→    for name, cluster in active_clusters.items():

   156→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   157→        tag_str = _cluster_tag_summary(cluster)
   158→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   159→        print(f"    {name}: {count} items{tag_str}{desc_str}")
   160→
   161→
   162→def _cluster_tag_summary(cluster: dict) -> str:
   163→    """Build compact tag summary for one cluster row."""
   164→    steps = cluster.get("action_steps", [])
   165→    auto = cluster.get("auto", False)
   166→    tags: list[str] = []
   167→    tags.append("auto" if auto else "manual")
   168→    tags.append("desc" if cluster.get("description") else "no desc")
   169→    if steps:
   170→        tags.append(f"{len(steps)} steps")
   171→    elif not auto:
   172→        tags.append("no steps")
   173→    return f" [{', '.join(tags)}]"
   174→
   175→
   176→def _collect_unclustered_findings(clusters: dict[str, dict], open_findings: dict) -> list[str]:
   177→    """Return finding IDs that are not attached to any cluster."""
   178→    all_clustered: set[str] = set()
   179→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   180→        all_clustered.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   181→    return [fid for fid in open_findings if fid not in all_clustered]
   182→
   183→
   184→def _print_unclustered_findings(
   185→    plan: dict,
   186→    open_findings: dict,
   187→    unclustered: list[str],
   188→) -> None:
   189→    """Print unclustered findings summary or all-clustered confirmation."""
   190→    if unclustered:
   191→        print(colorize(f"\n  {len(unclustered)} findings not yet in a cluster:", "yellow"))
   192→        for fid in unclustered[:10]:
   193→            finding = open_findings[fid]
   194→            dim = (
   195→                (finding.get("detail", {}) or {}).get("dimension", "")
   196→                if isinstance(finding.get("detail"), dict)
   197→                else ""
   198→            )
   199→            short = _short_id(fid)
   200→            print(f"    [{short}] [{dim}] {finding.get('summary', '')}")
   201→        if len(unclustered) > 10:
   202→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(unclustered) - 10} more", "dim"))
   203→        return
   204→    if open_findings:
   205→        organized, total, _ = _synthesis_coverage(plan)
   206→        print(colorize(f"\n  All {organized}/{total} findings are in clusters.", "green"))
   207→
   208→
   209→def refresh_stage_snapshot(plan: dict, state: dict) -> None:
   210→    """Mark stage progress as aligned with the current review-finding snapshot."""
   211→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   212→    meta["stage_snapshot_hash"] = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   213→    meta["stage_refresh_required"] = False
   214→
   215→
   216→def record_synthesis_stage(
   217→    plan: dict,
   218→    state: dict,
   219→    *,
   220→    stage: str,
   221→    report: str,
   222→    cited_ids: list[str],
   223→    finding_count: int,
   224→    extra: dict[str, object] | None = None,
   225→) -> dict[str, object]:
   226→    """Persist one stage payload, refresh snapshot metadata, and save the plan."""
   227→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   228→    stages = meta.setdefault("synthesis_stages", {})
   229→    payload: dict[str, object] = {
   230→        "stage": stage,
   231→        "report": report,
   232→        "cited_ids": list(cited_ids),
   233→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
   234→        "finding_count": finding_count,
   235→    }
   236→    if extra:
   237→        payload.update(extra)
   238→    stages[stage] = payload
   239→    refresh_stage_snapshot(plan, state)
   240→    save_plan(plan)
   241→    return payload
   242→
   243→
   244→def _apply_completion(args: argparse.Namespace, plan: dict, strategy: str) -> None:
   245→    """Shared completion logic: update meta, remove synthesis::pending, save."""
   246→    organized, total, clusters = _synthesis_coverage(plan)
   247→
   248→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   249→    if SYNTHESIS_ID in order:
   250→        order.remove(SYNTHESIS_ID)
   251→
   252→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   253→    state = runtime.state
   254→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   255→
   256→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   257→    meta["finding_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   258→    if strategy.strip().lower() != "same":
   259→        meta["strategy_summary"] = strategy
   260→    meta["trigger"] = "manual_synthesis"
   261→    meta["last_completed_at"] = utc_now()
   262→    meta["synthesis_stages"] = {}
   263→    meta.pop("stage_refresh_required", None)
   264→    meta.pop("stage_snapshot_hash", None)
   265→
   266→    save_plan(plan)
   267→
   268→    cluster_count = len([c for c in clusters.values() if c.get("finding_ids")])
   269→    print(
   270→        colorize(
   271→            f"  Synthesis complete: {organized}/{total} findings in {cluster_count} cluster(s).",
   272→            "green",
   273→        )
   274→    )
   275→    effective_strategy = (
   276→        strategy if strategy.strip().lower() != "same" else meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   277→    )
   278→    if effective_strategy:
   279→        print(colorize(f"  Strategy: {effective_strategy}", "cyan"))
   280→    print(colorize("  Run `desloppify next` to start implementation.", "green"))
   281→
   282→
   283→def _print_reflect_dashboard(si: object, plan: dict) -> None:
   284→    """Show completed clusters, resolved findings, and recurring patterns."""
   285→    completed = getattr(si, "completed_clusters", [])
   286→    resolved = getattr(si, "resolved_findings", {})
   287→    open_findings = getattr(si, "open_findings", {})
   288→
   289→    _print_completed_clusters(completed)
   290→    _print_resolved_findings(resolved)
   291→    recurring = _print_recurring_patterns(open_findings, resolved)
   292→    if not recurring and not completed and not resolved:
   293→        print(colorize("\n  First synthesis — no prior work to compare against.", "dim"))
   294→        print(colorize("  Focus your reflect report on your strategy:", "yellow"))
   295→        print(
   296→            colorize(
   297→                "  - How will you resolve contradictions you identified in observe?",
   298→                "dim",
   299→            )
   300→        )
   301→        print(colorize("  - Which findings will you cluster together vs defer?", "dim"))
   302→        print(colorize("  - What's the overall arc of work and why?", "dim"))
   303→
   304→
   305→def _print_completed_clusters(completed: list[dict]) -> None:
   306→    """Print completed cluster context for reflect stage."""
   307→    if not completed:
   308→        return
   309→    print(colorize("\n  Previously completed clusters:", "cyan"))
   310→    for cluster in completed[:10]:
   311→        name = cluster.get("name", "?")
   312→        count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   313→        thesis = cluster.get("thesis", "")
   314→        print(f"    {name}: {count} findings")
   315→        if thesis:
   316→            print(colorize(f"      {thesis}", "dim"))
   317→        for step in cluster.get("action_steps", [])[:3]:
   318→            print(colorize(f"      - {step}", "dim"))
   319→    if len(completed) > 10:
   320→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(completed) - 10} more", "dim"))
   321→
   322→
   323→def _print_resolved_findings(resolved: dict[str, dict]) -> None:
   324→    """Print resolved findings delta since last synthesis."""
   325→    if not resolved:
   326→        return
   327→    print(colorize(f"\n  Resolved findings since last synthesis: {len(resolved)}", "cyan"))
   328→    for fid, finding in sorted(resolved.items())[:10]:
   329→        status = finding.get("status", "")
   330→        summary = finding.get("summary", "")
   331→        detail = finding.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(finding.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   332→        dim = detail.get("dimension", "")
   333→        print(f"    [{status}] [{dim}] {summary}")
   334→        print(colorize(f"      {fid}", "dim"))
   335→    if len(resolved) > 10:
   336→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(resolved) - 10} more", "dim"))
   337→
   338→
   339→def _print_recurring_patterns(open_findings: dict, resolved: dict[str, dict]) -> bool:
   340→    """Print recurring pattern diagnostics for reflect stage."""
   341→    recurring = detect_recurring_patterns(open_findings, resolved)
   342→    if not recurring:
   343→        return False
   344→    print(colorize("\n  Recurring patterns detected:", "yellow"))
   345→    for dim, info in sorted(recurring.items()):
   346→        resolved_count = len(info["resolved"])
   347→        open_count = len(info["open"])
   348→        label = "potential loop" if open_count >= resolved_count else "root cause unaddressed"
   349→        print(
   350→            colorize(
   351→                f"    {dim}: {resolved_count} resolved, {open_count} still open — {label}",
   352→                "yellow",
   353→            )
   354→        )
   355→    return True
   356→
   357→
```

```
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.synthesis import shared as synth_shared_mod
    10→from desloppify.core.subjective_dimensions import DISPLAY_NAMES
    11→from desloppify.core.output import colorize

    18→
    19→def _normalize_report_text(text: str) -> str:
    20→    return " ".join(str(text).strip().lower().replace("_", " ").split())
    21→
    22→
    23→def _dimension_report_aliases(dimension: str) -> set[str]:
    24→    aliases: set[str] = set()
    25→    canonical = _normalize_report_text(dimension)
    26→    if canonical:
    27→        aliases.add(canonical)
    28→    display_name = DISPLAY_NAMES.get(dimension, "")
    29→    display = _normalize_report_text(display_name)
    30→    if display:
    31→        aliases.add(display)
    32→    return aliases
    33→
    34→
    35→def _report_mentions_dimension(report: str, dimension: str) -> bool:
    36→    normalized_report = _normalize_report_text(report)
    37→    if not normalized_report:
    38→        return False
    39→    padded_report = f" {normalized_report} "
    40→    for alias in _dimension_report_aliases(dimension):
    41→        if alias and f" {alias} " in padded_report:
    42→            return True
    43→        if alias and re.search(rf"\b{re.escape(alias)}\b", normalized_report):
    44→            return True
    45→    return False
    46→
    47→
    48→def cmd_stage_reflect(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    49→    """Record the REFLECT stage: compare current findings against completed work."""
    50→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
    51→
    52→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    53→    state = runtime.state
    54→    plan = load_plan()
    55→
    56→    if not synth_shared_mod._require_synthesis_pending(plan, action="reflect"):
    57→        return
    58→
    59→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    60→    stages = meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})
    61→    if "observe" not in stages:
    62→        print(colorize("  Cannot reflect: observe stage not complete.", "red"))
    63→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan synthesize --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))

    66→    si = collect_synthesis_input(plan, state)
    67→    finding_count = len(si.open_findings)

    69→    min_chars = 50 if finding_count <= 3 else 100
    70→    validated_report = synth_shared_mod._validate_stage_report(
    71→        report,

    74→        missing_guidance=[

   112→                    "dim",
   113→                )
   114→            )
   115→            return
   116→
   117→    synth_shared_mod.record_synthesis_stage(
   118→        plan,

   125→    )
   126→
   127→    print(
```

```
     1→"""Handler for ``plan synthesize`` subcommand.
```

```
   213→def _cmd_stage_observe(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   214→    """Record the OBSERVE stage: agent analyses themes and root causes.
   215→
   216→    No citation gate — the point is genuine analysis, not ID-stuffing.

   335→
   336→    # Detect recurring patterns

   433→
   434→def _cmd_stage_organize(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   435→    """Record the ORGANIZE stage: validates cluster enrichment.
   436→
   437→    Instead of gating on a text report, validates that the plan data
   438→    itself has been enriched: each manual cluster needs description +
   439→    action_steps. This forces the agent to actually think about each
   440→    cluster's execution plan.
   441→    """
   442→    plan = load_plan()
   443→
   444→    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   445→        print(colorize("  synthesis::pending is not in the queue \u2014 nothing to organize.", "yellow"))
   446→        return
   447→
   448→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   449→    stages = meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})
   450→    if "reflect" not in stages:
   451→        if "observe" not in stages:
   452→            print(colorize("  Cannot organize: observe stage not complete.", "red"))
   453→            print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan synthesize --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
   454→        else:
   455→            print(colorize("  Cannot organize: reflect stage not complete.", "red"))
   456→            print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan synthesize --stage reflect --report "..."', "dim"))
   457→        return
   458→
   459→    # Validate: at least 1 manual cluster with findings
   460→    manual_clusters = _manual_clusters_with_findings(plan)
   461→    if not manual_clusters:
   462→        # Check if there are ANY clusters with findings (including auto)
   463→        any_clusters = [
   464→            name for name, c in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   465→            if c.get("finding_ids")
   466→        ]
   467→        if any_clusters:
   468→            print(colorize("  Cannot organize: only auto-clusters exist.", "red"))
   469→            print(colorize("  Create manual clusters that group findings by root cause:", "dim"))
   470→        else:
   471→            print(colorize("  Cannot organize: no clusters with findings exist.", "red"))
   472→        print(colorize('    desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."', "dim"))
   473→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan cluster add <name> <finding-patterns>", "dim"))
   474→        return
   475→
   476→    # Validate: all manual clusters are enriched
   477→    gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
   478→    if gaps:
   479→        print(colorize(f"  Cannot organize: {len(gaps)} cluster(s) need enrichment.", "red"))
   480→        for name, missing in gaps:
   481→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
   482→        print()
   483→        print(colorize("  Each cluster needs a description and action steps:", "dim"))
   484→        print(colorize(
   485→            '    desloppify plan cluster update <name> --description "what this cluster addresses" '
   486→            '--steps "step 1" "step 2"',
   487→            "dim",
   488→        ))
   489→        return
   490→
   491→    # Require report — the agent must summarize what they organized and why
   492→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
   493→    if not report:
   494→        print(colorize("  --report is required for --stage organize.", "red"))
   495→        print(colorize("  Summarize your prioritized organization:", "dim"))
   496→        print(colorize("  - Did you defer contradictory findings before clustering?", "dim"))
   497→        print(colorize("  - What clusters did you create and why?", "dim"))
   498→        print(colorize("  - Explicit priority ordering: which cluster 1st, 2nd, 3rd and why?", "dim"))
   499→        print(colorize("  - What depends on what? What unblocks the most?", "dim"))
   500→        return
   501→    if len(report) < 100:
   502→        print(colorize(f"  Report too short: {len(report)} chars (minimum 100).", "red"))
   503→        print(colorize("  Explain what you organized, your priorities, and focus order.", "dim"))
   504→        return
   505→
   506→    stages = meta.setdefault("synthesis_stages", {})
   507→    stages["organize"] = {
   508→        "stage": "organize",
   509→        "report": report,
   510→        "cited_ids": [],
   511→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
   512→        "finding_count": len(manual_clusters),
   513→    }
   514→    save_plan(plan)
   515→
   516→    print(colorize(
   517→        f"  Organize stage recorded: {len(manual_clusters)} enriched cluster(s).",
   518→        "green",
   519→    ))
   520→    # Show cluster summary
   521→    for name in manual_clusters:
   522→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(name, {})
   523→        steps = cluster.get("action_steps", [])
   524→        desc = cluster.get("description", "")

   558→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   559→# Completion
   560→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   561→
   562→def _cmd_synthesize_complete(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   563→    """Complete synthesis \u2014 requires organize stage (or confirm-existing path)."""
   564→    strategy: str | None = getattr(args, "strategy", None)
   565→    plan = load_plan()
   566→
   567→    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   568→        print(colorize("  synthesis::pending is not in the queue \u2014 nothing to complete.", "yellow"))
   569→        return
   570→
   571→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   572→    stages = meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})
   573→
   574→    # Require organize stage (or confirmed-existing)
   575→    if "organize" not in stages:
   576→        if "observe" not in stages:
   577→            print(colorize("  Cannot complete: no stages done yet.", "red"))
   578→            print(colorize('  Start with: desloppify plan synthesize --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
   579→        else:
   580→            print(colorize("  Cannot complete: organize stage not done.", "red"))
   581→            gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
   582→            if gaps:
   583→                print(colorize(f"  {len(gaps)} cluster(s) still need enrichment:", "yellow"))
   584→                for name, missing in gaps:
   585→                    print(colorize(f"    {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
   586→                print(colorize(
   587→                    '  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --description "..." --steps "step1" "step2"',
   588→                    "dim",
   589→                ))
   590→                print(colorize(
   591→                    f"  Then: {SYNTH_CMD_ORGANIZE}",
   592→                    "dim",
   593→                ))
   594→            else:
   595→                manual = _manual_clusters_with_findings(plan)
   596→                if manual:
   597→                    print(colorize("  Clusters are enriched. Record the organize stage first:", "dim"))
   598→                    print(colorize(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   599→                else:
   600→                    print(colorize("  Create enriched clusters first, then record organize:", "dim"))
```

```
   790→def _print_reflect_dashboard(si: object, plan: dict) -> None:
   791→    """Show completed clusters, resolved findings, and recurring patterns."""
   792→    # si is a SynthesisInput
   793→    completed = getattr(si, "completed_clusters", [])
   794→    resolved = getattr(si, "resolved_findings", {})
   795→    open_findings = getattr(si, "open_findings", {})
   796→
   797→    if completed:
   798→        print(colorize("\n  Previously completed clusters:", "cyan"))
   799→        for c in completed[:10]:
   800→            name = c.get("name", "?")
   801→            count = len(c.get("finding_ids", []))
   802→            thesis = c.get("thesis", "")
   803→            print(f"    {name}: {count} findings")
   804→            if thesis:
   805→                print(colorize(f"      {thesis}", "dim"))
   806→            for step in c.get("action_steps", [])[:3]:
   807→                print(colorize(f"      - {step}", "dim"))
   808→        if len(completed) > 10:
   809→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(completed) - 10} more", "dim"))
   810→
   811→    if resolved:
   812→        print(colorize(f"\n  Resolved findings since last synthesis: {len(resolved)}", "cyan"))
   813→        for fid, f in sorted(resolved.items())[:10]:
   814→            status = f.get("status", "")
   815→            summary = f.get("summary", "")
   816→            detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   817→            dim = detail.get("dimension", "")
   818→            print(f"    [{status}] [{dim}] {summary}")
   819→            print(colorize(f"      {fid}", "dim"))
   820→        if len(resolved) > 10:
   821→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(resolved) - 10} more", "dim"))
   822→
   823→    recurring = detect_recurring_patterns(open_findings, resolved)
   824→    if recurring:
   825→        print(colorize("\n  Recurring patterns detected:", "yellow"))
   826→        for dim, info in sorted(recurring.items()):
   827→            resolved_count = len(info["resolved"])
   828→            open_count = len(info["open"])
   829→            label = "potential loop" if open_count >= resolved_count else "root cause unaddressed"
   830→            print(colorize(
   831→                f"    {dim}: {resolved_count} resolved, {open_count} still open — {label}",
   832→                "yellow",
   833→            ))
   834→    elif not completed and not resolved:
   835→        print(colorize("\n  First synthesis — no prior work to compare against.", "dim"))
   836→        print(colorize("  Focus your reflect report on your strategy:", "yellow"))
   837→        print(colorize("  - How will you resolve contradictions you identified in observe?", "dim"))
   838→        print(colorize("  - Which findings will you cluster together vs defer?", "dim"))
   839→        print(colorize("  - What's the overall arc of work and why?", "dim"))

   975→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   976→# Router
   977→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   978→
   979→def cmd_plan_synthesize(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   980→    """Run epic synthesis: staged workflow OBSERVE → REFLECT → ORGANIZE → COMMIT."""
   981→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   982→    state = runtime.state
   983→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   984→        return
   985→
   986→    # Route: --complete
   987→    if getattr(args, "complete", False):
   988→        _cmd_synthesize_complete(args)
   989→        return
   990→
   991→    # Route: --confirm-existing
   992→    if getattr(args, "confirm_existing", False):
   993→        _cmd_confirm_existing(args)
   994→        return
   995→
   996→    # Route: --stage observe/reflect/organize
   997→    stage = getattr(args, "stage", None)
   998→    if stage == "observe":
   999→        _cmd_stage_observe(args)
```

## Deep Understanding of the Desloppify Synthesis Flow

Based on my thorough analysis of the synthesis codebase, here's the complete picture:

---

### **ARCHITECTURE OVERVIEW**

The synthesis flow is a **4-stage human-in-the-loop workflow** with a fast-track skip path:

```
OBSERVE → REFLECT → ORGANIZE → COMMIT (with optional fast-track CONFIRM-EXISTING)
```

The workflow is **not automatic**—it requires deliberate human input at each gate to surface contradictions, prioritize work, and validate a coherent plan before execution.

**Key orchestration files:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/synthesize_handlers.py` (1028 lines) — main handler with stage routing
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/synthesis_playbook.py` — stage labels, dependencies, canonical command snippets
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/synthesis/` — shared utilities (shared.py, reflect.py, organize.py, __init__.py)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/epic_synthesis.py` (681 lines) — LLM synthesis engine
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/auto_cluster.py` (502 lines) — auto-clustering algorithm
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py` (260 lines) — dimension sync logic

---

### **STAGE DEPENDENCIES (from synthesis_playbook.py)**

```python
SYNTHESIS_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES: dict[str, set[str]] = {
    "observe": set(),                    # Can start anytime
    "reflect": {"observe"},              # Requires observe first
    "organize": {"reflect"},             # Requires reflect first
    "commit": {"organize"},              # Requires organize first
}

SYNTHESIS_STAGE_LABELS: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...] = (
    ("observe", "Analyse findings & spot contradictions"),
    ("reflect", "Form strategy & present to user"),
    ("organize", "Defer contradictions, cluster, & prioritize"),
    ("commit", "Write strategy & confirm"),
)
```

**Stage discovery:** Gates are validated in handlers by checking `plan["epic_synthesis_meta"]["synthesis_stages"][stage_name]` presence.

---

### **STAGE 1: OBSERVE**
**Handler:** `_cmd_stage_observe()` (lines 213–290)

**Purpose:** Agent analyses themes, root causes, and contradictions in review findings.

**Function Signature:**
```python
def _cmd_stage_observe(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    """Record the OBSERVE stage: agent analyses themes and root causes.
    
    No citation gate — the point is genuine analysis, not ID-stuffing.
    Just requires a 100-char report describing what the agent observed.
    """
```

**Input Requirements:**
- `--report <text>` — analysis of themes/root causes/contradictions (100+ chars; 50+ if ≤3 findings)
- `synthesis::pending` must be in `queue_order` (validated via `_require_synthesis_pending()`)

**Validation Logic:**
- Report length check: `min_chars = 50 if finding_count <= 3 else 100`
- **No citation gate** — the report is just validated for substantive length
- Findings are extracted via `extract_finding_citations()` but **not required**

**What Gets Recorded (plan["epic_synthesis_meta"]["synthesis_stages"]["observe"]):**
```python
{
    "stage": "observe",
    "report": <validated_text>,
    "cited_ids": sorted(extract_finding_citations(...)),  # For analytics, not gating
    "timestamp": utc_now(),
    "finding_count": len(si.open_findings),
}
```

**What User Sees:**
```
✓ Observe stage recorded: N findings analysed.
  Next: run `desloppify plan synthesize` to see completed work, resolved findings,
  and recurring patterns. Then record your reflect stage:
    desloppify plan synthesize --stage reflect --report "..."
```

**Triggers:**
- `synthesis::pending` added to queue when `review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)` changes (via `sync_synthesis_needed()`)
- Hash is stored in `plan["epic_synthesis_meta"]["finding_snapshot_hash"]`

---

### **STAGE 2: REFLECT**
**Handler:** `cmd_stage_reflect()` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/synthesis/reflect.py`

**Purpose:** Agent compares current findings against **completed work** and detects **recurring patterns** (dimensions with both resolved AND open findings).

**Function Signature:**
```python
def cmd_stage_reflect(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    """Record the REFLECT stage: compare current findings against completed work.
    
    Forces the agent to consider what was previously resolved and whether
    similar issues are recurring. Requires a 100-char report (50 if ≤3 findings).
    If recurring patterns are detected, the report must mention at least one
    recurring dimension name.
    """
```

**Input Requirements:**
- `--report <text>` — comparison against completed work (100+ chars; 50+ if ≤3 findings)
- `observe` stage must be complete
- `synthesis::pending` must be in `queue_order`

**Validation Logic:**

1. **Report length:** `min_chars = 50 if finding_count <= 3 else 100`

2. **Recurring pattern detection:**
   ```python
   recurring = detect_recurring_patterns(si.open_findings, si.resolved_findings)
   ```
   This computes `{dimension: {"open": [ids], "resolved": [ids]}}` for dimensions with BOTH open and resolved findings.

3. **Dimensional mention gate:** If `recurring_dims` is not empty, the report MUST mention at least one dimension name (case-insensitive, using `_dimension_report_aliases()` + `DISPLAY_NAMES`).
   - Aliases include both the canonical name (e.g., "test_coverage") and the display name (e.g., "Test Coverage")
   - Fails with guidance on what dimensions need to be mentioned

**What Gets Recorded:**
```python
plan["epic_synthesis_meta"]["synthesis_stages"]["reflect"] = {
    "stage": "reflect",
    "report": <validated_text>,
    "cited_ids": [],
    "timestamp": utc_now(),
    "finding_count": len(si.open_findings),
    "recurring_dimensions": sorted(recurring.keys()),
}
```

**What User Sees:**
```
✓ Reflect stage recorded: N findings, M recurring dimension(s).
  
  ┌─ Strategic briefing (share with user before organizing) ─┐
  │ <report text (first 8 lines)>
  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
  
  IMPORTANT: Present your holistic strategy to the user. Explain:
  - What themes and root causes you see
  - What contradictions you found and which direction you'll take
  - What you'll prioritize, what you'll defer, the overall arc of work
  Wait for their input before creating clusters.
  
  Then create clusters and enrich each with action steps:
    desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."
    desloppify plan cluster add <name> <finding-patterns>
    desloppify plan cluster update <name> --steps "step 1" "step 2"
    desloppify plan synthesize --stage organize --report "summary of..."
```

**Key Data Sources:**
- `si.completed_clusters` — clusters with `completed_at` > `meta["last_completed_at"]`
- `si.resolved_findings` — findings in `findings` dict with status != "open"
- `si.resolved_since_last` — difference between `synthesized_ids` and current open review IDs

---

### **STAGE 3: ORGANIZE**
**Handler:** `cmd_stage_organize()` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/synthesis/organize.py`

**Purpose:** Agent creates/enriches manual clusters with descriptions and action steps, then reports prioritized organization.

**Function Signature:**
```python
def cmd_stage_organize(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    """Record the ORGANIZE stage: validates cluster enrichment.
    
    Instead of gating on a text report, validates that the plan data
    itself has been enriched: each manual cluster needs description +
    action_steps. This forces the agent to actually think about each
    cluster's execution plan.
    """
```

**Input Requirements:**
- `--report <text>` — summary of organization and priorities (100+ chars)
- `reflect` stage must be complete
- **At least 1 manual cluster** (non-auto) with findings must exist
- **All manual clusters** must have `description` and `action_steps` fields (validated via `_unenriched_clusters()`)

**Validation Logic:**

1. **Manual cluster check:**
   ```python
   manual_clusters = _manual_clusters_with_findings(plan)
   if not manual_clusters:
       print("Cannot organize: no clusters with findings exist.")
   ```

2. **Enrichment check:**
   ```python
   gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)  # Returns clusters missing description OR action_steps
   if gaps:
       print(f"Cannot organize: {len(gaps)} cluster(s) need enrichment.")
       # Shows which fields are missing for each cluster
   ```

3. **Report length:** Minimum 100 characters

**What Gets Recorded:**
```python
plan["epic_synthesis_meta"]["synthesis_stages"]["organize"] = {
    "stage": "organize",
    "report": <validated_text>,
    "cited_ids": [],
    "timestamp": utc_now(),
    "finding_count": len(manual_clusters),
}
```

**What User Sees:**
```
✓ Organize stage recorded: N enriched cluster(s).
  
  <cluster details>:
    cluster-name: M findings, K steps — description text
  
  ┌─ Prioritized organization (share with user) ───────────┐
  │ <report text (first 8 lines)>
  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
  
  IMPORTANT: Present your prioritized organization to the user. Explain
  each cluster, why it exists, and your explicit priority ordering —
  which cluster comes first, second, third, what depends on what,
  and why that ordering matters.
  
  Next: desloppify plan synthesize --complete --strategy "plan summary..."
```

**Cluster Data Structure (from plan["clusters"][name]):**
```python
{
    "name": str,
    "description": str,  # REQUIRED by organize gate
    "finding_ids": list[str],
    "action_steps": list[str],  # REQUIRED by organize gate
    "auto": bool,  # False for manual clusters
    "cluster_key": str,  # e.g., "epic::cluster-name"
    "action": str,  # suggested command
    "user_modified": bool,
    "created_at": str,
    "updated_at": str,
    # Epic fields (optional, from synthesis):
    "thesis": str,
    "direction": str,  # delete|merge|flatten|enforce|simplify|decompose|extract|inline
    "root_cause": str,
    "source_clusters": list[str],
    "status": str,  # pending|in_progress|completed
    "synthesis_version": int,
}
```

---

### **STAGE 4: COMMIT (via --complete)**
**Handler:** `_cmd_synthesize_complete()` (lines 562–685)

**Purpose:** Finalize synthesis by removing `synthesis::pending` from queue and recording final strategy.

**Function Signature:**
```python
def _cmd_synthesize_complete(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    """Complete synthesis — requires organize stage (or confirm-existing path)."""
```

**Input Requirements:**
- `--strategy <text>` — execution plan (200+ chars OR the literal string "same")
- `organize` stage must be complete
- All clusters must remain enriched (re-validated)
- At least some coverage: if `total > 0`, then `organized > 0`

**Validation Logic:**

1. **Stage gate:** `organize` stage must be present
2. **Cluster re-validation:** All manual clusters must still have description + action_steps
3. **Coverage check:** If there are items in queue, at least 1 must be in a cluster (but warns if < 100% coverage)
4. **Strategy length:** Unless `strategy == "same"`, minimum 200 characters

**What Gets Recorded (via `_apply_completion()`):**
```python
meta["finding_snapshot_hash"] = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
if strategy.strip().lower() != "same":
    meta["strategy_summary"] = strategy
meta["trigger"] = "manual_synthesis"
meta["last_completed_at"] = utc_now()
meta["synthesis_stages"] = {}  # Cleared!
meta.pop("stage_refresh_required", None)
meta.pop("stage_snapshot_hash", None)

# Also remove synthesis::pending from queue
order.remove(SYNTHESIS_ID)
```

**What User Sees:**
```
✓ Synthesis complete: N/M findings in K cluster(s).
  Strategy: <strategy text>
  Run `desloppify next` to start implementation.
```

**Data Persistence:** `save_plan(plan)` writes to disk

---

### **FAST-TRACK: CONFIRM-EXISTING**
**Handler:** `_cmd_confirm_existing()` (lines 692–783)

**Purpose:** Skip the full OBSERVE → REFLECT → ORGANIZE flow if the existing plan is still valid (only new/changed findings have appeared).

**Function Signature:**
```python
def _cmd_confirm_existing(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    """Fast-track: confirm existing plan structure is still valid."""
```

**Input Requirements:**
- `--note <text>` — explain why plan is still valid (100+ chars)
- `--strategy <text>` — execution plan (200+ chars OR "same")
- `observe` stage must be complete
- `reflect` stage must be complete
- **A prior strategy must exist** (`meta["strategy_summary"]` must be non-empty)
- Existing enriched clusters must exist
- If new findings exist, at least 1 must be cited in the note

**Validation Logic:**

1. **Gate checks:** observe + reflect both required
2. **Prior synthesis check:** `meta["strategy_summary"]` must exist (prevents skipping full flow on first run)
3. **Cluster validation:** Manual clusters with findings must exist
4. **Note length:** 100+ chars
5. **New finding citation:** If `si.new_since_last` is non-empty, the note must cite at least 1 via `extract_finding_citations()`

**What Happens:**
```python
# Records organize as "confirmed-existing"
stages["organize"] = {
    "stage": "organize",
    "report": f"[confirmed-existing] {note}",
    "cited_ids": [],
    "timestamp": utc_now(),
    "finding_count": len(clusters_with_findings),
}

# Then calls _apply_completion() (same as --complete)
```

**What User Sees:**
```
✓ Confirmed existing plan — synthesis complete.
  Synthesis complete: N/M findings in K cluster(s).
  Run `desloppify next` to start implementation.
```

---

### **HELPER FUNCTIONS & VALIDATION**

**`_short_id(fid: str) -> str`**
- Extracts 8-char hash suffix from finding ID (e.g., `review::abcdef12` → `abcdef12`)
- Used in display

**`extract_finding_citations(text: str, valid_ids: set[str]) -> set[str]`** (epic_synthesis.py:40–58)
- Regex pattern: `r"[a-z_]+::[a-f0-9]{8,}"`
- Matches full finding IDs or 8+ char hex suffixes
- Returns only IDs present in `valid_ids`

**`detect_recurring_patterns(open_findings, resolved_findings) -> dict`** (epic_synthesis.py:81–115)
- Groups by dimension
- Returns `{dim: {"open": [ids], "resolved": [ids]}}` for dims with both types
- Uses `detail.get("dimension")` from finding dict

**`_unenriched_clusters(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, list[str]]]`** (shared.py:78–93)
- Returns `[(name, missing_fields), ...]` for manual clusters missing description or action_steps
- Skips auto-clusters

**`_manual_clusters_with_findings(plan: dict) -> list[str]`** (shared.py:96–102)
- Returns names of non-auto clusters that have at least 1 finding

**`_synthesis_coverage(plan: dict) -> tuple[int, int, dict]`** (shared.py:67–75)
- Returns `(organized_count, total_items, clusters)`
- Organized = items in `queue_order` that are members of some cluster

**`_print_stage_progress(stages: dict, plan: dict | None) -> None`** (shared.py:105–137)
- Prints the 4-stage progress with checkmarks/arrows/circles
- Shows enrichment gaps if between reflect and organize

**`refresh_stage_snapshot(plan, state) -> None`** (shared.py:209–213)
- Sets `meta["stage_snapshot_hash"]` and clears `stage_refresh_required`

**`record_synthesis_stage(plan, state, *, stage, report, cited_ids, finding_count, extra) -> dict`** (shared.py:216–241)
- Persists a stage payload to `plan["epic_synthesis_meta"]["synthesis_stages"][stage]`
- Refreshes snapshot
- Saves plan to disk

---

### **DATA MODEL: SynthesisInput (epic_synthesis.py:122–135)**

```python
@dataclass
class SynthesisInput:
    open_findings: dict[str, dict]       # id -> finding (review + concerns, status="open")
    mechanical_findings: dict[str, dict]  # id -> finding (non-review, for context)
    existing_epics: dict[str, Cluster]    # current synthesis-clusters to update
    dimension_scores: dict[str, Any]      # for context (e.g., tier, issues)
    new_since_last: set[str]             # finding IDs new since last synthesis
    resolved_since_last: set[str]        # finding IDs resolved since last (not in open)
    previously_dismissed: list[str]      # IDs dismissed in prior synthesis
    synthesis_version: int               # next version number
    resolved_findings: dict[str, dict]   # full finding objects for resolved IDs
    completed_clusters: list[dict]       # clusters with completed_at > last_completed_at
```

**How it's collected (`collect_synthesis_input()`, lines 184–235):**
```python
findings = state.get("findings", {})
meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
epics = synthesis_clusters(plan)  # Extracts clusters with "thesis" field

open_review = {fid: f for fid, f in findings.items() 
               if f.get("status") == "open" 
               and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")}

synthesized_ids = set(meta.get("synthesized_ids", []))  # Prior synthesis snapshot
current_review_ids = set(open_review.keys())
new_since = current_review_ids - synthesized_ids
resolved_since = synthesized_ids - current_review_ids

# Completed clusters are those completed after meta["last_completed_at"]
last_completed = meta.get("last_completed_at", "")
recent_completed = [c for c in plan.get("completed_clusters", [])
                    if c.get("completed_at", "") > last_completed]
```

---

### **DATA MODEL: SynthesisResult (epic_synthesis.py:156–163)**

```python
@dataclass
class SynthesisResult:
    strategy_summary: str  # 2-4 sentence narrative
    epics: list[dict]     # Each has name, thesis, direction, finding_ids, action_steps, etc.
    dismissed_findings: list[DismissedFinding] = []
    contradiction_notes: list[ContradictionNote] = []
    priority_rationale: str = ""
```

**Parsed from LLM output by `parse_synthesis_result()` (lines 377–450):**
- Filters epics to valid `direction` values: `delete|merge|flatten|enforce|simplify|decompose|extract|inline`
- Validates finding IDs against `valid_ids` set (silently drops unknown IDs)
- Extracts dismissed findings and contradiction notes

---

### **LLM SYNTHESIS ENGINE** (epic_synthesis.py:626–663)

**`synthesize_epics(plan, state, *, deps=None, dry_run=False, trigger="manual")`**

```python
def synthesize_epics(
    plan: PlanModel,
    state: StateModel,
    *,
    deps: SynthesisDeps | None = None,
    dry_run: bool = False,
    trigger: str = "manual",
) -> SynthesisMutationResult:
    """Run epic synthesis: collect input, call LLM, apply results."""
    si = collect_synthesis_input(plan, state)
    prompt = build_synthesis_prompt(si)
    valid_ids = set(si.open_findings.keys())

    if dry_run or deps is None or deps.llm_call is None:
        return SynthesisMutationResult(dry_run=True)

    try:
        raw_response = deps.llm_call(_SYNTHESIS_SYSTEM_PROMPT, prompt)
        raw_json = json.loads(raw_response)
    except (json.JSONDecodeError, ValueError) as exc:
        result = SynthesisMutationResult()
        result.strategy_summary = f"Synthesis failed: {exc}"
        return result

    synthesis = parse_synthesis_result(raw_json, valid_ids)
    return apply_synthesis_to_plan(plan, state, synthesis, trigger=trigger)
```

**Deps:**
```python
@dataclass
class SynthesisDeps:
    llm_call: Any = None  # Callable[[system_prompt, user_prompt], json_string]
```

**System Prompt** (lines 242–297): Instructs LLM to:
- Cluster by ROOT CAUSE, not detector/dimension
- Give each epic a one-liner thesis
- Order by dependency
- Dismiss false positives & contradictions
- Mark agent-safe vs human-judgment epics
- Output JSON with schema

**Prompt Building** (`build_synthesis_prompt()`, lines 300–370):
- Existing epics section (if any)
- New findings since last synthesis
- Resolved findings section
- All open review findings (with file, dimension, suggestion)
- Dimension scores (for context)
- Previously dismissed findings

---

### **APPLYING SYNTHESIS TO PLAN** (epic_synthesis.py:457–612)

**`apply_synthesis_to_plan(plan, state, synthesis, *, trigger="manual") -> SynthesisMutationResult`**

**What it does:**

1. **Create/update epics:**
   - For each epic in `synthesis.epics`, sorted by `dependency_order`:
     - If cluster with that name exists + has `thesis`: **update** existing
     - Otherwise: **create** new synthesis-cluster
   - Sets `synthesis_version`, `updated_at`, timestamps

2. **Dismiss findings:**
   - From `synthesis.dismissed_findings` and per-epic `dismissed` lists
   - Creates skip entries: `{finding_id: "synthesized_out", reason, note}`
   - Removes from `queue_order`

3. **Reorder queue:**
   - Epic findings grouped by `dependency_order`
   - Ordered epics placed at front of queue
   - Non-epic items follow in original order
   - `synthesis::pending` removed (if present)

4. **Update synthesis meta:**
   ```python
   meta["synthesized_ids"] = sorted([open review IDs])
   meta["last_run"] = utc_now()
   meta["version"] = version
   meta["dismissed_ids"] = [all dismissed IDs]
   meta["finding_snapshot_hash"] = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   meta["strategy_summary"] = synthesis.strategy_summary
   meta["trigger"] = trigger
   ```

**Returns:**
```python
@dataclass
class SynthesisMutationResult:
    epics_created: int = 0
    epics_updated: int = 0
    epics_completed: int = 0
    findings_dismissed: int = 0
    findings_reassigned: int = 0
    strategy_summary: str = ""
    synthesis_version: int = 0
    dry_run: bool = False
```

---

### **AUTO-CLUSTERING** (auto_cluster.py)

**`auto_cluster_findings(plan, state) -> int`** (lines 241–496)

Regenerates auto-clusters on each sync. **Separate from manual synthesis.**

**Grouping logic (`_grouping_key()`, lines 53–92):**
- Review findings → by dimension (`review::dimension_name`)
- Structural/responsibility_cohesion → by file
- Detectors with subtypes → by subtype (`typed::detector::subtype`)
- Pure auto-fix (no judgment) → by detector
- Otherwise → by detector

**Cluster membership:**
- Excludes manually-clustered findings
- Excludes suppressed findings
- Groups with `MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE = 2` (drops singletons)

**Special subjective clusters:**
- `auto/initial-review` — unscored (placeholder) dimensions (min size 1)
- `auto/stale-review` — previously-scored, now stale dimensions

**Overrides tracking:**
```python
overrides[finding_id] = {
    "finding_id": finding_id,
    "cluster": cluster_name,
    "created_at": now,
    "updated_at": now,
}
```

---

### **DIMENSION SYNCING** (stale_dimensions.py)

**`sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult`** (lines 104–141)

**Unconditional:**
- Prunes subjective IDs that are no longer unscored AND not stale
- **Prepends** unscored IDs to front of queue (onboarding priority)

**`sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult`** (lines 148–187)

**Conditional:**
- Prunes subjective IDs that are no longer stale AND not unscored
- **Appends** stale IDs to back only if NO objective items remain

**`sync_synthesis_needed(plan, state) -> SynthesisSyncResult`** (lines 222–246)

**Conditional injection:**
- Computes `review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)` from open review finding IDs
- If hash differs from `meta["finding_snapshot_hash"]` AND `synthesis::pending` not already present:
  - **Injects** `synthesis::pending` at front of queue
  - Sets `result.injected = True`
- Never auto-prunes (only explicit completion removes it)

---

### **DASHBOARD VIEW (synthesize_handlers.py, lines 846–972)**

**`_cmd_synthesize_dashboard(args)`**

Called when no `--stage`, `--complete`, `--confirm-existing`, or `--dry-run` flag.

**Displays:**

1. **Header:** Count of open review findings, existing epics, new/resolved since last
2. **Stage progress:** Checkmarks, current stage indicator, enrichment gaps
3. **Action guidance:** Context-aware next-step command
4. **Prior stage reports:** Last 8 lines of observe/reflect reports (if recorded)
5. **Findings grouped by dimension:** With suggestions, highlighting contradictions
6. **Reflect dashboard** (if observe done, reflect not): completed clusters, resolved findings, recurring patterns
7. **Cluster progress:** Current clusters (with tags), unclustered findings count

### **PLAYBOOK STRUCTURE**

From `synthesis_playbook.py`:

```python
# Stage labels (4-tuple of name, display label)
SYNTHESIS_STAGE_LABELS = (
    ("observe", "Analyse findings & spot contradictions"),
    ("reflect", "Form strategy & present to user"),
    ("organize", "Defer contradictions, cluster, & prioritize"),
    ("commit", "Write strategy & confirm"),
)

# Canonical command snippets
SYNTH_CMD_OBSERVE = 'desloppify plan synthesize --stage observe --report "..."'
SYNTH_CMD_REFLECT = 'desloppify plan synthesize --stage reflect --report "..."'
SYNTH_CMD_ORGANIZE = 'desloppify plan synthesize --stage organize --report "..."'
SYNTH_CMD_COMPLETE_VERBOSE = 'desloppify plan synthesize --complete --strategy "..."'
SYNTH_CMD_CONFIRM_EXISTING = 'desloppify plan synthesize --confirm-existing --note "..." --strategy "..."'
```

### **QUEUE RENDERING** (queue_render.py)

**`cmd_plan_queue(args)`** (lines 113–171)

Renders compact table of work queue items with columns:
- Position, Tier, Detector, Summary (truncated), Cluster assignment
- Shows cluster tier range (e.g., T2-T3) from member tiers
- Includes skipped items count

**Work queue built via `build_work_queue(state, options=...)`** with:
- Plan data (if available) for clustering/filtering
- Tier filtering
- Cluster filtering (active_cluster from plan or --cluster arg)
- Collapse clusters option

---

### **PLAN RENDERING** (engine/planning/render.py)

**`generate_plan_md(state, plan=None) -> str`** (lines 451–515)

Generates markdown report with:
1. **Header:** health scores, stats (open/fixed/wontfix/auto-resolved), codebase metrics
2. **Dimension health table:** per-dimension tier, checks, issues, health %, action type
3. **Tier summary:** progress by tier
4. **User-ordered queue section:** clustered items grouped by cluster, then unclustered (if plan exists)
5. **Remaining section:** items NOT in plan
6. **Tier sections:** full breakdown by tier/file (if no plan)
7. **Skipped section:** temporary/permanent/false-positive
8. **Superseded section:** items with candidate remaps
9. **Addressed section:** wontfix with explanations

---

### **KEY VALIDATION RULES SUMMARY**

| Stage | Min Report | Required Prior | Data Gate | Report Gate |
|-------|-----------|-----------------|-----------|------------|
| **OBSERVE** | 100 chars (50 if ≤3 findings) | None | synthesis::pending in queue | Report length only |
| **REFLECT** | 100 chars (50 if ≤3 findings) | observe | synthesis::pending in queue | Report length + dimension mention (if recurring) |
| **ORGANIZE** | 100 chars | reflect | ≥1 manual cluster + all enriched | Report length + data validation |
| **COMPLETE** | — | organize | ≥1 manual cluster + all enriched | Strategy: 200+ chars or "same" |
| **CONFIRM-EXISTING** | — (note: 100+) | observe + reflect | Prior strategy exists + ≥1 cluster | Note: 100+ + cite 1 new finding (if any) |

---

### **DATA PERSISTENCE**

All stages persist via `save_plan(plan)`, which:
- Writes to `.desloppify/plan.json`
- Uses atomic temp + replace pattern
- Backs up to `.desloppify/plan.json.bak`

---

### **ENTRY POINTS**

**Router (`cmd_plan_synthesize()`, lines 979–1025):**
```python
if getattr(args, "complete", False):
    _cmd_synthesize_complete(args)
elif getattr(args, "confirm_existing", False):
    _cmd_confirm_existing(args)
elif stage == "observe":
    _cmd_stage_observe(args)
elif stage == "reflect":
    cmd_stage_reflect(args)  # In synthesis/reflect.py
elif stage == "organize":
    cmd_stage_organize(args)  # In synthesis/organize.py
elif getattr(args, "dry_run", False):
    # Build prompt and print (no LLM call)
else:
    # Default dashboard view
    _cmd_synthesize_dashboard(args)
```

---

### **CRITICAL INSIGHTS**

1. **Synthesis is opt-in:** The `synthesis::pending` item only appears when review findings change. User must explicitly record stages.

2. **Gates are about planning rigor:** OBSERVE, REFLECT, ORGANIZE gates ensure the agent thinks deeply about findings, contradictions, and execution. They're not just text validation—they're forcing deliberate synthesis.

3. **Report vs. data validation:** ORGANIZE uniquely validates **plan data** (cluster enrichment), not just report length. This ensures each cluster has a thesis and action plan.

4. **Snapshot hashing prevents drift:** `review_finding_snapshot_hash()` detects when open review IDs change, forcing re-synthesis rather than stale strategies.

5. **Recurring patterns are the key insight:** REFLECT gate forces acknowledgment of recurring dimensions, surfacing potential loops in the codebase.

6. **Fast-track is guarded:** CONFIRM-EXISTING requires a prior strategy AND citation of new findings, preventing lazy re-synthesis.

7. **Cluster membership is distributed:** Manual clusters (user-defined), auto-clusters (detector-based), synthesis-clusters (LLM-created) coexist in `plan["clusters"]`.

8. **Subjective dimensions are special:** They have their own sync logic (unscored front, stale back) separate from objective findings.

---

This is the complete synthesis architecture. All validation, reporting, and persistence patterns are now clear.
